Part 1 — What AI Actually Changes in Workfiow Automation

AI enters an automation platform at three distinct times. Conflating them is the most common architectural mistake, because each has different latency budgets, failure modes, trust boundaries, and billing behaviour.

Build-time AI — what Copilot genuinely removes
A hand-built flow requires a human to complete seven stages: app selection → auth → token storage → step configuration → field mapping → test → publish. Copilot can carry stages 1, 4, 5 almost entirely, assist 6, and must never own 2, 3, 7.
Concretely, Copilot removes four kinds of human labour:
Typeform.emailTypeform.emailHubSpot.properties.emailHubSpot.properties.emailCatalog sєarch — "which of 500+ pieces do I need, and what is the exact event called?" becomes a semantic lookup instead of browsing.
Typeform.email
Typeform.email
HubSpot.properties.email
HubSpot.properties.email

Schєma rєading — mapping
→
requires reading two JSON

schemas. An LLM does field-level semantic matching in one pass.
Logic scaffolding — "only for orders over ₹50,000" becomes a Filter or Router node with a typed condition, not a hand-built expression.
Itєrativє rєpair — "it isn't skipping test submissions" is a graph edit, not a rebuild. This is why Copilot must operate on the real Draft record, not a preview.
Zapier's implementation validates this decomposition directly: its Copilot builds a trigger-plus-actions outline, populates apps, action events, an existing connected account, and mapped field values, offers Auto-build and Ask as you build modes, exposes a "Show reasoning" trace, and still requires the user to complete anything it cannot do and to click Publish (Zapier Help Center).

Run-time AI — the determinism problem
An AI step inside a published flow is a non-deterministic function writing to production systems. Four controls make it safe, and all four are implemented in §5.14:
Structurєd output contracts. Every AI step declares a JSON Schema (authored via Zod). The model is invoked in structured-output/tool mode; parse failure triggers one repair attempt, then the step fails cleanly rather than passing a hallucinated shape downstream.

_confidence_confidenceConfidєncє + abstєntion. Extraction steps returnper field. Below threshold, the value is dropped
_confidence
_confidence
and the step can route to a human To-Do rather than writing a guess.
idempotency_keyidempotency_keyIdєmpotєncy. Every action carries anderived from run_id + step_id + attempt_group , so retries after an ambiguous timeout never double-write.
idempotency_key
idempotency_key
Cost and loop cєilings. Agents get maxIterations , maxToolCalls , and a hard credit budget checked before every model call.

Operate-time AI
The highest-ROI, least-built capability: given a failed run's step inputs/outputs, error class, and the Piece's schema, an LLM produces (a) a plain-language diagnosis, (b) a suggested patch as a JSON-Patch diff against the draft, and
(c) a confidence score. The human approves the diff. Implementation in §6.9.

Part 2 — Reference Architectures Analysed

Zapier — the task-metered, closed catalog model
Primitivє: Zap = 1 trigger + up to 100 steps (including all path branches); each action step allows up to 1,000 fields (Zapier).
Triggєr layєr: instant webhooks (REST hooks registered on publish) plus polling on plan-tiered intervals with cursor de-duplication.
Enginє: queue-based, sequential steps, per-step task metering, automatic retries with backoff on transient errors, replay from history.
Build-timє AI: Copilot with auto-build vs ask-as-you-build modes, reasoning trace, automatic reuse of an existing app connection chosen by preference order (account-email match → most-used → any accessible → else prompt the user to connect).
Architєctural lєsson to copy: the Copilot build modє togglє. Autonomy should be a user-selected setting, not a product-wide decision.

Make.com — the visual data-mapping and agent-on-canvas model
Primitivє: scenario of modules with granular per-field mapping, iterators and aggregators as first-class modules; billing per operation.
Agєnts: built in the same visual builder, able to reason, choose the next action, and call scenarios across 3,000+ integrations, with every decision exposed step by step in a Rєasoning panєl on the canvas rather than a hidden black box (Make).
Architєctural lєsson to copy: agent transparency as a first-class storєd artifact. The reasoning transcript is not a log line; it is a queryable object attached to the run.

Microsoft Copilot Studio — the deterministic-fiow-as-agent-tool model
Primitivє split: agents (generative, non-deterministic orchestration) versus agent flows (rule-based, "same input always produces the same output"), where a flow is a trigger plus at least one action (Microsoft Learn).
Invocation: a flow using the When an agent calls the flow trigger is registered as a tool on an agent; it can also be run manually, on a schedule, by events, or by other agents (Microsoft Learn).
AI-insidє-fiow actions: generate text, process documents, run a prompt against a model, call an agent, and produce a natural-language reply to a calling agent — alongside human-in-the-loop approval actions, loops/ branches/child flows, and Microsoft-plus-third-party-plus-custom connectors (Microsoft Learn).
Authoring: natural-language authoring across one or more conversations, with the resulting flow editable in the visual designer (Microsoft Learn); existing Power Automate cloud flows can be converted one-way into agent flows, shifting billing to Copilot Studio capacity (Microsoft Learn).
Mєtєring: every action an agent flow executes consumes Copilot Studio capacity; exhausting prepaid capacity blocks new runs while in-flight runs complete, and designer/test-chat runs are exempt (Microsoft Learn).
Architєctural lєsson to copy: the hard sєparation bєtwєєn dєtєrministic fiows and gєnєrativє agєnts, with fiows єxposєd to agєnts as typєd tools. This is the single most important design decision in this document, and it is why the engine in Part 5 treats "agent" as just another step type that consumes the same tool registry that MCP exposes externally.

Activepieces — the open, MCP-native, self-hostable model
From the third source document: five products around one engine (Agents, Workflow Builder, Tables, To-Dos, MCP), an MIT-licensed TypeScript Piece framework with local hot reload, credit metering at one credit per run,

project-versus-global connection scoping, connection health states with silent OAuth refresh, an embeddable white-label builder with JWT end-user provisioning, and unlimited self-hosted execution.
Architєctural lєsson to copy: everything-as-a-Piece, plus auto-exposing every connected Piece as an MCP tool so the platform becomes an action surface for external LLM clients with zero extra auth.

Part 3 — Target Architecture

The platform is named Orchєstra in this document. It has three planes. Keeping them separate is what allows AI to be added without destabilising execution.
Control planє — authoring, versioning, connections, RBAC, billing. Synchronous, human-facing.
Data planє — trigger detection, queue, workers, step execution, run history. Asynchronous, machine-facing, the only plane that touches customer systems.
AI planє — Copilot (build time), model gateway + agent runtime (run time), ops copilot (operate time), MCP bridge. Stateless, and always mediated by the control or data plane — it never writes to third-party systems directly.

Non-negotiable invariants
A step stores connectionId , never a credential. Decryption happens only inside a worker, in memory, per invocation.
A run pins flowVersionId . Editing a draft never affects in-flight runs.
The execution context is append-only and keyed by step name. A step may read any executed ancestor, and writes only its own key.
runIdrunIdCopilot has writє accєss to drafts only — enforced at the service layer, not by prompt instruction.
runId
runId

Every model call is attributed: BYO key was used.
or copilotSessionId , tokens in/out, cost, latency, model, and whether a

Every third-party write carries an idempotency key.
The tool registry used by the agent runtime and the MCP server is the same code path, so an agent can never reach an action MCP would refuse.

Part 4 — Data Model

Full DDL.appears on every tenant table for row-level security.

org_id
org_id
idUUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
nameTEXT NOT NULL,
plan_tierTEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'free', credit_balance BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
meter_modeTEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'run',-- 'run' | 'step' (resolves conflict #1) byo_ai_keysBOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
created_atTIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE TABLE users (
idUUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
org_idUUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id) ON DELETE CASCADE, emailCITEXT NOT NULL,
password_hash TEXT, sso_subject TEXT,

);

CREATE TABLE projects (
idUUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
org_idUUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id) ON DELETE CASCADE, nameTEXT NOT NULL,
environment TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'prod'-- dev|stage|prod
);

-- ============ Pieces (integration registry) ============ CREATE TABLE pieces (
idUUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(), nameTEXT NOT NULL,-- 'slack'
versionTEXT NOT NULL,-- semver '1.4.2' display_name TEXT NOT NULL,
categoriesTEXT[] NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',
auth_schemaJSONB NOT NULL,
triggersJSONB NOT NULL,-- [{name, displayName, type, props, sampleSchema}] actionsJSONB NOT NULL,
org_idUUID REFERENCES organizations(id), -- NULL = public piece UNIqUE (name, version, org_id)
);

-- Semantic index powering Copilot app/operation resolution CREATE TABLE piece_embeddings (
idUUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(), piece_name TEXT NOT NULL,
op_kindTEXT NOT NULL,-- 'piece' | 'trigger' | 'action' op_nameTEXT,
textTEXT NOT NULL,-- display name + description + aliases
embeddingVECTOR(1536) NOT NULL,-- pgvector UNIqUE (piece_name, op_kind, op_name)
);
CREATE INDEX ON piece_embeddings USING hnsw (embedding vector_cosine_ops);

-- ============ Connections ============
CREATE TABLE connections (
idUUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
org_idUUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id) ON DELETE CASCADE, project_idUUID REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE, -- NULL = org-global piece_nameTEXT NOT NULL,
labelTEXT NOT NULL,
auth_typeTEXT NOT NULL,-- oauth2|api_key|basic|custom|none
enc_payloadBYTEA NOT NULL,-- AES-256-GCM ciphertext enc_ivBYTEA NOT NULL,
enc_tagBYTEA NOT NULL,
dek_wrappedBYTEA NOT NULL,-- KMS-wrapped data key expires_atTIMESTAMPTZ,
statusTEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'active', -- active|expired|error owner_user_id UUID REFERENCES users(id),
last_used_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
use_countBIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,-- powers Copilot "most used" preference created_atTIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
CREATE INDEX ON connections (org_id, piece_name, status);



-- ============ Flows & versions ============ CREATE TABLE flows (
idUUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
org_idUUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id) ON DELETE CASCADE, project_idUUID NOT NULL REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
nameTEXT NOT NULL,
statusTEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft', -- draft|published|paused draft_definitionJSONB NOT NULL,-- live editable graph published_version_id UUID,
created_byUUID REFERENCES users(id),
originTEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'manual', -- manual|copilot|template|api|import updated_atTIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE TABLE flow_versions (
idUUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
flow_idUUID NOT NULL REFERENCES flows(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
version_number INT NOT NULL, definitionJSONB NOT NULL,
definition_hash TEXT NOT NULL,-- sha256 → enables git-sync / env promotion published_atTIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
published_byUUID REFERENCES users(id), UNIqUE (flow_id, version_number)
);

CREATE TABLE triggers_registry (-- what publish() actually activated idUUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
flow_idUUID NOT NULL REFERENCES flows(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
kindTEXT NOT NULL,-- webhook|poll|cron|form|chat|manual|agent_call webhook_token TEXT UNIqUE,
webhook_secret TEXT,
external_hook_id TEXT,-- id returned by the third-party subscribe API cron_exprTEXT,
poll_cursorJSONB, next_poll_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
activeBOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT true
);

-- ============ Runs ============
CREATE TABLE flow_runs (
idUUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
org_idUUID NOT NULL,
project_idUUID NOT NULL,
flow_idUUID NOT NULL REFERENCES flows(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
flow_version_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES flow_versions(id),
statusTEXT NOT NULL,-- queued|running|paused|succeeded|failed|filtered|cancelled trigger_kindTEXT NOT NULL,
contextJSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}', -- append-only execution context cursor_stepTEXT,-- resume point after dehydration credits_usedINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
billable_steps INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, ai_cost_usdNUMERIC(12,6) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
errorJSONB,
started_atTIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(), finished_atTIMESTAMPTZ
) PARTITION BY RANGE (started_at);

CREATE TABLE run_steps (
idUUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(), run_idUUID NOT NULL,
step_idTEXT NOT NULL, step_typeTEXT NOT NULL,
attemptINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
statusTEXT NOT NULL,
input_json JSONB,-- masked
output_json JSONB,-- masked; >256KB offloaded to blob_ref blob_refTEXT,
error_class TEXT,-- transient|auth|validation|fatal|budget error_json JSONB,
duration_ms INT,
created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
CREATE INDEX ON run_steps (run_id, created_at);

-- ============ Human in the loop ============ CREATE TABLE todos (
idUUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
org_idUUID NOT NULL,
run_idUUID NOT NULL,
step_idTEXT NOT NULL,

assignee_idUUID REFERENCES users(id), assignee_role TEXT,
titleTEXT NOT NULL,
payload_json JSONB NOT NULL, editable_fields JSONB,
statusTEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending', -- pending|approved|rejected|expired resolutionJSONB,
expires_atTIMESTAMPTZ, resolved_atTIMESTAMPTZ
);

-- ============ Built-in Tables ============
CREATE TABLE data_tables (
id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(), org_id UUID NOT NULL, project_id UUID NOT NULL, name TEXT NOT NULL, schema_json JSONB NOT NULL
);
CREATE TABLE data_table_records (
id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
table_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES data_tables(id) ON DELETE CASCADE, data JSONB NOT NULL,
created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(), updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

-- ============ AI plane ============
CREATE TABLE copilot_sessions (
idUUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(), org_idUUID NOT NULL,
project_id UUID NOT NULL,
user_idUUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),
flow_idUUID REFERENCES flows(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
modeTEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'auto_build', -- auto_build | ask_as_you_build transcript JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]',
created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE TABLE copilot_actions (-- audit of every graph mutation Copilot made idUUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
session_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES copilot_sessions(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
kindTEXT NOT NULL,-- plan|add_step|set_field|add_logic|repair|explain patchJSONB NOT NULL,-- RFC-6902 JSON Patch applied to the draft reasoning TEXT,
confidence NUMERIC(4,3), acceptedBOOLEAN,
created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE TABLE agent_transcripts (-- Make-style queryable reasoning artifact idUUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
run_idUUID NOT NULL, step_idTEXT NOT NULL, iteration INT NOT NULL, thoughtTEXT, tool_name TEXT, tool_args JSONB, tool_result JSONB,
tokens_in INT, tokens_out INT, cost_usd NUMERIC(12,6), created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE TABLE ai_usage (
id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
org_id UUID NOT NULL, run_id UUID, copilot_session_id UUID, provider TEXT NOT NULL, model TEXT NOT NULL,
tokens_in INT, tokens_out INT, cost_usd NUMERIC(12,6), byo_key BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
latency_ms INT, created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE TABLE audit_logs (
id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
org_id UUID NOT NULL, actor_id UUID, actor_kind TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'user', -- user|copilot|agent|system action TEXT NOT NULL, target TEXT, metadata JSONB,
created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

-- ============ Row-level security ============ ALTER TABLE flows ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
CREATE POLICY tenant_isolation ON flows


USING (org_id = current_setting('app.current_org', true)::uuid);-- repeat for connections, flow_runs, run_steps, todos, data_tables, copilot_sessions…USING (org_id = current_setting('app.current_org', true)::uuid);-- repeat for connections, flow_runs, run_steps, todos, data_tables, copilot_sessions…
USING (org_id = current_setting('app.current_org', true)::uuid);
-- repeat for connections, flow_runs, run_steps, todos, data_tables, copilot_sessions…
USING (org_id = current_setting('app.current_org', true)::uuid);
-- repeat for connections, flow_runs, run_steps, todos, data_tables, copilot_sessions…

Flow definition JSON schema
This is the contract Copilot generates and the engine executes. Getting it small and strict is what makes AI generation reliable.

{
"schemaVersion": 1, "trigger": {
"id": "trigger",
"type": "piece_trigger",// piece_trigger|webhook|cron|form|chat|manual|agent_call "piece": { "name": "typeform", "version": "^1.0.0" },
"operation": "new_submission", "connectionId": "conn_9f2…", "props": { "formId": "abc123" },
"sampleOutput": { "email": "jane@x.com", "budget": 15000 }
},
"steps": [
{
"id": "step_1",
"name": "extract_intent", "type": "ai",
"model": "auto",// auto → routed by Model Gateway "operation": "extract",
"props": {
"input": "{{trigger.use_case}}",
"schema": { "type": "object",
"properties": { "segment": { "type": "string" },
"urgency": { "type": "string", "enum": ["low","med","high"] } }, "required": ["segment"] }
}
},
{
"id": "step_2", "name": "create_contact", "type": "piece_action", "piece": { "name": "hubspot", "version": "^2.0.0" },
"operation": "create_or_update_contact", "connectionId": "conn_a41…",
"props": { "email": "{{trigger.email}}",
"firstname": "{{trigger.name}}",
"notes": "{{step_1.segment}} / urgency {{step_1.urgency}}" }, "retry": { "maxAttempts": 4, "backoff": "exponential" },
"onError": "fail"// fail|continue|goto:step_id
},
{
"id": "step_3", "type": "router", "branches": [
{ "id": "b_high", "label": "Big deal",
"condition": { "op": "gt", "left": "{{trigger.budget}}", "right": 50000 }, "steps": [
{ "id": "step_4", "type": "approval",
"props": { "title": "Approve enterprise lead {{trigger.name}}", "assigneeRole": "admin", "timeoutHours": 24, "onTimeout": "reject" } },
{ "id": "step_5", "type": "piece_action",
"piece": { "name": "slack", "version": "^1.0.0" }, "operation": "send_message",
"connectionId": "conn_bb7…",
"props": { "channel": "#sales-enterprise",
"text": "Approved: {{trigger.name}} ({{trigger.budget}})" } }
] },
{ "id": "b_default", "label": "Standard", "default": true, "steps": [ /* … */ ] }
]
}
],
"settings": { "concurrency": 10,
"errorNotification": ["email"], "autoPauseAfterFailures": 50,
"dataRetentionDays": 90
}
}

Step types supported by the engine: piece_action , piece_search , ai , agent , code , http , filter , branch , router ,
loop , delay , approval , form_response , subflow , table_op .


Part 5 — Step-by-Step Implementation

Every snippet below is real, coherent TypeScript from one codebase. Follow the steps in order; each one is independently runnable and testable.

Step 2 — Core fiow schema (single source of truth)
Both the builder UI and Copilot validate against this. If Copilot's output does not parse here, it never reaches the database.
// packages/core/src/flow-schema.ts import { z } from 'zod';export const PieceRef = z.object({ name: z.string(), version: z.string().default('*') }); export const Condition: z.ZodType<any> = z.lazy(() =>z.union([ z.object({op: z.enum(['eq','neq','gt','gte','lt','lte','contains','not_contains','starts_with','ends_with','exists','not_exists','matches','in']),left: z.union([z.string(), z.number(), z.boolean()]), right: z.any().optional(),}),z.object({ op: z.literal('and'), operands: z.array(Condition).min(2) }), z.object({ op: z.literal('or'), operands: z.array(Condition).min(2) }), z.object({ op: z.literal('not'), operand: Condition }),]),// packages/core/src/flow-schema.ts import { z } from 'zod';export const PieceRef = z.object({ name: z.string(), version: z.string().default('*') }); export const Condition: z.ZodType<any> = z.lazy(() =>z.union([ z.object({op: z.enum(['eq','neq','gt','gte','lt','lte','contains','not_contains','starts_with','ends_with','exists','not_exists','matches','in']),left: z.union([z.string(), z.number(), z.boolean()]), right: z.any().optional(),}),z.object({ op: z.literal('and'), operands: z.array(Condition).min(2) }), z.object({ op: z.literal('or'), operands: z.array(Condition).min(2) }), z.object({ op: z.literal('not'), operand: Condition }),]),
// packages/core/src/flow-schema.ts import { z } from 'zod';
export const PieceRef = z.object({ name: z.string(), version: z.string().default('*') }); export const Condition: z.ZodType<any> = z.lazy(() =>
z.union([ z.object({
op: z.enum(['eq','neq','gt','gte','lt','lte','contains','not_contains',
'starts_with','ends_with','exists','not_exists','matches','in']),
left: z.union([z.string(), z.number(), z.boolean()]), right: z.any().optional(),
}),

// packages/core/src/flow-schema.ts import { z } from 'zod';
export const PieceRef = z.object({ name: z.string(), version: z.string().default('*') }); export const Condition: z.ZodType<any> = z.lazy(() =>
z.union([ z.object({
op: z.enum(['eq','neq','gt','gte','lt','lte','contains','not_contains',
'starts_with','ends_with','exists','not_exists','matches','in']),
left: z.union([z.string(), z.number(), z.boolean()]), right: z.any().optional(),
}),
z.object({ op: z.literal('and'), operands: z.array(Condition).min(2) }), z.object({ op: z.literal('or'), operands: z.array(Condition).min(2) }), z.object({ op: z.literal('not'), operand: Condition }),
]),

);

const Base = {
id: z.string().regex(/^[a-z0-9_]+$/), name: z.string().optional(),
notes: z.string().optional(), retry: z.object({
maxAttempts: z.number().int().min(1).max(10).default(3), backoff: z.enum(['fixed','exponential']).default('exponential'),
}).optional(),
onError: z.string().default('fail'),// 'fail' | 'continue' | 'goto:step_x'
};

export const Step: z.ZodType<any> = z.lazy(() => z.discriminatedUnion('type', [
z.object({ ...Base, type: z.literal('piece_action'), piece: PieceRef, operation: z.string(), connectionId: z.string().nullable(),
props: z.record(z.any()) }),
z.object({ ...Base, type: z.literal('piece_search'), piece: PieceRef, operation: z.string(), connectionId: z.string().nullable(),
props: z.record(z.any()) }),
z.object({ ...Base, type: z.literal('http'),
props: z.object({ method: z.enum(['GET','POST','PUT','PATCH','DELETE']),
url: z.string(), headers: z.record(z.string()).default({}),
body: z.any().optional(), connectionId: z.string().nullable().optional() }) }), z.object({ ...Base, type: z.literal('ai'),
model: z.string().default('auto'),
operation: z.enum(['generate','summarize','classify','extract','vision', 'transcribe','embed','image']),
props: z.record(z.any()) }),
z.object({ ...Base, type: z.literal('agent'),
props: z.object({ instructions: z.string(), tools: z.array(z.object({
piece: z.string(), operation: z.string(), connectionId: z.string().nullable() })), maxIterations: z.number().int().min(1).max(25).default(8),
maxCreditBudget: z.number().int().default(50), input: z.string() }) }),
z.object({ ...Base, type: z.literal('code'),
props: z.object({ language: z.enum(['js']), source: z.string(),
inputs: z.record(z.any()).default({}), timeoutMs: z.number().default(15000) }) }), z.object({ ...Base, type: z.literal('filter'), condition: Condition }),
z.object({ ...Base, type: z.literal('branch'), condition: Condition, onTrue: z.array(Step), onFalse: z.array(Step) }),
z.object({ ...Base, type: z.literal('router'), branches: z.array(z.object({ id: z.string(), label: z.string(), condition: Condition.optional(), default: z.boolean().optional(), steps: z.array(Step) })) }),
z.object({ ...Base, type: z.literal('loop'),
props: z.object({ items: z.string(), concurrency: z.number().int().min(1).max(20).default(1) }), steps: z.array(Step) }),
z.object({ ...Base, type: z.literal('delay'),
props: z.object({ mode: z.enum(['duration','until']),
seconds: z.number().optional(), untilIso: z.string().optional() }) }), z.object({ ...Base, type: z.literal('approval'),
props: z.object({ title: z.string(), assigneeId: z.string().optional(), assigneeRole: z.string().optional(), editableFields: z.record(z.any()).optional(), timeoutHours: z.number().default(72),
onTimeout: z.enum(['approve','reject','fail']).default('reject') }) }), z.object({ ...Base, type: z.literal('subflow'),
props: z.object({ flowId: z.string(), input: z.record(z.any()), waitForCompletion: z.boolean().default(true) }) }),
z.object({ ...Base, type: z.literal('table_op'), props: z.object({ tableId: z.string(),
op: z.enum(['create','update','delete','get','find']), data: z.any() }) }),
]),
);

export const Trigger = z.discriminatedUnion('type', [
z.object({ id: z.literal('trigger'), type: z.literal('piece_trigger'),
piece: PieceRef, operation: z.string(), connectionId: z.string().nullable(), props: z.record(z.any()), sampleOutput: z.any().optional() }),
z.object({ id: z.literal('trigger'), type: z.literal('webhook'),
props: z.object({ authMode: z.enum(['none','hmac','bearer']).default('hmac') }) }), z.object({ id: z.literal('trigger'), type: z.literal('cron'),
props: z.object({ expression: z.string(), timezone: z.string().default('UTC') }) }), z.object({ id: z.literal('trigger'), type: z.literal('form'),
props: z.object({ fields: z.array(z.any()), respondWith: z.string().optional() }) }),
z.object({ id: z.literal('trigger'), type: z.literal('chat'), props: z.record(z.any()) }),
z.object({ id: z.literal('trigger'), type: z.literal('manual'), props: z.record(z.any()) }), z.object({ id: z.literal('trigger'), type: z.literal('agent_call'),
props: z.object({ description: z.string(), inputSchema: z.any() }) }),
]);


export const FlowDefinition = z.object({ schemaVersion: z.literal(1),trigger: Trigger, steps: z.array(Step), settings: z.object({concurrency: z.number().int().min(1).max(100).default(10),errorNotification: z.array(z.enum(['email','slack','webhook'])).default(['email']), autoPauseAfterFailures: z.number().int().default(50),dataRetentionDays: z.number().int().default(90),}).default({}),});export type TFlowDefinition = z.infer<typeof FlowDefinition>; export type TStep = z.infer<typeof Step>;export const FlowDefinition = z.object({ schemaVersion: z.literal(1),trigger: Trigger, steps: z.array(Step), settings: z.object({concurrency: z.number().int().min(1).max(100).default(10),errorNotification: z.array(z.enum(['email','slack','webhook'])).default(['email']), autoPauseAfterFailures: z.number().int().default(50),dataRetentionDays: z.number().int().default(90),}).default({}),});export type TFlowDefinition = z.infer<typeof FlowDefinition>; export type TStep = z.infer<typeof Step>;
export const FlowDefinition = z.object({ schemaVersion: z.literal(1),
trigger: Trigger, steps: z.array(Step), settings: z.object({
concurrency: z.number().int().min(1).max(100).default(10),
errorNotification: z.array(z.enum(['email','slack','webhook'])).default(['email']), autoPauseAfterFailures: z.number().int().default(50),
dataRetentionDays: z.number().int().default(90),
}).default({}),
});

export type TFlowDefinition = z.infer<typeof FlowDefinition>; export type TStep = z.infer<typeof Step>;
export const FlowDefinition = z.object({ schemaVersion: z.literal(1),
trigger: Trigger, steps: z.array(Step), settings: z.object({
concurrency: z.number().int().min(1).max(100).default(10),
errorNotification: z.array(z.enum(['email','slack','webhook'])).default(['email']), autoPauseAfterFailures: z.number().int().default(50),
dataRetentionDays: z.number().int().default(90),
}).default({}),
});

export type TFlowDefinition = z.infer<typeof FlowDefinition>; export type TStep = z.infer<typeof Step>;
Step 3 — Piece SDK

// packages/pieces-sdk/src/index.ts import { z } from 'zod';
export type AuthType = 'oauth2' | 'api_key' | 'basic' | 'custom' | 'none'; export interface PropDef {
kind: 'shortText'|'longText'|'number'|'checkbox'|'dropdown'|'multiSelect'|'json'|'file'|'dateTime'; displayName: string;
description?: string; required?: boolean;
/** Dynamic dropdowns: recomputed when any refresher prop changes. */ refreshers?: string[];
options?: (ctx: PropContext) => Promise<Array<{ label: string; value: unknown }>>;
/** Fed to Copilot so it can map without a live API call. */ aiHint?: string;
}
export interface PropContext { auth: unknown; propsValue: Record<string, unknown>; } export interface ActionContext<A = unknown> {
auth: A;
propsValue: Record<string, any>;
/** Deduped HTTP client: retries, rate-limit awareness, idempotency header. */ http: HttpClient;
idempotencyKey: string;
store: KeyValueStore;// per-flow persistent scratch space
logger: { info(m: string, meta?: any): void; warn(m: string, meta?: any): void };
}

export interface ActionDef<A = unknown> { name: string;
displayName: string; description: string;
/** Alias terms improve Copilot's semantic resolution recall. */ aliases?: string[];
props: Record<string, PropDef>; outputSchema?: z.ZodTypeAny;
/** Declared so the platform can warn Copilot before generating destructive graphs. */ sideEffect: 'read' | 'create' | 'update' | 'delete';
run(ctx: ActionContext<A>): Promise<unknown>;
}

export interface TriggerDef<A = unknown> { name: string;
displayName: string; description: string; aliases?: string[];
type: 'webhook' | 'polling'; props: Record<string, PropDef>; sampleOutput?: unknown;
onEnable?(ctx: TriggerLifecycleContext<A>): Promise<void>;// subscribe webhook onDisable?(ctx: TriggerLifecycleContext<A>): Promise<void>; // unsubscribe onWebhook?(ctx: WebhookContext<A>): Promise<unknown[]>;// normalise → items poll?(ctx: PollContext<A>): Promise<{ items: unknown[]; cursor: unknown }>;
}

export interface PieceDef {
name: string; displayName: string; version: string; categories: string[]; description: string;
auth: { type: AuthType; props?: Record<string, PropDef>; scopes?: string[]; authUrl?: string; tokenUrl?: string;


validate?: (auth: unknown) => Promise<boolean> }; triggers: TriggerDef[];actions: ActionDef[];}export const createPiece= (d: PieceDef): PieceDef => d;export const createAction = <A>(d: ActionDef<A>): ActionDef<A> => d; export const createTrigger = <A>(d: TriggerDef<A>): TriggerDef<A> => d;export const Property = {ShortText: (o: Omit<PropDef,'kind'>): PropDef => ({ kind: 'shortText', ...o }), LongText: (o: Omit<PropDef,'kind'>): PropDef => ({ kind: 'longText', ...o }), Number: (o: Omit<PropDef,'kind'>): PropDef => ({ kind: 'number', ...o }), Checkbox: (o: Omit<PropDef,'kind'>): PropDef => ({ kind: 'checkbox', ...o }), Dropdown: (o: Omit<PropDef,'kind'>): PropDef => ({ kind: 'dropdown', ...o }), Json:   (o: Omit<PropDef,'kind'>): PropDef => ({ kind: 'json',   ...o }),};validate?: (auth: unknown) => Promise<boolean> }; triggers: TriggerDef[];actions: ActionDef[];}export const createPiece= (d: PieceDef): PieceDef => d;export const createAction = <A>(d: ActionDef<A>): ActionDef<A> => d; export const createTrigger = <A>(d: TriggerDef<A>): TriggerDef<A> => d;export const Property = {ShortText: (o: Omit<PropDef,'kind'>): PropDef => ({ kind: 'shortText', ...o }), LongText: (o: Omit<PropDef,'kind'>): PropDef => ({ kind: 'longText', ...o }), Number: (o: Omit<PropDef,'kind'>): PropDef => ({ kind: 'number', ...o }), Checkbox: (o: Omit<PropDef,'kind'>): PropDef => ({ kind: 'checkbox', ...o }), Dropdown: (o: Omit<PropDef,'kind'>): PropDef => ({ kind: 'dropdown', ...o }), Json:   (o: Omit<PropDef,'kind'>): PropDef => ({ kind: 'json',   ...o }),};
validate?: (auth: unknown) => Promise<boolean> }; triggers: TriggerDef[];
actions: ActionDef[];
}

};

}

// packages/pieces/slack/src/index.ts
import { createPiece, createAction, createTrigger, Property } from '@orchestra/pieces-sdk'; import { z } from 'zod';
type SlackAuth = { access_token: string; team?: { name?: string } }; export const sendMessage = createAction<SlackAuth>({
name: 'send_message',
displayName: 'Send Channel Message',
description: 'Post a message to a Slack channel.',
aliases: ['notify slack', 'post to slack', 'alert team', 'slack message'], sideEffect: 'create',
props: {
channel: Property.Dropdown({
displayName: 'Channel', required: true, refreshers: ['auth'],
aiHint: 'Slack channel id or #name. Prefer a channel the user names in their prompt.', options: async ({ auth }) => {
const a = auth as SlackAuth;
const r = await fetch('https://slack.com/api/conversations.list?limit=1000',
{ headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${a.access_token}` } }); const j = await r.json();
return (j.channels ?? []).map((c: any) => ({ label: `#${c.name}`, value: c.id }));
},
}),
text: Property.LongText({ displayName: 'Message', required: true, aiHint: 'Supports {{step.field}} tokens and Slack mrkdwn.' }),
thread_ts: Property.ShortText({ displayName: 'Thread timestamp', required: false }),
},
outputSchema: z.object({ ok: z.boolean(), ts: z.string().optional(),
channel: z.string().optional() }), async run({ auth, propsValue, http, idempotencyKey }) {
return http.post('https://slack.com/api/chat.postMessage', { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${auth.access_token}`,
'X-Idempotency-Key': idempotencyKey },
body: { channel: propsValue.channel, text: propsValue.text, thread_ts: propsValue.thread_ts },
});
},
});

export const newMessage = createTrigger<SlackAuth>({
name: 'new_message', displayName: 'New Message in Channel', description: 'Fires when a message is posted to a channel.', aliases: ['slack message received', 'when someone posts in slack'], type: 'webhook',
props: { channel: Property.Dropdown({ displayName: 'Channel', required: true,
refreshers: ['auth'] }) },
sampleOutput: { channel: 'C123', user: 'U456', text: 'hello', ts: '1724500000.001' }, async onEnable({ auth, webhookUrl, propsValue, store }) {
const res = await fetch('https://slack.com/api/apps.event.authorizations.subscribe', { method: 'POST',
headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${auth.access_token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
body: JSON.stringify({ url: webhookUrl, channel: propsValue.channel }),
}).then(r => r.json());
await store.put('external_hook_id', res.subscription_id);
},
async onDisable({ auth, store }) {
const id = await store.get<string>('external_hook_id');

Step 4 — Piece registry + semantic catalog (Copilot's map of the world)
// apps/api/src/pieces/registry.service.ts import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';import type { PieceDef, ActionDef, TriggerDef } from '@orchestra/pieces-sdk';export interface OperationCard {// the compact form given to the LLMpiece: string; pieceDisplay: string; kind: 'trigger' | 'action'; operation: string; display: string; description: string; aliases: string[]; sideEffect?: string;requiredProps: string[]; allProps: Record<string, { type: string; hint?: string;required: boolean }>;outputFields: string[];}@Injectable()export class PieceRegistry {private pieces = new Map<string, PieceDef>();register(p: PieceDef) { this.pieces.set(`${p.name}@${p.version}`, p); } resolve(name: string, versionRange = '*'): PieceDef {const match = [...this.pieces.values()].filter(p => p.name === name).sort((a, b) => b.version.localeCompare(a.version, undefined, { numeric: true }))[0]; if (!match) throw new Error(`PIECE_NOT_FOUND:${name}`);return match;}getAction(piece: string, op: string): ActionDef {const a = this.resolve(piece).actions.find(x => x.name === op); if (!a) throw new Error(`ACTION_NOT_FOUND:${piece}.${op}`); return a;}getTrigger(piece: string, op: string): TriggerDef {const t = this.resolve(piece).triggers.find(x => x.name === op); if (!t) throw new Error(`TRIGGER_NOT_FOUND:${piece}.${op}`); return t;}/** Flatten the whole catalog into LLM-consumable cards. */ cards(): OperationCard[] {const out: OperationCard[] = [];for (const p of this.pieces.values()) {const push = (kind: 'trigger'|'action', d: any) => out.push({ piece: p.name, pieceDisplay: p.displayName, kind,operation: d.name, display: d.displayName, description: d.description, aliases: d.aliases ?? [], sideEffect: d.sideEffect,requiredProps: Object.entries(d.props ?? {}).filter(([, v]: any) => v.required).map(([k]) => k),allProps: Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(d.props ?? {}).map(([k, v]: any) => [k, { type: v.kind, hint: v.aiHint, required: !!v.required }])),outputFields: Object.keys((d.sampleOutput ?? {}) as object),});p.triggers.forEach(t => push('trigger', t)); p.actions.forEach(a => push('action', a));// apps/api/src/pieces/registry.service.ts import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';import type { PieceDef, ActionDef, TriggerDef } from '@orchestra/pieces-sdk';export interface OperationCard {// the compact form given to the LLMpiece: string; pieceDisplay: string; kind: 'trigger' | 'action'; operation: string; display: string; description: string; aliases: string[]; sideEffect?: string;requiredProps: string[]; allProps: Record<string, { type: string; hint?: string;required: boolean }>;outputFields: string[];}@Injectable()export class PieceRegistry {private pieces = new Map<string, PieceDef>();register(p: PieceDef) { this.pieces.set(`${p.name}@${p.version}`, p); } resolve(name: string, versionRange = '*'): PieceDef {const match = [...this.pieces.values()].filter(p => p.name === name).sort((a, b) => b.version.localeCompare(a.version, undefined, { numeric: true }))[0]; if (!match) throw new Error(`PIECE_NOT_FOUND:${name}`);return match;}getAction(piece: string, op: string): ActionDef {const a = this.resolve(piece).actions.find(x => x.name === op); if (!a) throw new Error(`ACTION_NOT_FOUND:${piece}.${op}`); return a;}getTrigger(piece: string, op: string): TriggerDef {const t = this.resolve(piece).triggers.find(x => x.name === op); if (!t) throw new Error(`TRIGGER_NOT_FOUND:${piece}.${op}`); return t;}/** Flatten the whole catalog into LLM-consumable cards. */ cards(): OperationCard[] {const out: OperationCard[] = [];for (const p of this.pieces.values()) {const push = (kind: 'trigger'|'action', d: any) => out.push({ piece: p.name, pieceDisplay: p.displayName, kind,operation: d.name, display: d.displayName, description: d.description, aliases: d.aliases ?? [], sideEffect: d.sideEffect,requiredProps: Object.entries(d.props ?? {}).filter(([, v]: any) => v.required).map(([k]) => k),allProps: Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(d.props ?? {}).map(([k, v]: any) => [k, { type: v.kind, hint: v.aiHint, required: !!v.required }])),outputFields: Object.keys((d.sampleOutput ?? {}) as object),});p.triggers.forEach(t => push('trigger', t)); p.actions.forEach(a => push('action', a));
// apps/api/src/pieces/registry.service.ts import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import type { PieceDef, ActionDef, TriggerDef } from '@orchestra/pieces-sdk';
export interface OperationCard {
// the compact form given to the LLM
piece: string; pieceDisplay: string; kind: 'trigger' | 'action'; operation: string; display: string; description: string; aliases: string[]; sideEffect?: string;
requiredProps: string[]; allProps: Record<string, { type: string; hint?: string;
required: boolean }>;
outputFields: string[];
}

@Injectable()
export class PieceRegistry {
private pieces = new Map<string, PieceDef>();
register(p: PieceDef) { this.pieces.set(`${p.name}@${p.version}`, p); } resolve(name: string, versionRange = '*'): PieceDef {
const match = [...this.pieces.values()]
.filter(p => p.name === name)
.sort((a, b) => b.version.localeCompare(a.version, undefined, { numeric: true }))[0]; if (!match) throw new Error(`PIECE_NOT_FOUND:${name}`);
return match;
}

getAction(piece: string, op: string): ActionDef {
const a = this.resolve(piece).actions.find(x => x.name === op); if (!a) throw new Error(`ACTION_NOT_FOUND:${piece}.${op}`); return a;
}
getTrigger(piece: string, op: string): TriggerDef {
const t = this.resolve(piece).triggers.find(x => x.name === op); if (!t) throw new Error(`TRIGGER_NOT_FOUND:${piece}.${op}`); return t;
}

/** Flatten the whole catalog into LLM-consumable cards. */ cards(): OperationCard[] {
const out: OperationCard[] = [];
for (const p of this.pieces.values()) {
const push = (kind: 'trigger'|'action', d: any) => out.push({ piece: p.name, pieceDisplay: p.displayName, kind,
operation: d.name, display: d.displayName, description: d.description, aliases: d.aliases ?? [], sideEffect: d.sideEffect,
requiredProps: Object.entries(d.props ?? {})
.filter(([, v]: any) => v.required).map(([k]) => k),
allProps: Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(d.props ?? {}).map(([k, v]: any) => [k, { type: v.kind, hint: v.aiHint, required: !!v.required }])),
outputFields: Object.keys((d.sampleOutput ?? {}) as object),
});
p.triggers.forEach(t => push('trigger', t)); p.actions.forEach(a => push('action', a));
// apps/api/src/pieces/registry.service.ts import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import type { PieceDef, ActionDef, TriggerDef } from '@orchestra/pieces-sdk';
export interface OperationCard {
// the compact form given to the LLM
piece: string; pieceDisplay: string; kind: 'trigger' | 'action'; operation: string; display: string; description: string; aliases: string[]; sideEffect?: string;
requiredProps: string[]; allProps: Record<string, { type: string; hint?: string;
required: boolean }>;
outputFields: string[];
}

@Injectable()
export class PieceRegistry {
private pieces = new Map<string, PieceDef>();
register(p: PieceDef) { this.pieces.set(`${p.name}@${p.version}`, p); } resolve(name: string, versionRange = '*'): PieceDef {
const match = [...this.pieces.values()]
.filter(p => p.name === name)
.sort((a, b) => b.version.localeCompare(a.version, undefined, { numeric: true }))[0]; if (!match) throw new Error(`PIECE_NOT_FOUND:${name}`);
return match;
}

getAction(piece: string, op: string): ActionDef {
const a = this.resolve(piece).actions.find(x => x.name === op); if (!a) throw new Error(`ACTION_NOT_FOUND:${piece}.${op}`); return a;
}
getTrigger(piece: string, op: string): TriggerDef {
const t = this.resolve(piece).triggers.find(x => x.name === op); if (!t) throw new Error(`TRIGGER_NOT_FOUND:${piece}.${op}`); return t;
}

/** Flatten the whole catalog into LLM-consumable cards. */ cards(): OperationCard[] {
const out: OperationCard[] = [];
for (const p of this.pieces.values()) {
const push = (kind: 'trigger'|'action', d: any) => out.push({ piece: p.name, pieceDisplay: p.displayName, kind,
operation: d.name, display: d.displayName, description: d.description, aliases: d.aliases ?? [], sideEffect: d.sideEffect,
requiredProps: Object.entries(d.props ?? {})
.filter(([, v]: any) => v.required).map(([k]) => k),
allProps: Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(d.props ?? {}).map(([k, v]: any) => [k, { type: v.kind, hint: v.aiHint, required: !!v.required }])),
outputFields: Object.keys((d.sampleOutput ?? {}) as object),
});
p.triggers.forEach(t => push('trigger', t)); p.actions.forEach(a => push('action', a));


}return out;}}}return out;}}
}
return out;
}
}
}
return out;
}
}
// apps/api/src/pieces/catalog-index.service.ts — embeddings for app resolution import { Injectable, OnModuleInit } from '@nestjs/common';import { PieceRegistry, OperationCard } from './registry.service'; import { ModelGateway } from '@orchestra/ai';import { Db } from '@orchestra/db';@Injectable()export class CatalogIndex implements OnModuleInit {constructor(private reg: PieceRegistry, private ai: ModelGateway, private db: Db) {} async onModuleInit() { await this.reindex(); }/** Re-run whenever a piece is published; cheap and idempotent. */ async reindex() {const cards = this.reg.cards(); const texts = cards.map(c =>`${c.pieceDisplay} — ${c.display}. ${c.description} ` +`Also known as: ${c.aliases.join(', ')}. // apps/api/src/pieces/catalog-index.service.ts — embeddings for app resolution import { Injectable, OnModuleInit } from '@nestjs/common';
import { PieceRegistry, OperationCard } from './registry.service'; import { ModelGateway } from '@orchestra/ai';
import { Db } from '@orchestra/db';

@Injectable()
export class CatalogIndex implements OnModuleInit {
constructor(private reg: PieceRegistry, private ai: ModelGateway, private db: Db) {} async onModuleInit() { await this.reindex(); }
/** Re-run whenever a piece is published; cheap and idempotent. */ async reindex() {
const cards = this.reg.cards(); const texts = cards.map(c =>
`${c.pieceDisplay} — ${c.display}. ${c.description} ` +
`Also known as: ${c.aliases.join(', ')}. Kind: ${c.kind}.`); const vectors = await this.ai.embedBatch(texts);
await this.db.upsertPieceEmbeddings(cards.map((c, i) => ({ pieceName: c.piece, opKind: c.kind, opName: c.operation, text: texts[i], embedding: vectors[i],
})));
}

/** Hybrid retrieval: vector + trigram keyword, reciprocal-rank fused. */
async search(query: string, kind?: 'trigger'|'action', k = 12): Promise<OperationCard[]> { const [vec] = await this.ai.embedBatch([query]);
const [dense, lexical] = await Promise.all([ this.db.searchEmbeddings(vec, kind, k * 2), this.db.searchTrigram(query, kind, k * 2),
]);
const score = new Map<string, number>();
const add = (rows: any[], w: number) => rows.forEach((r, i) => { const key = `${r.piece_name}:${r.op_kind}:${r.op_name}`; score.set(key, (score.get(key) ?? 0) + w / (60 + i));
});
add(dense, 1); add(lexical, 0.8); const all = this.reg.cards(); return [...score.entries()]
.sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]).slice(0, k)
.map(([key]) => {
const [piece, kindK, op] = key.split(':');
return all.find(c => c.piece === piece && c.kind === kindK && c.operation === op)!;
})
.filter(Boolean);
}
}
// apps/api/src/pieces/catalog-index.service.ts — embeddings for app resolution import { Injectable, OnModuleInit } from '@nestjs/common';
import { PieceRegistry, OperationCard } from './registry.service'; import { ModelGateway } from '@orchestra/ai';
import { Db } from '@orchestra/db';

@Injectable()
export class CatalogIndex implements OnModuleInit {
constructor(private reg: PieceRegistry, private ai: ModelGateway, private db: Db) {} async onModuleInit() { await this.reindex(); }
/** Re-run whenever a piece is published; cheap and idempotent. */ async reindex() {
const cards = this.reg.cards(); const texts = cards.map(c =>
`${c.pieceDisplay} — ${c.display}. ${c.description} ` +
`Also known as: ${c.aliases.join(', ')}. Kind: ${c.kind}.`); const vectors = await this.ai.embedBatch(texts);
await this.db.upsertPieceEmbeddings(cards.map((c, i) => ({ pieceName: c.piece, opKind: c.kind, opName: c.operation, text: texts[i], embedding: vectors[i],
})));
}

/** Hybrid retrieval: vector + trigram keyword, reciprocal-rank fused. */
async search(query: string, kind?: 'trigger'|'action', k = 12): Promise<OperationCard[]> { const [vec] = await this.ai.embedBatch([query]);
const [dense, lexical] = await Promise.all([ this.db.searchEmbeddings(vec, kind, k * 2), this.db.searchTrigram(query, kind, k * 2),
]);
const score = new Map<string, number>();
const add = (rows: any[], w: number) => rows.forEach((r, i) => { const key = `${r.piece_name}:${r.op_kind}:${r.op_name}`; score.set(key, (score.get(key) ?? 0) + w / (60 + i));
});
add(dense, 1); add(lexical, 0.8); const all = this.reg.cards(); return [...score.entries()]
.sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]).slice(0, k)
.map(([key]) => {
const [piece, kindK, op] = key.split(':');
return all.find(c => c.piece === piece && c.kind === kindK && c.operation === op)!;
})
.filter(Boolean);
}
}

Step 5 — Envelope encryption and the Connection service
// packages/crypto/src/envelope.tsimport { createCipheriv, createDecipheriv, randomBytes } from 'node:crypto';export interface Kms {generateDataKey(orgId: string): Promise<{ plaintext: Buffer; wrapped: Buffer }>; unwrap(orgId: string, wrapped: Buffer): Promise<Buffer>;}export interface Sealed { payload: Buffer; iv: Buffer; tag: Buffer; dekWrapped: Buffer } export async function seal(kms: Kms, orgId: string, plain: unknown): Promise<Sealed> {const { plaintext: dek, wrapped } = await kms.generateDataKey(orgId); const iv = randomBytes(12);const c = createCipheriv('aes-256-gcm', dek, iv);const payload = Buffer.concat([c.update(JSON.stringify(plain), 'utf8'), c.final()]); dek.fill(0);return { payload, iv, tag: c.getAuthTag(), dekWrapped: wrapped };}export async function open<T>(kms: Kms, orgId: string, s: Sealed): Promise<T> { const dek = await kms.unwrap(orgId, s.dekWrapped);// packages/crypto/src/envelope.tsimport { createCipheriv, createDecipheriv, randomBytes } from 'node:crypto';export interface Kms {generateDataKey(orgId: string): Promise<{ plaintext: Buffer; wrapped: Buffer }>; unwrap(orgId: string, wrapped: Buffer): Promise<Buffer>;}export interface Sealed { payload: Buffer; iv: Buffer; tag: Buffer; dekWrapped: Buffer } export async function seal(kms: Kms, orgId: string, plain: unknown): Promise<Sealed> {const { plaintext: dek, wrapped } = await kms.generateDataKey(orgId); const iv = randomBytes(12);const c = createCipheriv('aes-256-gcm', dek, iv);const payload = Buffer.concat([c.update(JSON.stringify(plain), 'utf8'), c.final()]); dek.fill(0);return { payload, iv, tag: c.getAuthTag(), dekWrapped: wrapped };}export async function open<T>(kms: Kms, orgId: string, s: Sealed): Promise<T> { const dek = await kms.unwrap(orgId, s.dekWrapped);
// packages/crypto/src/envelope.ts
import { createCipheriv, createDecipheriv, randomBytes } from 'node:crypto';

export interface Kms {
generateDataKey(orgId: string): Promise<{ plaintext: Buffer; wrapped: Buffer }>; unwrap(orgId: string, wrapped: Buffer): Promise<Buffer>;
}
export interface Sealed { payload: Buffer; iv: Buffer; tag: Buffer; dekWrapped: Buffer } export async function seal(kms: Kms, orgId: string, plain: unknown): Promise<Sealed> {
const { plaintext: dek, wrapped } = await kms.generateDataKey(orgId); const iv = randomBytes(12);
const c = createCipheriv('aes-256-gcm', dek, iv);
const payload = Buffer.concat([c.update(JSON.stringify(plain), 'utf8'), c.final()]); dek.fill(0);
return { payload, iv, tag: c.getAuthTag(), dekWrapped: wrapped };
}

export interface Kms {

export interface Sealed { payload: Buffer; iv: Buffer; tag: Buffer; dekWrapped: Buffer } export async function seal(kms: Kms, orgId: string, plain: unknown): Promise<Sealed> {

}

// apps/api/src/connections/connections.service.ts
import { Injectable, ForbiddenException } from '@nestjs/common'; import { seal, open, Kms } from '@orchestra/crypto';
import { Db } from '@orchestra/db';
import { PieceRegistry } from '../pieces/registry.service';

@Injectable()
export class ConnectionsService {
constructor(private db: Db, private kms: Kms, private reg: PieceRegistry) {}

/** API-key / basic / custom auth: validate, then seal. */
async createDirect(ctx: { orgId: string; projectId: string; userId: string }, input: { pieceName: string; label: string;
authType: 'api_key'|'basic'|'custom'; payload: any }) { const piece = this.reg.resolve(input.pieceName);
if (piece.auth.validate && !(await piece.auth.validate(input.payload))) throw new ForbiddenException('CONNECTION_VALIDATION_FAILED');
const sealed = await seal(this.kms, ctx.orgId, input.payload);
return this.db.connections.insert({ ...ctx, ...input, sealed, status: 'active' });
}

/** Never returns plaintext to HTTP callers — worker-only path. */ async loadForExecution(orgId: string, connectionId: string) {
const row = await this.db.connections.byId(orgId, connectionId); if (!row) throw new Error('CONNECTION_NOT_FOUND');
if (row.status === 'error') throw new Error('CONNECTION_NEEDS_RECONNECT'); let auth = await open<any>(this.kms, orgId, row.sealed);
if (row.authType === 'oauth2' && this.isExpiring(row.expiresAt)) { auth = await this.refresh(orgId, row, auth);
}
await this.db.connections.touch(connectionId);// last_used_at + use_count return { auth, pieceName: row.pieceName };
}

private isExpiring(exp?: Date | null) {
return !!exp && exp.getTime() - Date.now() < 120_000;// 2-minute skew
}

/** Silent refresh; a failure marks the connection so the UI can prompt reconnect. */ private async refresh(orgId: string, row: any, auth: any) {
const piece = this.reg.resolve(row.pieceName); try {
const res = await fetch(piece.auth.tokenUrl!, { method: 'POST',
headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded' }, body: new URLSearchParams({
grant_type: 'refresh_token', refresh_token: auth.refresh_token, client_id: process.env[`OAUTH_${row.pieceName.toUpperCase()}_ID`]!,
client_secret: process.env[`OAUTH_${row.pieceName.toUpperCase()}_SECRET`]!,
}),
});
if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`REFRESH_HTTP_${res.status}`); const t = await res.json();
const next = { ...auth, ...t, refresh_token: t.refresh_token ?? auth.refresh_token }; const sealed = await seal(this.kms, orgId, next);
await this.db.connections.update(row.id, { sealed, status: 'active',
expiresAt: t.expires_in ? new Date(Date.now() + t.expires_in * 1000) : null,
});
return next;
} catch (e) {
await this.db.connections.update(row.id, { status: 'error' }); await this.db.notifications.connectionBroken(orgId, row.id); throw new Error('CONNECTION_NEEDS_RECONNECT');
}
}

/**
* Copilot's connection picker. Mirrors Zapier's documented preference order:
* account-email match → most used → any accessible → else prompt the human.
* Copilot may SELECT an existing connection but can never CREATE one.
*/
async pickForCopilot(orgId: string, projectId: string, userEmail: string,


pieceName: string) {const candidates = await this.db.connections.listUsable(orgId, projectId, pieceName); if (!candidates.length) return { connectionId: null, needsHuman: true as const };const byEmail = candidates.find(c => c.label?.toLowerCase().includes(userEmail.toLowerCase())); const mostUsed = [...candidates].sort((a, b) => Number(b.useCount) - Number(a.useCount))[0]; const chosen = byEmail ?? mostUsed ?? candidates[0];return { connectionId: chosen.id, label: chosen.label, needsHuman: false as const };}}pieceName: string) {const candidates = await this.db.connections.listUsable(orgId, projectId, pieceName); if (!candidates.length) return { connectionId: null, needsHuman: true as const };const byEmail = candidates.find(c => c.label?.toLowerCase().includes(userEmail.toLowerCase())); const mostUsed = [...candidates].sort((a, b) => Number(b.useCount) - Number(a.useCount))[0]; const chosen = byEmail ?? mostUsed ?? candidates[0];return { connectionId: chosen.id, label: chosen.label, needsHuman: false as const };}}
pieceName: string) {
const candidates = await this.db.connections.listUsable(orgId, projectId, pieceName); if (!candidates.length) return { connectionId: null, needsHuman: true as const };
const byEmail = candidates.find(c => c.label?.toLowerCase().includes(userEmail.toLowerCase())); const mostUsed = [...candidates].sort((a, b) => Number(b.useCount) - Number(a.useCount))[0]; const chosen = byEmail ?? mostUsed ?? candidates[0];
return { connectionId: chosen.id, label: chosen.label, needsHuman: false as const };
}
}
pieceName: string) {
const candidates = await this.db.connections.listUsable(orgId, projectId, pieceName); if (!candidates.length) return { connectionId: null, needsHuman: true as const };
const byEmail = candidates.find(c => c.label?.toLowerCase().includes(userEmail.toLowerCase())); const mostUsed = [...candidates].sort((a, b) => Number(b.useCount) - Number(a.useCount))[0]; const chosen = byEmail ?? mostUsed ?? candidates[0];
return { connectionId: chosen.id, label: chosen.label, needsHuman: false as const };

OAuth2 controller

// apps/api/src/connections/oauth.controller.ts
import { Controller, Get, query, Res, Req } from '@nestjs/common'; import { randomBytes, createHash } from 'node:crypto';

@Controller('oauth')
export class OAuthController { constructor(private svc: OAuthService) {}

@Get('start')
async start(@query() q: { piece: string; projectId: string }, @Req() req, @Res() res) { const piece = this.svc.registry.resolve(q.piece);
const state = randomBytes(24).toString('base64url'); const verifier = randomBytes(32).toString('base64url'); await this.svc.cache.set(`oauth:${state}`, {
orgId: req.user.orgId, userId: req.user.id, projectId: q.projectId, piece: q.piece, verifier,
}, 600);
const url = new URL(piece.auth.authUrl!);
url.searchParams.set('client_id', process.env[`OAUTH_${q.piece.toUpperCase()}_ID`]!); url.searchParams.set('redirect_uri', `${process.env.PUBLIC_URL}/oauth/callback`); url.searchParams.set('response_type', 'code');
url.searchParams.set('scope', (piece.auth.scopes ?? []).join(' ')); url.searchParams.set('state', state); url.searchParams.set('code_challenge',
createHash('sha256').update(verifier).digest('base64url')); url.searchParams.set('code_challenge_method', 'S256');
return res.redirect(url.toString());
}

@Get('callback')
async callback(@query() q: { code: string; state: string }, @Res() res) { const s = await this.svc.cache.take(`oauth:${q.state}`);
if (!s) return res.status(400).send('INVALID_STATE');// CSRF guard
const conn = await this.svc.exchangeAndStore(s, q.code); // server-to-server only
// The popup posts only an opaque id back to the opener — never a token. return res.type('html').send(`<script>
window.opener.postMessage(${JSON.stringify({ type: 'connection:created', connectionId: conn.id, label: conn.label })}, '*'); window.close();
</script>`);
}
}

Step 6 — Flow service: draft, validate, publish, version

// apps/api/src/flows/flows.service.ts
import { Injectable, BadRequestException } from '@nestjs/common'; import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
import { FlowDefinition, TFlowDefinition } from '@orchestra/core'; import { FlowValidator } from './flow-validator';

@Injectable()
export class FlowsService {
constructor(private db: Db, private validator: FlowValidator, private triggers: TriggerActivationService) {}

async saveDraft(orgId: string, flowId: string, def: unknown) { const parsed = FlowDefinition.safeParse(def);
if (!parsed.success)
throw new BadRequestException({ code: 'SCHEMA_INVALID', issues: parsed.error.issues }); await this.db.flows.update(orgId, flowId, { draftDefinition: parsed.data });
return { ok: true, warnings: await this.validator.lint(orgId, parsed.data) };
}

/** The commit gate. Publishing is the only path that touches external systems. */
// apps/api/src/flows/flows.service.ts
import { Injectable, BadRequestException } from '@nestjs/common'; import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
import { FlowDefinition, TFlowDefinition } from '@orchestra/core'; import { FlowValidator } from './flow-validator';

@Injectable()
export class FlowsService {
constructor(private db: Db, private validator: FlowValidator, private triggers: TriggerActivationService) {}

async saveDraft(orgId: string, flowId: string, def: unknown) { const parsed = FlowDefinition.safeParse(def);
if (!parsed.success)
throw new BadRequestException({ code: 'SCHEMA_INVALID', issues: parsed.error.issues }); await this.db.flows.update(orgId, flowId, { draftDefinition: parsed.data });
return { ok: true, warnings: await this.validator.lint(orgId, parsed.data) };
}

/** The commit gate. Publishing is the only path that touches external systems. */


async publish(orgId: string, flowId: string, userId: string) { const flow = await this.db.flows.byId(orgId, flowId);const def = FlowDefinition.parse(flow.draftDefinition);const blocking = (await this.validator.lint(orgId, def)).filter(i => i.severity === 'error'); if (blocking.length)throw new BadRequestException({ code: 'NOT_PUBLISHABLE', issues: blocking });const hash = createHash('sha256').update(JSON.stringify(def)).digest('hex');const version = await this.db.flowVersions.insert({ flowId, definition: def, definitionHash: hash,versionNumber: (flow.latestVersionNumber ?? 0) + 1, publishedBy: userId,});await this.triggers.deactivate(flow);await this.triggers.activate(orgId, flow, def); await this.db.flows.update(orgId, flowId,// idempotent teardown// subscribe/schedule{ status: 'published', publishedVersionId: version.id });await this.db.audit.log({ orgId, actorId: userId, action: 'flow.publish', target: flowId, metadata: { version: version.versionNumber, hash } });return version;}async rollback(orgId: string, flowId: string, versionNumber: number, userId: string) { const v = await this.db.flowVersions.byNumber(flowId, versionNumber);await this.db.flows.update(orgId, flowId, { draftDefinition: v.definition }); return this.publish(orgId, flowId, userId);}}async publish(orgId: string, flowId: string, userId: string) { const flow = await this.db.flows.byId(orgId, flowId);const def = FlowDefinition.parse(flow.draftDefinition);const blocking = (await this.validator.lint(orgId, def)).filter(i => i.severity === 'error'); if (blocking.length)throw new BadRequestException({ code: 'NOT_PUBLISHABLE', issues: blocking });const hash = createHash('sha256').update(JSON.stringify(def)).digest('hex');const version = await this.db.flowVersions.insert({ flowId, definition: def, definitionHash: hash,versionNumber: (flow.latestVersionNumber ?? 0) + 1, publishedBy: userId,});await this.triggers.deactivate(flow);await this.triggers.activate(orgId, flow, def); await this.db.flows.update(orgId, flowId,// idempotent teardown// subscribe/schedule{ status: 'published', publishedVersionId: version.id });await this.db.audit.log({ orgId, actorId: userId, action: 'flow.publish', target: flowId, metadata: { version: version.versionNumber, hash } });return version;}async rollback(orgId: string, flowId: string, versionNumber: number, userId: string) { const v = await this.db.flowVersions.byNumber(flowId, versionNumber);await this.db.flows.update(orgId, flowId, { draftDefinition: v.definition }); return this.publish(orgId, flowId, userId);}}
async publish(orgId: string, flowId: string, userId: string) { const flow = await this.db.flows.byId(orgId, flowId);
const def = FlowDefinition.parse(flow.draftDefinition);

const blocking = (await this.validator.lint(orgId, def))
.filter(i => i.severity === 'error'); if (blocking.length)
throw new BadRequestException({ code: 'NOT_PUBLISHABLE', issues: blocking });

const hash = createHash('sha256')
.update(JSON.stringify(def)).digest('hex');
const version = await this.db.flowVersions.insert({ flowId, definition: def, definitionHash: hash,
versionNumber: (flow.latestVersionNumber ?? 0) + 1, publishedBy: userId,
});
await this.triggers.deactivate(flow);
await this.triggers.activate(orgId, flow, def); await this.db.flows.update(orgId, flowId,
// idempotent teardown
// subscribe/schedule
{ status: 'published', publishedVersionId: version.id });
await this.db.audit.log({ orgId, actorId: userId, action: 'flow.publish', target: flowId, metadata: { version: version.versionNumber, hash } });
return version;
}

async rollback(orgId: string, flowId: string, versionNumber: number, userId: string) { const v = await this.db.flowVersions.byNumber(flowId, versionNumber);
await this.db.flows.update(orgId, flowId, { draftDefinition: v.definition }); return this.publish(orgId, flowId, userId);
}
}
async publish(orgId: string, flowId: string, userId: string) { const flow = await this.db.flows.byId(orgId, flowId);
const def = FlowDefinition.parse(flow.draftDefinition);

const blocking = (await this.validator.lint(orgId, def))
.filter(i => i.severity === 'error'); if (blocking.length)
throw new BadRequestException({ code: 'NOT_PUBLISHABLE', issues: blocking });

const hash = createHash('sha256')
.update(JSON.stringify(def)).digest('hex');
const version = await this.db.flowVersions.insert({ flowId, definition: def, definitionHash: hash,
versionNumber: (flow.latestVersionNumber ?? 0) + 1, publishedBy: userId,
});
await this.triggers.deactivate(flow);
await this.triggers.activate(orgId, flow, def); await this.db.flows.update(orgId, flowId,
// idempotent teardown
// subscribe/schedule
{ status: 'published', publishedVersionId: version.id });
await this.db.audit.log({ orgId, actorId: userId, action: 'flow.publish', target: flowId, metadata: { version: version.versionNumber, hash } });
return version;
}

async rollback(orgId: string, flowId: string, versionNumber: number, userId: string) { const v = await this.db.flowVersions.byNumber(flowId, versionNumber);
await this.db.flows.update(orgId, flowId, { draftDefinition: v.definition }); return this.publish(orgId, flowId, userId);
}
}

// apps/api/src/flows/flow-validator.ts — shared by the UI, the API, and Copilot import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { TFlowDefinition, TStep } from '@orchestra/core';
export type Issue = { severity: 'error'|'warning'; path: string; code: string; message: string }; @Injectable()
export class FlowValidator {
constructor(private reg: PieceRegistry, private db: Db) {}

async lint(orgId: string, def: TFlowDefinition): Promise<Issue[]> { const issues: Issue[] = [];
const seen = new Set<string>(['trigger']);

// 1. Trigger must resolve and be connected if (def.trigger.type === 'piece_trigger') {
this.checkOp(issues, 'trigger', def.trigger.piece.name, def.trigger.operation, 'trigger');
if (!def.trigger.connectionId)
issues.push({ severity: 'error', path: 'trigger.connectionId', code: 'CONNECTION_REqUIRED',
message: `Connect your ${def.trigger.piece.name} account to continue.` });
}

const walk = async (steps: TStep[], base: string) => { for (const [i, s] of steps.entries()) {
const path = `${base}[${i}]`; if (seen.has(s.id))
issues.push({ severity: 'error', path, code: 'DUPLICATE_STEP_ID', message: `Step id ${s.id} is used more than once.` });
seen.add(s.id);

if (s.type === 'piece_action' || s.type === 'piece_search') { this.checkOp(issues, path, s.piece.name, s.operation, 'action'); if (!s.connectionId)
issues.push({ severity: 'error', path: `${path}.connectionId`, code: 'CONNECTION_REqUIRED',
message: `Connect ${s.piece.name} to run this step.` }); const def2 = this.reg.getAction(s.piece.name, s.operation); for (const [k, p] of Object.entries(def2.props))
if (p.required && (s.props[k] === undefined || s.props[k] === '')) issues.push({ severity: 'error', path: `${path}.props.${k}`,
code: 'REqUIRED_FIELD_EMPTY',
message: `${p.displayName} is required.` });
}

// 2. Every {{token}} must reference a step that already executed for (const tok of this.tokens(s))
// apps/api/src/flows/flow-validator.ts — shared by the UI, the API, and Copilot import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { TFlowDefinition, TStep } from '@orchestra/core';
export type Issue = { severity: 'error'|'warning'; path: string; code: string; message: string }; @Injectable()
export class FlowValidator {
constructor(private reg: PieceRegistry, private db: Db) {}

async lint(orgId: string, def: TFlowDefinition): Promise<Issue[]> { const issues: Issue[] = [];
const seen = new Set<string>(['trigger']);

// 1. Trigger must resolve and be connected if (def.trigger.type === 'piece_trigger') {
this.checkOp(issues, 'trigger', def.trigger.piece.name, def.trigger.operation, 'trigger');
if (!def.trigger.connectionId)
issues.push({ severity: 'error', path: 'trigger.connectionId', code: 'CONNECTION_REqUIRED',
message: `Connect your ${def.trigger.piece.name} account to continue.` });
}

const walk = async (steps: TStep[], base: string) => { for (const [i, s] of steps.entries()) {
const path = `${base}[${i}]`; if (seen.has(s.id))
issues.push({ severity: 'error', path, code: 'DUPLICATE_STEP_ID', message: `Step id ${s.id} is used more than once.` });
seen.add(s.id);

if (s.type === 'piece_action' || s.type === 'piece_search') { this.checkOp(issues, path, s.piece.name, s.operation, 'action'); if (!s.connectionId)
issues.push({ severity: 'error', path: `${path}.connectionId`, code: 'CONNECTION_REqUIRED',
message: `Connect ${s.piece.name} to run this step.` }); const def2 = this.reg.getAction(s.piece.name, s.operation); for (const [k, p] of Object.entries(def2.props))
if (p.required && (s.props[k] === undefined || s.props[k] === '')) issues.push({ severity: 'error', path: `${path}.props.${k}`,
code: 'REqUIRED_FIELD_EMPTY',
message: `${p.displayName} is required.` });
}

// 2. Every {{token}} must reference a step that already executed for (const tok of this.tokens(s))


if (!seen.has(tok.split('.')[0]))issues.push({ severity: 'error', path, code: 'UNKNOWN_REFERENCE',message: `{{${tok}}} refers to a step that does not run before this one.` });if ('steps' in s && Array.isArray((s as any).steps)) await walk((s as any).steps, `${path}.steps`);if (s.type === 'branch') {await walk(s.onTrue, `${path}.onTrue`); await walk(s.onFalse, `${path}.onFalse`);}if (s.type === 'router')for (const [bi, b] of s.branches.entries())await walk(b.steps, `${path}.branches[${bi}].steps`);}};await walk(def.steps, 'steps');if (def.trigger.type === 'cron' && !def.steps.length)issues.push({ severity: 'warning', path: 'steps', code: 'EMPTY_FLOW', message: 'This flow has a trigger but no actions.' });return issues;}private checkOp(issues: Issue[], path: string, piece: string, op: string, kind: 'trigger'|'action') {try { kind === 'trigger' ? this.reg.getTrigger(piece, op) : this.reg.getAction(piece, op); } catch { issues.push({ severity: 'error', path, code: 'OPERATION_NOT_FOUND',message: `${piece} has no ${kind} named "${op}".` }); }}private tokens(s: any): string[] { const found: string[] = [];JSON.stringify(s).replace(/\{\{\s*([a-z0-9_]+(?:\.[^}\s]+)*)\s*\}\}/gi, (_m, g1) => { found.push(g1); return _m; });return found.filter(t => !t.startsWith('loop.'));}}if (!seen.has(tok.split('.')[0]))issues.push({ severity: 'error', path, code: 'UNKNOWN_REFERENCE',message: `{{${tok}}} refers to a step that does not run before this one.` });if ('steps' in s && Array.isArray((s as any).steps)) await walk((s as any).steps, `${path}.steps`);if (s.type === 'branch') {await walk(s.onTrue, `${path}.onTrue`); await walk(s.onFalse, `${path}.onFalse`);}if (s.type === 'router')for (const [bi, b] of s.branches.entries())await walk(b.steps, `${path}.branches[${bi}].steps`);}};await walk(def.steps, 'steps');if (def.trigger.type === 'cron' && !def.steps.length)issues.push({ severity: 'warning', path: 'steps', code: 'EMPTY_FLOW', message: 'This flow has a trigger but no actions.' });return issues;}private checkOp(issues: Issue[], path: string, piece: string, op: string, kind: 'trigger'|'action') {try { kind === 'trigger' ? this.reg.getTrigger(piece, op) : this.reg.getAction(piece, op); } catch { issues.push({ severity: 'error', path, code: 'OPERATION_NOT_FOUND',message: `${piece} has no ${kind} named "${op}".` }); }}private tokens(s: any): string[] { const found: string[] = [];JSON.stringify(s).replace(/\{\{\s*([a-z0-9_]+(?:\.[^}\s]+)*)\s*\}\}/gi, (_m, g1) => { found.push(g1); return _m; });return found.filter(t => !t.startsWith('loop.'));}}
if (!seen.has(tok.split('.')[0]))
issues.push({ severity: 'error', path, code: 'UNKNOWN_REFERENCE',
message: `{{${tok}}} refers to a step that does not run before this one.` });

if ('steps' in s && Array.isArray((s as any).steps)) await walk((s as any).steps, `${path}.steps`);
if (s.type === 'branch') {
await walk(s.onTrue, `${path}.onTrue`); await walk(s.onFalse, `${path}.onFalse`);
}
if (s.type === 'router')
for (const [bi, b] of s.branches.entries())
await walk(b.steps, `${path}.branches[${bi}].steps`);
}
};
await walk(def.steps, 'steps');

if (def.trigger.type === 'cron' && !def.steps.length)
issues.push({ severity: 'warning', path: 'steps', code: 'EMPTY_FLOW', message: 'This flow has a trigger but no actions.' });
return issues;
}

private checkOp(issues: Issue[], path: string, piece: string, op: string, kind: 'trigger'|'action') {
try { kind === 'trigger' ? this.reg.getTrigger(piece, op) : this.reg.getAction(piece, op); } catch { issues.push({ severity: 'error', path, code: 'OPERATION_NOT_FOUND',
message: `${piece} has no ${kind} named "${op}".` }); }
}

private tokens(s: any): string[] { const found: string[] = [];
JSON.stringify(s).replace(/\{\{\s*([a-z0-9_]+(?:\.[^}\s]+)*)\s*\}\}/gi, (_m, g1) => { found.push(g1); return _m; });
return found.filter(t => !t.startsWith('loop.'));
}
}
if (!seen.has(tok.split('.')[0]))
issues.push({ severity: 'error', path, code: 'UNKNOWN_REFERENCE',
message: `{{${tok}}} refers to a step that does not run before this one.` });

if ('steps' in s && Array.isArray((s as any).steps)) await walk((s as any).steps, `${path}.steps`);
if (s.type === 'branch') {
await walk(s.onTrue, `${path}.onTrue`); await walk(s.onFalse, `${path}.onFalse`);
}
if (s.type === 'router')
for (const [bi, b] of s.branches.entries())
await walk(b.steps, `${path}.branches[${bi}].steps`);
}
};
await walk(def.steps, 'steps');

if (def.trigger.type === 'cron' && !def.steps.length)
issues.push({ severity: 'warning', path: 'steps', code: 'EMPTY_FLOW', message: 'This flow has a trigger but no actions.' });
return issues;
}

private checkOp(issues: Issue[], path: string, piece: string, op: string, kind: 'trigger'|'action') {
try { kind === 'trigger' ? this.reg.getTrigger(piece, op) : this.reg.getAction(piece, op); } catch { issues.push({ severity: 'error', path, code: 'OPERATION_NOT_FOUND',
message: `${piece} has no ${kind} named "${op}".` }); }
}

private tokens(s: any): string[] { const found: string[] = [];
JSON.stringify(s).replace(/\{\{\s*([a-z0-9_]+(?:\.[^}\s]+)*)\s*\}\}/gi, (_m, g1) => { found.push(g1); return _m; });
return found.filter(t => !t.startsWith('loop.'));
}

// apps/api/src/triggers/trigger-activation.service.ts @Injectable()export class TriggerActivationService { constructor(private db: Db, private reg: PieceRegistry,private conns: ConnectionsService, private queues: queues) {}async activate(orgId: string, flow: any, def: TFlowDefinition) { const t = def.trigger;if (t.type === 'piece_trigger') {const trig = this.reg.getTrigger(t.piece.name, t.operation); const token = randomBytes(18).toString('base64url');const secret = randomBytes(24).toString('hex'); const reg = await this.db.triggers.upsert({flowId: flow.id, kind: trig.type === 'webhook' ? 'webhook' : 'poll', webhookToken: token, webhookSecret: secret, active: true, nextPollAt: trig.type === 'polling' ? new Date() : null,});if (trig.type === 'webhook' && trig.onEnable) {const { auth } = await this.conns.loadForExecution(orgId, t.connectionId!); await trig.onEnable({auth, propsValue: t.props,webhookUrl: `${process.env.PUBLIC_URL}/hooks/${token}`, store: this.db.triggerStore(reg.id),});}return;}if (t.type === 'cron')await this.db.triggers.upsert({ flowId: flow.id, kind: 'cron', cronExpr: t.props.expression, active: true,nextPollAt: nextCronTime(t.props.expression, t.props.timezone) }); if (t.type === 'webhook' || t.type === 'form' || t.type === 'chat')await this.db.triggers.upsert({ flowId: flow.id, kind: t.type, webhookToken: randomBytes(18).toString('base64url'), webhookSecret: randomBytes(24).toString('hex'), active: true });}/** Unsubscribing externally is what makes "unpublish" actually stop traffic. */// apps/api/src/triggers/trigger-activation.service.ts @Injectable()export class TriggerActivationService { constructor(private db: Db, private reg: PieceRegistry,private conns: ConnectionsService, private queues: queues) {}async activate(orgId: string, flow: any, def: TFlowDefinition) { const t = def.trigger;if (t.type === 'piece_trigger') {const trig = this.reg.getTrigger(t.piece.name, t.operation); const token = randomBytes(18).toString('base64url');const secret = randomBytes(24).toString('hex'); const reg = await this.db.triggers.upsert({flowId: flow.id, kind: trig.type === 'webhook' ?
