// apps/api/src/triggers/trigger-activation.service.ts @Injectable()
export class TriggerActivationService { constructor(private db: Db, private reg: PieceRegistry,
private conns: ConnectionsService, private queues: queues) {}

async activate(orgId: string, flow: any, def: TFlowDefinition) { const t = def.trigger;
if (t.type === 'piece_trigger') {
const trig = this.reg.getTrigger(t.piece.name, t.operation); const token = randomBytes(18).toString('base64url');
const secret = randomBytes(24).toString('hex'); const reg = await this.db.triggers.upsert({
flowId: flow.id, kind: trig.type === 'webhook' ? 'webhook' : 'poll', webhookToken: token, webhookSecret: secret, active: true, nextPollAt: trig.type === 'polling' ? new Date() : null,
});
if (trig.type === 'webhook' && trig.onEnable) {
const { auth } = await this.conns.loadForExecution(orgId, t.connectionId!); await trig.onEnable({
auth, propsValue: t.props,
webhookUrl: `${process.env.PUBLIC_URL}/hooks/${token}`, store: this.db.triggerStore(reg.id),
});
}
return;
}
if (t.type === 'cron')
await this.db.triggers.upsert({ flowId: flow.id, kind: 'cron', cronExpr: t.props.expression, active: true,
nextPollAt: nextCronTime(t.props.expression, t.props.timezone) }); if (t.type === 'webhook' || t.type === 'form' || t.type === 'chat')
await this.db.triggers.upsert({ flowId: flow.id, kind: t.type, webhookToken: randomBytes(18).toString('base64url'), webhookSecret: randomBytes(24).toString('hex'), active: true });
}

/** Unsubscribing externally is what makes "unpublish" actually stop traffic. */
// apps/api/src/triggers/trigger-activation.service.ts @Injectable()
export class TriggerActivationService { constructor(private db: Db, private reg: PieceRegistry,
private conns: ConnectionsService, private queues: queues) {}

async activate(orgId: string, flow: any, def: TFlowDefinition) { const t = def.trigger;
if (t.type === 'piece_trigger') {
const trig = this.reg.getTrigger(t.piece.name, t.operation); const token = randomBytes(18).toString('base64url');
const secret = randomBytes(24).toString('hex'); const reg = await this.db.triggers.upsert({
flowId: flow.id, kind: trig.type === 'webhook' ? 'webhook' : 'poll', webhookToken: token, webhookSecret: secret, active: true, nextPollAt: trig.type === 'polling' ? new Date() : null,
});
if (trig.type === 'webhook' && trig.onEnable) {
const { auth } = await this.conns.loadForExecution(orgId, t.connectionId!); await trig.onEnable({
auth, propsValue: t.props,
webhookUrl: `${process.env.PUBLIC_URL}/hooks/${token}`, store: this.db.triggerStore(reg.id),
});
}
return;
}
if (t.type === 'cron')
await this.db.triggers.upsert({ flowId: flow.id, kind: 'cron', cronExpr: t.props.expression, active: true,
nextPollAt: nextCronTime(t.props.expression, t.props.timezone) }); if (t.type === 'webhook' || t.type === 'form' || t.type === 'chat')
await this.db.triggers.upsert({ flowId: flow.id, kind: t.type, webhookToken: randomBytes(18).toString('base64url'), webhookSecret: randomBytes(24).toString('hex'), active: true });
}

/** Unsubscribing externally is what makes "unpublish" actually stop traffic. */


async deactivate(flow: any) {const regs = await this.db.triggers.byFlow(flow.id); for (const r of regs) {if (r.kind === 'webhook' && flow.publishedVersionId) {const def = (await this.db.flowVersions.byId(flow.publishedVersionId)).definition; if (def.trigger.type === 'piece_trigger') {const trig = this.reg.getTrigger(def.trigger.piece.name, def.trigger.operation); if (trig.onDisable && def.trigger.connectionId) {const { auth } = await this.conns.loadForExecution(flow.orgId,def.trigger.connectionId);await trig.onDisable({ auth, store: this.db.triggerStore(r.id) }).catch(() => {});}}}await this.db.triggers.update(r.id, { active: false });}}}async deactivate(flow: any) {const regs = await this.db.triggers.byFlow(flow.id); for (const r of regs) {if (r.kind === 'webhook' && flow.publishedVersionId) {const def = (await this.db.flowVersions.byId(flow.publishedVersionId)).definition; if (def.trigger.type === 'piece_trigger') {const trig = this.reg.getTrigger(def.trigger.piece.name, def.trigger.operation); if (trig.onDisable && def.trigger.connectionId) {const { auth } = await this.conns.loadForExecution(flow.orgId,def.trigger.connectionId);await trig.onDisable({ auth, store: this.db.triggerStore(r.id) }).catch(() => {});}}}await this.db.triggers.update(r.id, { active: false });}}}
async deactivate(flow: any) {
const regs = await this.db.triggers.byFlow(flow.id); for (const r of regs) {
if (r.kind === 'webhook' && flow.publishedVersionId) {
const def = (await this.db.flowVersions.byId(flow.publishedVersionId)).definition; if (def.trigger.type === 'piece_trigger') {
const trig = this.reg.getTrigger(def.trigger.piece.name, def.trigger.operation); if (trig.onDisable && def.trigger.connectionId) {
const { auth } = await this.conns.loadForExecution(flow.orgId,
def.trigger.connectionId);
await trig.onDisable({ auth, store: this.db.triggerStore(r.id) }).catch(() => {});
}
}
}
await this.db.triggers.update(r.id, { active: false });
}
}
}

// apps/api/src/triggers/webhook.controller.ts
import { Controller, Post, Param, Body, Headers, HttpCode } from '@nestjs/common'; import { createHmac, timingSafeEqual } from 'node:crypto';

@Controller('hooks')
export class WebhookController {
constructor(private db: Db, private runs: RunCreationService,
private reg: PieceRegistry, private conns: ConnectionsService) {}

/** Ack fast (<500 ms target), do the work on the queue. */ @Post(':token')
@HttpCode(200)
async ingest(@Param('token') token: string, @Body() body: any, @Headers() headers: Record<string, string>) {
const trig = await this.db.triggers.byToken(token);
if (!trig?.active) return { ok: true, ignored: 'inactive' };

const flow = await this.db.flows.byId(trig.flowId);
const def = (await this.db.flowVersions.byId(flow.publishedVersionId)).definition;

if (def.trigger.type === 'webhook' && def.trigger.props.authMode === 'hmac') { const sig = headers['x-signature'] ?? '';
const expect = createHmac('sha256', trig.webhookSecret)
.update(JSON.stringify(body)).digest('hex'); if (sig.length !== expect.length ||
!timingSafeEqual(Buffer.from(sig), Buffer.from(expect))) return { ok: false, error: 'BAD_SIGNATURE' };
}

let items: unknown[] = [body];
if (def.trigger.type === 'piece_trigger') {
const t = this.reg.getTrigger(def.trigger.piece.name, def.trigger.operation); if (t.onWebhook) {
const { auth } = await this.conns.loadForExecution(flow.orgId,
def.trigger.connectionId!); items = await t.onWebhook({ auth, payload: body, headers,
propsValue: def.trigger.props });
}
}
for (const item of items)
await this.runs.create({ flow, def, triggerKind: 'webhook', payload: item }); return { ok: true, runs: items.length };
}
}
// apps/api/src/triggers/webhook.controller.ts
import { Controller, Post, Param, Body, Headers, HttpCode } from '@nestjs/common'; import { createHmac, timingSafeEqual } from 'node:crypto';

@Controller('hooks')
export class WebhookController {
constructor(private db: Db, private runs: RunCreationService,
private reg: PieceRegistry, private conns: ConnectionsService) {}

/** Ack fast (<500 ms target), do the work on the queue. */ @Post(':token')
@HttpCode(200)
async ingest(@Param('token') token: string, @Body() body: any, @Headers() headers: Record<string, string>) {
const trig = await this.db.triggers.byToken(token);
if (!trig?.active) return { ok: true, ignored: 'inactive' };

const expect = createHmac('sha256', trig.webhookSecret)
.update(JSON.stringify(body)).digest('hex'); if (sig.length !== expect.length ||
!timingSafeEqual(Buffer.from(sig), Buffer.from(expect))) return { ok: false, error: 'BAD_SIGNATURE' };
}

let items: unknown[] = [body];
if (def.trigger.type === 'piece_trigger') {
const t = this.reg.getTrigger(def.trigger.piece.name, def.trigger.operation); if (t.onWebhook) {
const { auth } = await this.conns.loadForExecution(flow.orgId,
def.trigger.connectionId!); items = await t.onWebhook({ auth, payload: body, headers,
propsValue: def.trigger.props });
}
}
for (const item of items)
await this.runs.create({ flow, def, triggerKind: 'webhook', payload: item }); return { ok: true, runs: items.length };
}
}

// apps/api/src/runs/run-creation.service.ts @Injectable()export class RunCreationService {constructor(private db: Db, private queues: queues, private billing: BillingService) {}async create(a: { flow: any; def: TFlowDefinition; triggerKind: string; payload: any }) { const gate = await this.billing.checkAdmission(a.flow.orgId);if (!gate.allowed) {await this.db.runs.insert({ ...this.base(a), status: 'failed', error: { code: 'qUOTA_EXCEEDED', message: gate.reason } });return null;}const run = await this.db.runs.insert({...this.base(a), status: 'queued',context: { trigger: a.payload },// trigger output = context root});await this.db.runSteps.insert({ runId: run.id, stepId: 'trigger', stepType: a.def.trigger.type, status: 'succeeded',outputJson: mask(a.payload), durationMs: 0 });await this.queues.exec.add('run', { runId: run.id, orgId: a.flow.orgId },{ jobId: run.id,// dedupes accidental double-enqueue priority: priorityFor(a.flow.orgPlanTier),attempts: 1 });// engine owns retries, not BullMq return run;}private base(a: any) {return { orgId: a.flow.orgId, projectId: a.flow.projectId, flowId: a.flow.id, flowVersionId: a.flow.publishedVersionId, triggerKind: a.triggerKind };}}// apps/api/src/runs/run-creation.service.ts @Injectable()export class RunCreationService {constructor(private db: Db, private queues: queues, private billing: BillingService) {}async create(a: { flow: any; // apps/api/src/runs/run-creation.service.ts @Injectable()
export class RunCreationService {
constructor(private db: Db, private queues: queues, private billing: BillingService) {}

async create(a: { flow: any; def: TFlowDefinition; triggerKind: string; payload: any }) { const gate = await this.billing.checkAdmission(a.flow.orgId);
if (!gate.allowed) {
await this.db.runs.insert({ ...this.base(a), status: 'failed', error: { code: 'qUOTA_EXCEEDED', message: gate.reason } });
return null;
}
const run = await this.db.runs.insert({
...this.base(a), status: 'queued',
context: { trigger: a.payload },// trigger output = context root
});
await this.db.runSteps.insert({ runId: run.id, stepId: 'trigger', stepType: a.def.trigger.type, status: 'succeeded',
outputJson: mask(a.payload), durationMs: 0 });
await this.queues.exec.add('run', { runId: run.id, orgId: a.flow.orgId },
{ jobId: run.id,// dedupes accidental double-enqueue priority: priorityFor(a.flow.orgPlanTier),
attempts: 1 });// engine owns retries, not BullMq return run;
}

private base(a: any) {
return { orgId: a.flow.orgId, projectId: a.flow.projectId, flowId: a.flow.id, flowVersionId: a.flow.publishedVersionId, triggerKind: a.triggerKind };
}
}

return null;
}

...this.base(a), status: 'queued',
context: { trigger: a.payload },// trigger output = context root
});

outputJson: mask(a.payload), durationMs: 0 });
await this.queues.exec.add('run', { runId: run.id, orgId: a.flow.orgId },
{ jobId: run.id,// dedupes accidental double-enqueue priority: priorityFor(a.flow.orgPlanTier),
attempts: 1 });// engine owns retries, not BullMq return run;

export const connection = new IORedis(process.env.REDIS_URL!,
{ maxRetriesPerRequest: null });

export const queues = {
exec: new queue('exec', { connection }), delay: new queue('delay', { connection }), ai:  new queue('ai',  { connection }),
hooks: new queue('hooks', { connection }),
// step execution
// dehydrated Delay resumptions
// long AI/agent work
// outbound notifications
};

/** Per-tenant fairness: group rate limiting stops one org starving others. */ export function makeExecWorker(handler: (runId: string) => Promise<void>) {
return new Worker('exec', async job => handler(job.data.runId), {

Step 9 — Expression resolver

// packages/core/src/resolver.ts
const TOKEN = /\{\{\s*([^}]+?)\s*\}\}/g; export type Ctx = Record<string, any>;
function readPath(ctx: Ctx, path: string): unknown { return path.split('.').reduce<any>((acc, seg) => {
if (acc == null) return undefined;
const m = seg.match(/^(.+?)\[(\d+)\]$/);// items[0] if (m) return acc[m[1]]?.[Number(m[2])];
return acc[seg];
}, ctx);
}

/** Pipe filters keep 90% of Formatter needs out of a separate step. */ const FILTERS: Record<string, (v: any, arg?: string) => any> = {
upper: v => String(v).toUpperCase(), lower: v => String(v).toLowerCase(), trim: v => String(v).trim(),
default: (v, arg) => (v === undefined || v === null || v === '' ? arg : v), json: v => JSON.stringify(v),
number: v => Number(v),
date: (v, fmt) => formatDate(new Date(v as string), fmt ?? 'YYYY-MM-DD'), truncate: (v, n) => String(v).slice(0, Number(n ?? 100)),
first: v => (Array.isArray(v) ? v[0] : v),
join: (v, sep) => (Array.isArray(v) ? v.join(sep ?? ', ') : v),
};

function evalToken(ctx: Ctx, expr: string): unknown {
const [pathPart, ...pipes] = expr.split('|').map(s => s.trim()); let val = readPath(ctx, pathPart);
for (const p of pipes) {
const [fn, ...args] = p.split(':'); const f = FILTERS[fn];
if (!f) throw new Error(`UNKNOWN_FILTER:${fn}`); val = f(val, args.join(':'));
}
return val;
}

/** Whole-string token → preserve the native type. Mixed string → interpolate. */ export function resolveValue(value: unknown, ctx: Ctx): unknown {
if (typeof value === 'string') {
const only = value.match(/^\{\{\s*([^}]+?)\s*\}\}$/); if (only) return evalToken(ctx, only[1]);
return value.replace(TOKEN, (_m, e) => { const v = evalToken(ctx, e);
return v === undefined || v === null ? '' : typeof v === 'object'
? JSON.stringify(v) : String(v);
});
}
if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.map(v => resolveValue(v, ctx)); if (value && typeof value === 'object')
return Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(value)
.map(([k, v]) => [k, resolveValue(v, ctx)])); return value;
}

export function evaluateCondition(c: any, ctx: Ctx): boolean { switch (c.op) {
case 'and': return c.operands.every((o: any) => evaluateCondition(o, ctx)); case 'or': return c.operands.some((o: any) => evaluateCondition(o, ctx)); case 'not': return !evaluateCondition(c.operand, ctx);
}
const l = resolveValue(c.left, ctx); const r = resolveValue(c.right, ctx); switch (c.op) {
case 'eq': return looseEq(l, r); case 'neq': return !looseEq(l, r);
case 'gt': return Number(l) > Number(r);


case 'gte': return Number(l) >= Number(r); case 'lt': return Number(l) < Number(r); case 'lte': return Number(l) <= Number(r);case 'contains':return String(l).toLowerCase().includes(String(r).toLowerCase()); case 'not_contains': return !String(l).toLowerCase().includes(String(r).toLowerCase()); case 'starts_with':return String(l).startsWith(String(r));case 'ends_with':return String(l).endsWith(String(r));case 'exists':   return l !== undefined && l !== null && l !== ''; case 'not_exists':  return l === undefined || l === null || l === ''; case 'matches':   return new RegExp(String(r)).test(String(l));case 'in':return Array.isArray(r) && r.some(x => looseEq(x, l)); default: throw new Error(`UNKNOWN_OP:${c.op}`);}}const looseEq = (a: any, b: any) =>a === b || String(a).trim().toLowerCase() === String(b).trim().toLowerCase();case 'gte': return Number(l) >= Number(r); case 'lt': return Number(l) < Number(r); case 'lte': return Number(l) <= Number(r);case 'contains':return String(l).toLowerCase().includes(String(r).toLowerCase()); case 'not_contains': return !String(l).toLowerCase().includes(String(r).toLowerCase()); case 'starts_with':return String(l).startsWith(String(r));case 'ends_with':return String(l).endsWith(String(r));case 'exists':   return l !== undefined && l !== null && l !== ''; case 'not_exists':  return l === undefined || l === null || l === ''; case 'matches':   return new RegExp(String(r)).test(String(l));case 'in':return Array.isArray(r) && r.some(x => looseEq(x, l)); default: throw new Error(`UNKNOWN_OP:${c.op}`);}}const looseEq = (a: any, b: any) =>a === b || String(a).trim().toLowerCase() === String(b).trim().toLowerCase();

// packages/engine/src/executor.ts
import { TFlowDefinition, TStep, resolveValue, evaluateCondition } from '@orchestra/core';
export type StepOutcome =
| { kind: 'ok'; output: unknown }
| { kind: 'halt'; reason: string }
| { kind: 'pause'; resume: PauseToken }
| { kind: 'error'; error: EngineError };
// Filter stopped the run
// Delay / Approval
export interface HandlerCtx {
run: RunRow; def: TFlowDefinition; step: TStep;
props: Record<string, any>;// already resolved
ctx: Record<string, any>;// execution context (read-only) orgId: string; attempt: number; idempotencyKey: string;
services: Services;
}
export interface StepHandler { type: string; execute(c: HandlerCtx): Promise<StepOutcome> } export class Executor {
constructor(private db: Db, private handlers: Map<string, StepHandler>, private billing: BillingService, private tracer: Tracer) {}

async runOne(runId: string) {
const run = await this.db.runs.byId(runId);
if (['succeeded','failed','cancelled'].includes(run.status)) return;
const def = (await this.db.flowVersions.byId(run.flowVersionId)).definition as TFlowDefinition;

await this.db.runs.update(runId, { status: 'running' });
const span = this.tracer.startSpan('flow.run', { runId, flowId: run.flowId });

try {
const res = await this.walk(run, def, def.steps, run.context ?? {},
run.cursorStep ?? null);
if (res.kind === 'pause') {
await this.db.runs.update(runId, { status: 'paused', cursorStep: res.resume.stepId, context: res.ctx });
return;// worker slot released
}
const status = res.kind === 'halt' ? 'filtered'
: res.kind === 'error' ? 'failed' : 'succeeded'; await this.db.runs.update(runId, {
status, context: res.ctx, finishedAt: new Date(),
error: res.kind === 'error' ? serialise(res.error) : null,
});
await this.billing.meterRun(run.orgId, runId);
if (status === 'failed') await this.notifyFailure(run, res);
} catch (e) {
await this.db.runs.update(runId, { status: 'failed', error: serialise(e), finishedAt: new Date() });
throw e;
} finally { span.end(); }
}

import { TFlowDefinition, TStep, resolveValue, evaluateCondition } from '@orchestra/core';
export type StepOutcome =
| { kind: 'ok'; output: unknown }
| { kind: 'halt'; reason: string }
| { kind: 'pause'; resume: PauseToken }
| { kind: 'error'; error: EngineError };
// Filter stopped the run
// Delay / Approval
export interface HandlerCtx {
run: RunRow; def: TFlowDefinition; step: TStep;
props: Record<string, any>;// already resolved
ctx: Record<string, any>;// execution context (read-only) orgId: string; attempt: number; idempotencyKey: string;
services: Services;
}
export interface StepHandler { type: string; execute(c: HandlerCtx): Promise<StepOutcome> } export class Executor {
constructor(private db: Db, private handlers: Map<string, StepHandler>, private billing: BillingService, private tracer: Tracer) {}

async runOne(runId: string) {
const run = await this.db.runs.byId(runId);
if (['succeeded','failed','cancelled'].includes(run.status)) return;
const def = (await this.db.flowVersions.byId(run.flowVersionId)).definition as TFlowDefinition;

await this.db.runs.update(runId, { status: 'running' });
const span = this.tracer.startSpan('flow.run', { runId, flowId: run.flowId });

try {
const res = await this.walk(run, def, def.steps, run.context ?? {},
run.cursorStep ?? null);
if (res.kind === 'pause') {
await this.db.runs.update(runId, { status: 'paused', cursorStep: res.resume.stepId, context: res.ctx });
return;// worker slot released
}
const status = res.kind === 'halt' ? 'filtered'
: res.kind === 'error' ? 'failed' : 'succeeded'; await this.db.runs.update(runId, {
status, context: res.ctx, finishedAt: new Date(),
error: res.kind === 'error' ? serialise(res.error) : null,
});
await this.billing.meterRun(run.orgId, runId);
if (status === 'failed') await this.notifyFailure(run, res);
} catch (e) {
await this.db.runs.update(runId, { status: 'failed', error: serialise(e), finishedAt: new Date() });
throw e;
} finally { span.end(); }
}

/** Depth-first walk with resume support. Returns final ctx alongside the outcome. */ private async walk(run: RunRow, def: TFlowDefinition, steps: TStep[],
ctx: Record<string, any>, resumeAt: string | null): Promise<any & { ctx: Record<string, any> }> {
let skipping = !!resumeAt;



for (const step of steps) { if (skipping) {
if (containsStep(step, resumeAt!)) skipping = false; else continue;
}

// ---- container step types are walked, not "executed" -------------------if (step.type === 'filter') {
if (!evaluateCondition(step.condition, ctx))
return { kind: 'halt', reason: `filter:${step.id}`, ctx };
await this.record(run, step, 'succeeded', {}, { passed: true }, 0); continue;
}
if (step.type === 'branch') {
const take = evaluateCondition(step.condition, ctx);
await this.record(run, step, 'succeeded', {}, { branch: take }, 0); const r = await this.walk(run, def, take ? step.onTrue : step.onFalse,
ctx, skipping ? resumeAt : null); if (r.kind !== 'ok') return r;
ctx = r.ctx; continue;
}
if (step.type === 'router') {
const chosen = step.branches.find(b => b.condition && evaluateCondition(b.condition, ctx))
?? step.branches.find(b => b.default); await this.record(run, step, 'succeeded', {},
{ branch: chosen?.id ?? null }, 0); if (!chosen) continue;
const r = await this.walk(run, def, chosen.steps, ctx, null); if (r.kind !== 'ok') return r;
ctx = r.ctx; continue;
}
if (step.type === 'loop') {
const items = resolveValue(step.props.items, ctx); if (!Array.isArray(items))
return { kind: 'error', ctx,
error: new EngineError('validation', `LOOP_NOT_ARRAY:${step.id}`) }; const limit = pLimit(step.props.concurrency ?? 1);
const results: unknown[] = new Array(items.length);
await Promise.all(items.map((item, index) => limit(async () => {
const child = { ...ctx, loop: { item, index, total: items.length } }; const r = await this.walk(run, def, step.steps, child, null); results[index] = r.ctx;// deterministic by index
})));
ctx = { ...ctx, [step.id]: { items: results, count: items.length } }; await this.record(run, step, 'succeeded', { count: items.length },
{ count: items.length }, 0);
continue;
}

//leaf step: retry loop
const handler = this.handlers.get(step.type); if (!handler)
return { kind: 'error', ctx,
error: new EngineError('fatal', `NO_HANDLER:${step.type}`) };

const maxAttempts = step.retry?.maxAttempts ?? 3; let attempt = 0, last: StepOutcome | null = null;

while (attempt < maxAttempts) { attempt++;
const t0 = Date.now();
let props: Record<string, any>;
try { props = resolveValue((step as any).props ?? {}, ctx) as any; } catch (e: any) {
last = { kind: 'error',
error: new EngineError('validation', e.message) };
break;// template errors never retry
}

const outcome = await handler.execute({
run, def, step, props, ctx, orgId: run.orgId, attempt, idempotencyKey: `${run.id}:${step.id}:${attempt === 1 ? 1 : 'r'}`, services: this.services,
}).catch(e => ({ kind: 'error',
error: EngineError.from(e) } as StepOutcome));

const ms = Date.now() - t0; last = outcome;

if (outcome.kind === 'ok') {


ctx = { ...ctx, [step.id]: outcome.output };await this.record(run, step, 'succeeded', props, outcome.output, ms, attempt); await this.db.runs.update(run.id, { context: ctx });// checkpointbreak;}if (outcome.kind === 'pause' || outcome.kind === 'halt') break;// error pathconst cls = outcome.error.class;await this.record(run, step, 'failed', props, null, ms, attempt, outcome.error); if (cls !== 'transient' || attempt >= maxAttempts) break;await sleep(backoffMs(attempt, step.retry?.backoff ?? 'exponential'));}if (last!.kind === 'pause') return { ...last, ctx }; if (last!.kind === 'halt') return { ...last, ctx }; if (last!.kind === 'error') {const policy = step.onError ?? 'fail'; if (policy === 'continue') {ctx = { ...ctx, [step.id]: { error: serialise(last!.error) } }; continue;}if (policy.startsWith('goto:')) { const target = policy.slice(5);const r = await this.walk(run, def, def.steps, ctx, target); return r;}return { ...last, ctx };}}return { kind: 'ok', ctx };}private async record(run: RunRow, step: TStep, status: string, input: any, output: any, ms: number, attempt = 1, err?: EngineError) {const big = JSON.stringify(output ?? {}).length > 256_000; await this.db.runSteps.insert({runId: run.id, stepId: step.id, stepType: step.type, attempt, status, inputJson: mask(input), outputJson: big ? null : mask(output),blobRef: big ? await this.services.blob.put(run.id, step.id, output) : null, errorClass: err?.class, errorJson: err ? serialise(err) : null, durationMs: ms,});}}ctx = { ...ctx, [step.id]: outcome.output };await this.record(run, step, 'succeeded', props, outcome.output, ms, attempt); await this.db.runs.update(run.id, { context: ctx });// checkpointbreak;}if (outcome.kind === 'pause' || outcome.kind === 'halt') break;// error pathconst cls = outcome.error.class;await this.record(run, step, 'failed', props, null, ms, attempt, outcome.error); if (cls !== 'transient' || attempt >= maxAttempts) break;await sleep(backoffMs(attempt, step.retry?.backoff ?? 'exponential'));}if (last!.kind === 'pause') return { ...last, ctx }; if (last!.kind === 'halt') return { ...last, ctx }; if (last!.kind === 'error') {const policy = step.onError ?? 'fail'; if (policy === 'continue') {ctx = { ...ctx, [step.id]: { error: serialise(last!.error) } }; continue;}if (policy.startsWith('goto:')) { const target = policy.slice(5);const r = await this.walk(run, def, def.steps, ctx, target); return r;}return { ...last, ctx };}}return { kind: 'ok', ctx };}private async record(run: RunRow, step: TStep, status: string, input: any, output: any, ms: number, attempt = 1, err?: EngineError) {const big = JSON.stringify(output ?? {}).length > 256_000; await this.db.runSteps.insert({runId: run.id, stepId: step.id, stepType: step.type, attempt, status, inputJson: mask(input), outputJson: big ? null : mask(output),blobRef: big ? await this.services.blob.put(run.id, step.id, output) : null, errorClass: err?.class, errorJson: err ? serialise(err) : null, durationMs: ms,});}}
ctx = { ...ctx, [step.id]: outcome.output };
await this.record(run, step, 'succeeded', props, outcome.output, ms, attempt); await this.db.runs.update(run.id, { context: ctx });// checkpoint
break;
}
if (outcome.kind === 'pause' || outcome.kind === 'halt') break;

// error path
const cls = outcome.error.class;
await this.record(run, step, 'failed', props, null, ms, attempt, outcome.error); if (cls !== 'transient' || attempt >= maxAttempts) break;
await sleep(backoffMs(attempt, step.retry?.backoff ?? 'exponential'));
}

if (last!.kind === 'pause') return { ...last, ctx }; if (last!.kind === 'halt') return { ...last, ctx }; if (last!.kind === 'error') {
const policy = step.onError ?? 'fail'; if (policy === 'continue') {
ctx = { ...ctx, [step.id]: { error: serialise(last!.error) } }; continue;
}
if (policy.startsWith('goto:')) { const target = policy.slice(5);
const r = await this.walk(run, def, def.steps, ctx, target); return r;
}
return { ...last, ctx };
}
}
return { kind: 'ok', ctx };
}

private async record(run: RunRow, step: TStep, status: string, input: any, output: any, ms: number, attempt = 1, err?: EngineError) {
const big = JSON.stringify(output ?? {}).length > 256_000; await this.db.runSteps.insert({
runId: run.id, stepId: step.id, stepType: step.type, attempt, status, inputJson: mask(input), outputJson: big ? null : mask(output),
blobRef: big ? await this.services.blob.put(run.id, step.id, output) : null, errorClass: err?.class, errorJson: err ? serialise(err) : null, durationMs: ms,
});
}
}
ctx = { ...ctx, [step.id]: outcome.output };
await this.record(run, step, 'succeeded', props, outcome.output, ms, attempt); await this.db.runs.update(run.id, { context: ctx });// checkpoint
break;
}
if (outcome.kind === 'pause' || outcome.kind === 'halt') break;

// error path
const cls = outcome.error.class;
await this.record(run, step, 'failed', props, null, ms, attempt, outcome.error); if (cls !== 'transient' || attempt >= maxAttempts) break;
await sleep(backoffMs(attempt, step.retry?.backoff ?? 'exponential'));
}

if (last!.kind === 'pause') return { ...last, ctx }; if (last!.kind === 'halt') return { ...last, ctx }; if (last!.kind === 'error') {
const policy = step.onError ?? 'fail'; if (policy === 'continue') {
ctx = { ...ctx, [step.id]: { error: serialise(last!.error) } }; continue;
}
if (policy.startsWith('goto:')) { const target = policy.slice(5);
const r = await this.walk(run, def, def.steps, ctx, target); return r;
}
return { ...last, ctx };
}
}
return { kind: 'ok', ctx };
}

private async record(run: RunRow, step: TStep, status: string, input: any, output: any, ms: number, attempt = 1, err?: EngineError) {
const big = JSON.stringify(output ?? {}).length > 256_000; await this.db.runSteps.insert({
runId: run.id, stepId: step.id, stepType: step.type, attempt, status, inputJson: mask(input), outputJson: big ? null : mask(output),
blobRef: big ? await this.services.blob.put(run.id, step.id, output) : null, errorClass: err?.class, errorJson: err ? serialise(err) : null, durationMs: ms,
});
}

Error classification
// packages/engine/src/errors.tsexport type ErrorClass = 'transient' | 'auth' | 'validation' | 'budget' | 'fatal';export class EngineError extends Error {constructor(public class_: ErrorClass, message: string, public meta?: any) { super(message);}get class() { return this.class_; }static from(e: any): EngineError {if (e instanceof EngineError) return e;const status = e?.status ?? e?.response?.status;if (status === 429 || (status >= 500 && status < 600))return new EngineError('transient', `HTTP_${status}`, { status }); if (status === 401 || status === 403)return new EngineError('auth', `HTTP_${status}`, { status }); if (status === 400 || status === 422)return new EngineError('validation', `HTTP_${status}`,{ status, body: e?.response?.body });if (['ETIMEDOUT','ECONNRESET','EAI_AGAIN','ECONNREFUSED'].includes(e?.code))return new EngineError('transient', e.code);if (String(e?.message).includes('CONNECTION_NEEDS_RECONNECT')) return new EngineError('auth', 'CONNECTION_NEEDS_RECONNECT');return new EngineError('fatal', e?.message ?? // packages/engine/src/errors.ts
export type ErrorClass = 'transient' | 'auth' | 'validation' | 'budget' | 'fatal';

export class EngineError extends Error {
constructor(public class_: ErrorClass, message: string, public meta?: any) { super(message);
}
get class() { return this.class_; }

static from(e: any): EngineError {
if (e instanceof EngineError) return e;
const status = e?.status ?? e?.response?.status;
if (status === 429 || (status >= 500 && status < 600))
return new EngineError('transient', `HTTP_${status}`, { status }); if (status === 401 || status === 403)
return new EngineError('auth', `HTTP_${status}`, { status }); if (status === 400 || status === 422)
return new EngineError('validation', `HTTP_${status}`,
{ status, body: e?.response?.body });
if (['ETIMEDOUT','ECONNRESET','EAI_AGAIN','ECONNREFUSED'].includes(e?.code))
return new EngineError('transient', e.code);
if (String(e?.message).includes('CONNECTION_NEEDS_RECONNECT')) return new EngineError('auth', 'CONNECTION_NEEDS_RECONNECT');
return new EngineError('fatal', e?.message ?? 'UNKNOWN');
}
}

export const backoffMs = (attempt: number, mode: 'fixed'|'exponential') => mode === 'fixed' ? 2000
: Math.min(60_000, 1000 * 2 ** (attempt - 1)) + Math.random() * 500; // jitter
// packages/engine/src/errors.ts
export type ErrorClass = 'transient' | 'auth' | 'validation' | 'budget' | 'fatal';

export class EngineError extends Error {
constructor(public class_: ErrorClass, message: string, public meta?: any) { super(message);
}
get class() { return this.class_; }

static from(e: any): EngineError {
if (e instanceof EngineError) return e;
const status = e?.status ?? e?.response?.status;
if (status === 429 || (status >= 500 && status < 600))
return new EngineError('transient', `HTTP_${status}`, { status }); if (status === 401 || status === 403)
return new EngineError('auth', `HTTP_${status}`, { status }); if (status === 400 || status === 422)
return new EngineError('validation', `HTTP_${status}`,
{ status, body: e?.response?.body });
if (['ETIMEDOUT','ECONNRESET','EAI_AGAIN','ECONNREFUSED'].includes(e?.code))
return new EngineError('transient', e.code);
if (String(e?.message).includes('CONNECTION_NEEDS_RECONNECT')) return new EngineError('auth', 'CONNECTION_NEEDS_RECONNECT');
return new EngineError('fatal', e?.message ?? 'UNKNOWN');
}
}

export const backoffMs = (attempt: number, mode: 'fixed'|'exponential') => mode === 'fixed' ? 2000
: Math.min(60_000, 1000 * 2 ** (attempt - 1)) + Math.random() * 500; // jitter


Error class
Triggers
Engine behaviour
transient
429, 5xx, socket resets, DNS
Exponential backoff with jitter up to
maxAttempts
auth
401/403, refresh failure
No retry. Connection flagged error , owner notified, run failed
validation
400/422, template error, schema mismatch
No retry. Step highlighted with the offending field path
budget
Credit/quota/iteration ceiling hit
No retry. Run failed with an actionable billing message

// packages/engine/src/handlers/piece-action.handler.ts export class PieceActionHandler implements StepHandler {
type = 'piece_action';
constructor(private reg: PieceRegistry, private conns: ConnectionsService, private pool: SandboxPool) {}

async execute(c: HandlerCtx): Promise<StepOutcome> { const step = c.step as any;
const action = this.reg.getAction(step.piece.name, step.operation);

// Required-field guard runs before any network call for (const [k, p] of Object.entries(action.props))
if (p.required && (c.props[k] === undefined || c.props[k] === '')) return { kind: 'error',
error: new EngineError('validation', `REqUIRED_FIELD_EMPTY:${k}`) };

let auth: unknown = undefined; if (step.connectionId) {
const loaded = await this.conns.loadForExecution(c.orgId, step.connectionId); auth = loaded.auth;// plaintext exists only in this frame
}

// Piece code runs in a pooled child process: crash/leak isolation + timeout const output = await this.pool.run({
piece: step.piece.name, operation: step.operation,
auth, propsValue: c.props, idempotencyKey: c.idempotencyKey, timeoutMs: 120_000,
});

if (action.outputSchema) {
const parsed = action.outputSchema.safeParse(output); if (!parsed.success)
return { kind: 'error', error: new EngineError('validation', 'PIECE_OUTPUT_SCHEMA_MISMATCH', parsed.error.issues) };
}
return { kind: 'ok', output };
}
}
// packages/engine/src/handlers/piece-action.handler.ts export class PieceActionHandler implements StepHandler {
type = 'piece_action';
constructor(private reg: PieceRegistry, private conns: ConnectionsService, private pool: SandboxPool) {}

async execute(c: HandlerCtx): Promise<StepOutcome> { const step = c.step as any;
const action = this.reg.getAction(step.piece.name, step.operation);

// Required-field guard runs before any network call for (const [k, p] of Object.entries(action.props))
if (p.required && (c.props[k] === undefined || c.props[k] === '')) return { kind: 'error',
error: new EngineError('validation', `REqUIRED_FIELD_EMPTY:${k}`) };

let auth: unknown = undefined; if (step.connectionId) {
const loaded = await this.conns.loadForExecution(c.orgId, step.connectionId); auth = loaded.auth;// plaintext exists only in this frame
}

// Piece code runs in a pooled child process: crash/leak isolation + timeout const output = await this.pool.run({
piece: step.piece.name, operation: step.operation,
auth, propsValue: c.props, idempotencyKey: c.idempotencyKey, timeoutMs: 120_000,
});

if (action.outputSchema) {
const parsed = action.outputSchema.safeParse(output); if (!parsed.success)
return { kind: 'error', error: new EngineError('validation', 'PIECE_OUTPUT_SCHEMA_MISMATCH', parsed.error.issues) };

}
}

const context = await isolate.createContext();
await context.global.set('inputs', new ivm.ExternalCopy(inputs).copyInto()); const script = await isolate.compileScript(
`(async () => { ${source} })()`); const result = await script.run(context,
{ timeout: timeoutMs ?? 15_000, promise: true, copy: true }); return { kind: 'ok', output: result };

// packages/engine/src/handlers/delay.handler.ts export class DelayHandler implements StepHandler {
type = 'delay';
constructor(private queues: typeof queues) {}
async execute(c: HandlerCtx): Promise<StepOutcome> { const p = c.props as any;
const ms = p.mode === 'until'
? Math.max(0, new Date(p.untilIso).getTime() - Date.now())
: p.seconds * 1000;
// Long waits must not hold a worker: dehydrate and schedule rehydration. await this.queues.delay.add('resume', { runId: c.run.id, stepId: c.step.id },
{ delay: ms, jobId: `${c.run.id}:${c.step.id}` });
return { kind: 'pause', resume: { stepId: c.step.id, reason: 'delay' } };
}
}
// packages/engine/src/handlers/delay.handler.ts export class DelayHandler implements StepHandler {
type = 'delay';
constructor(private queues: typeof queues) {}
async execute(c: HandlerCtx): Promise<StepOutcome> { const p = c.props as any;
const ms = p.mode === 'until'
? Math.max(0, new Date(p.untilIso).getTime() - Date.now())
: p.seconds * 1000;
// Long waits must not hold a worker: dehydrate and schedule rehydration. await this.queues.delay.add('resume', { runId: c.run.id, stepId: c.step.id },
{ delay: ms, jobId: `${c.run.id}:${c.step.id}` });
return { kind: 'pause', resume: { stepId: c.step.id, reason: 'delay' } };
}
}
// packages/engine/src/handlers/approval.handler.ts export class ApprovalHandler implements StepHandler {
type = 'approval';
constructor(private db: Db, private queues: typeof queues, private notify: NotificationService) {}

async execute(c: HandlerCtx): Promise<StepOutcome> { const p = c.props as any;
const todo = await this.db.todos.insert({
orgId: c.orgId, runId: c.run.id, stepId: c.step.id,
title: p.title, assigneeId: p.assigneeId, assigneeRole: p.assigneeRole, payloadJson: { context: publicSubset(c.ctx) },
editableFields: p.editableFields ?? null,
expiresAt: new Date(Date.now() + p.timeoutHours * 3600_000),
});
await this.notify.todoAssigned(todo);
await this.queues.delay.add('todo_timeout',
{ todoId: todo.id, onTimeout: p.onTimeout },
{ delay: p.timeoutHours * 3600_000, jobId: `todo:${todo.id}` });
return { kind: 'pause', resume: { stepId: c.step.id, reason: 'approval' } };
}
}

// apps/api/src/todos/todos.service.ts — human action rehydrates the run @Injectable()
export class TodosService {
constructor(private db: Db, private queues: typeof queues) {} async resolve(orgId: string, todoId: string, userId: string,
decision: 'approved'|'rejected', edits?: any, comment?: string) { const todo = await this.db.todos.byId(orgId, todoId);
if (todo.status !== 'pending') throw new BadRequestException('ALREADY_RESOLVED'); await this.db.todos.update(todoId, { status: decision, resolvedAt: new Date(),
resolution: { by: userId, comment, edits } });

const run = await this.db.runs.byId(todo.runId); const ctx = { ...run.context,
[todo.stepId]: { decision, by: userId, comment, ...(edits ?? {}) } }; await this.db.runs.update(run.id, { context: ctx, status: 'queued' });

if (decision === 'rejected') {
await this.db.runs.update(run.id, { status: 'filtered', finishedAt: new Date() }); return;
}
await this.queues.exec.add('run', { runId: run.id, orgId },
{ jobId: `${run.id}:resume:${todo.stepId}` });
}
}

type = 'approval';
constructor(private db: Db, private queues: typeof queues, private notify: NotificationService) {}

const todo = await this.db.todos.insert({
orgId: c.orgId, runId: c.run.id, stepId: c.step.id,
title: p.title, assigneeId: p.assigneeId, assigneeRole: p.assigneeRole, payloadJson: { context: publicSubset(c.ctx) },
editableFields: p.editableFields ?? null,
expiresAt: new Date(Date.now() + p.timeoutHours * 3600_000),
});
await this.notify.todoAssigned(todo);
await this.queues.delay.add('todo_timeout',
{ todoId: todo.id, onTimeout: p.onTimeout },
{ delay: p.timeoutHours * 3600_000, jobId: `todo:${todo.id}` });
return { kind: 'pause', resume: { stepId: c.step.id, reason: 'approval' } };
}
}

export class TodosService {

decision: 'approved'|'rejected', edits?: any, comment?: string) { const todo = await this.db.todos.byId(orgId, todoId);
if (todo.status !== 'pending') throw new BadRequestException('ALREADY_RESOLVED'); await this.db.todos.update(todoId, { status: decision, resolvedAt: new Date(),
resolution: { by: userId, comment, edits } });

const run = await this.db.runs.byId(todo.runId); const ctx = { ...run.context,
[todo.stepId]: { decision, by: userId, comment, ...(edits ?? {}) } }; await this.db.runs.update(run.id, { context: ctx, status: 'queued' });

if (decision === 'rejected') {
await this.db.runs.update(run.id, { status: 'filtered', finishedAt: new Date() }); return;
}
await this.queues.exec.add('run', { runId: run.id, orgId },
{ jobId: `${run.id}:resume:${todo.stepId}` });
}

import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';

export class SandboxPool {
private idle: ChildProcess[] = [];
private inflight = new Map<string, { resolve: Function; reject: Function;
timer: NodeJS.Timeout; child: ChildProcess }>();

constructor(private size = Number(process.env.SANDBOX_POOL ?? 8)) { for (let i = 0; i < size; i++) this.idle.push(this.spawn());
}

private spawn(): ChildProcess {
const child = fork(require.resolve('./sandbox-child.js'), [], { env: { NODE_OPTIONS: '--max-old-space-size=512' },
execArgv: [], stdio: ['ignore','pipe','pipe','ipc'],
});
child.on('message', (m: any) => {
const slot = this.inflight.get(m.id); if (!slot) return; clearTimeout(slot.timer); this.inflight.delete(m.id); this.idle.push(slot.child);
m.ok ? slot.resolve(m.result) : slot.reject(Object.assign(new Error(m.error.message), m.error));
});
child.on('exit', () => { this.idle = this.idle.filter(c => c !== child);
this.idle.push(this.spawn()); });
return child;
}

run(req: { piece: string; operation: string; auth: unknown;
propsValue: any; idempotencyKey: string; timeoutMs: number }) { return new Promise<unknown>((resolve, reject) => {
const child = this.idle.pop() ?? this.spawn(); const id = randomUUID();
const timer = setTimeout(() => { this.inflight.delete(id);
child.kill('SIGKILL');// hard stop; pool self-heals on exit reject(new EngineError('transient', 'PIECE_TIMEOUT'));
}, req.timeoutMs);
this.inflight.set(id, { resolve, reject, timer, child }); child.send({ id, ...req });
});
}
}
import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';

export class SandboxPool {
private idle: ChildProcess[] = [];
private inflight = new Map<string, { resolve: Function; reject: Function;
timer: NodeJS.Timeout; child: ChildProcess }>();

constructor(private size = Number(process.env.SANDBOX_POOL ?? 8)) { for (let i = 0; i < size; i++) this.idle.push(this.spawn());
}

private spawn(): ChildProcess {
const child = fork(require.resolve('./sandbox-child.js'), [], { env: { NODE_OPTIONS: '--max-old-space-size=512' },
execArgv: [], stdio: ['ignore','pipe','pipe','ipc'],
});
child.on('message', (m: any) => {
const slot = this.inflight.get(m.id); if (!slot) return; clearTimeout(slot.timer); this.inflight.delete(m.id); this.idle.push(slot.child);
m.ok ? slot.resolve(m.result) : slot.reject(Object.assign(new Error(m.error.message), m.error));
});
child.on('exit', () => { this.idle = this.idle.filter(c => c !== child);
this.idle.push(this.spawn()); });
return child;
}

run(req: { piece: string; operation: string; auth: unknown;
propsValue: any; idempotencyKey: string; timeoutMs: number }) { return new Promise<unknown>((resolve, reject) => {
const child = this.idle.pop() ?? this.spawn(); const id = randomUUID();
const timer = setTimeout(() => { this.inflight.delete(id);
child.kill('SIGKILL');// hard stop; pool self-heals on exit reject(new EngineError('transient', 'PIECE_TIMEOUT'));
}, req.timeoutMs);
this.inflight.set(id, { resolve, reject, timer, child }); child.send({ id, ...req });
});
}
}

// packages/ai/src/model-gateway.ts import OpenAI from 'openai';
import Anthropic from '@anthropic-ai/sdk'; import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';

export type Purpose = 'copilot_plan' | 'copilot_map' | 'copilot_repair'
| 'flow_ai_step' | 'agent' | 'ops_diagnose' | 'embed';

export interface CallSpec { purpose: Purpose; system?: string;
messages: Array<{ role: 'user'|'assistant'|'tool'; content: any; tool_call_id?: string }>; tools?: Array<{ name: string; description: string; parameters: any }>;
jsonSchema?: { name: string; schema: any };// structured output contract temperature?: number;
maxTokens?: number;
model?: string;// 'auto' → routed attribution: { orgId: string; runId?: string; copilotSessionId?: string }; byoKey?: { provider: string; key: string };
cacheable?: boolean;
}

/** Purpose-based routing keeps model choice out of business logic. */
const ROUTES: Record<Purpose, { provider: 'openai'|'anthropic'; model: string;
temperature: number }> = {
copilot_plan:{ provider: 'anthropic', model: 'claude-sonnet-4-5', temperature: 0.1 }, copilot_map:{ provider: 'openai',model: 'gpt-4.1',temperature: 0 }, copilot_repair: { provider: 'anthropic', model: 'claude-sonnet-4-5', temperature: 0.1 }, flow_ai_step:{ provider: 'openai',model: 'gpt-4.1-mini',temperature: 0.2 }, agent:{ provider: 'anthropic', model: 'claude-sonnet-4-5', temperature: 0.3 },
// packages/ai/src/model-gateway.ts import OpenAI from 'openai';
import Anthropic from '@anthropic-ai/sdk'; import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';

export type Purpose = 'copilot_plan' | 'copilot_map' | 'copilot_repair'
| 'flow_ai_step' | 'agent' | 'ops_diagnose' | 'embed';

export interface CallSpec { purpose: Purpose; system?: string;
messages: Array<{ role: 'user'|'assistant'|'tool'; content: any; tool_call_id?: string }>; tools?: Array<{ name: string; description: string; parameters: any }>;
jsonSchema?: { name: string; schema: any };// structured output contract temperature?: number;
maxTokens?: number;
model?: string;// 'auto' → routed attribution: { orgId: string; runId?: string; copilotSessionId?: string }; byoKey?: { provider: string; key: string };
cacheable?: boolean;
}

/** Purpose-based routing keeps model choice out of business logic. */
const ROUTES: Record<Purpose, { provider: 'openai'|'anthropic'; model: string;
temperature: number }> = {
copilot_plan:{ provider: 'anthropic', model: 'claude-sonnet-4-5', temperature: 0.1 }, copilot_map:{ provider: 'openai',model: 'gpt-4.1',temperature: 0 }, copilot_repair: { provider: 'anthropic', model: 'claude-sonnet-4-5', temperature: 0.1 }, flow_ai_step:{ provider: 'openai',model: 'gpt-4.1-mini',temperature: 0.2 }, agent:{ provider: 'anthropic', model: 'claude-sonnet-4-5', temperature: 0.3 },

ops_diagnose:{ provider: 'openai',model: 'gpt-4.1',temperature: 0 }, embed:{ provider: 'openai',model: 'text-embedding-3-small', temperature: 0 },
};

const PRICE: Record<string, { in: number; out: number }> = {// USD per 1M tokens 'gpt-4.1':{ in: 2.00, out: 8.00 },
'gpt-4.1-mini':{ in: 0.40, out: 1.60 },
'claude-sonnet-4-5': { in: 3.00, out: 15.00 },
};

export class ModelGateway {
private oa = new OpenAI({ apiKey: process.env.OPENAI_API_KEY }); private an = new Anthropic({ apiKey: process.env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY });

constructor(private db: Db, private cache: Cache) {}

async call<T = any>(spec: CallSpec): Promise<{ data: T; raw: any; usage: Usage }> { const route = ROUTES[spec.purpose];
const model = spec.model && spec.model !== 'auto' ? spec.model : route.model; const key = spec.cacheable
? 'ai:' + createHash('sha256').update(JSON.stringify({ model, spec: {
...spec, attribution: undefined } })).digest('hex')
: null;
if (key) { const hit = await this.cache.get<any>(key); if (hit) return hit; }

const t0 = Date.now();
const out = await withRetry(() =>
route.provider === 'openai' ? this.openai(model, spec) : this.anthropic(model, spec),
{ attempts: 3, isRetryable: e => [429, 500, 502, 503, 529]
.includes(e?.status ?? 0) }); const latency = Date.now() - t0;

const price = PRICE[model] ?? { in: 0, out: 0 }; const usage: Usage = {
model, provider: route.provider,
tokensIn: out.usage.in, tokensOut: out.usage.out, costUsd: spec.byoKey ? 0
: (out.usage.in * price.in + out.usage.out * price.out) / 1_000_000, latencyMs: latency, byoKey: !!spec.byoKey,
};
await this.db.aiUsage.insert({ ...spec.attribution, ...usage });

const result = { data: out.data as T, raw: out.raw, usage }; if (key) await this.cache.set(key, result, 3600);
return result;
}

/** Structured output with one repair attempt, then hard failure. */
async callJson<T>(spec: CallSpec & { jsonSchema: NonNullable<CallSpec['jsonSchema']> }, validate: (v: unknown) => T): Promise<{ data: T; usage: Usage }> {
const first = await this.call<any>(spec);
try { return { data: validate(first.data), usage: first.usage }; } catch (e: any) {
const retry = await this.call<any>({ ...spec, temperature: 0, messages: [
...spec.messages,
{ role: 'assistant', content: JSON.stringify(first.data) },
{ role: 'user', content:
`That output failed validation: ${e.message}\n` +
`Return ONLY corrected JSON matching the schema. No prose.` },
]});
return { data: validate(retry.data), usage: retry.usage };// throws if still bad
}
}

async embedBatch(texts: string[]): Promise<number[][]> { const res = await this.oa.embeddings.create({
model: ROUTES.embed.model, input: texts }); return res.data.map(d => d.embedding);
}

private async openai(model: string, spec: CallSpec) { const client = spec.byoKey?.provider === 'openai'
? new OpenAI({ apiKey: spec.byoKey.key }) : this.oa; const r = await client.chat.completions.create({
model,
temperature: spec.temperature ?? ROUTES[spec.purpose].temperature, max_tokens: spec.maxTokens ?? 4096,
messages: [...(spec.system ? [{ role: 'system' as const,
content: spec.system }] : []),
...(spec.messages as any)],
...(spec.jsonSchema ? { response_format: { type: 'json_schema',


json_schema: { name: spec.jsonSchema.name, strict: true, schema: spec.jsonSchema.schema } } } : {}),...(spec.tools ? { tools: spec.tools.map(t => ({ type: 'function' as const, function: { name: t.name, description: t.description,parameters: t.parameters } })) } : {}),});const msg = r.choices[0].message; return {data: spec.jsonSchema ? JSON.parse(msg.content ?? '{}') : msg, raw: r,usage: { in: r.usage?.prompt_tokens ?? 0, out: r.usage?.completion_tokens ?? 0 },};}private async anthropic(model: string, spec: CallSpec) { /* symmetric implementation */ }}json_schema: { name: spec.jsonSchema.name, strict: true, schema: spec.jsonSchema.schema } } } : {}),...(spec.tools ? { tools: spec.tools.map(t => ({ type: 'function' as const, function: { name: t.name, description: t.description,parameters: t.parameters } })) } : {}),});const msg = r.choices[0].message; return {data: spec.jsonSchema ? JSON.parse(msg.content ?? '{}') : msg, raw: r,usage: { in: r.usage?.prompt_tokens ?? 0, out: r.usage?.completion_tokens ?? 0 },};}private async anthropic(model: string, spec: CallSpec) { /* symmetric implementation */ }}
json_schema: { name: spec.jsonSchema.name, strict: true, schema: spec.jsonSchema.schema } } } : {}),
...(spec.tools ? { tools: spec.tools.map(t => ({ type: 'function' as const, function: { name: t.name, description: t.description,
parameters: t.parameters } })) } : {}),
});
const msg = r.choices[0].message; return {
data: spec.jsonSchema ? JSON.parse(msg.content ?? '{}') : msg, raw: r,
usage: { in: r.usage?.prompt_tokens ?? 0, out: r.usage?.completion_tokens ?? 0 },
};
}

private async anthropic(model: string, spec: CallSpec) { /* symmetric implementation */ }
}
json_schema: { name: spec.jsonSchema.name, strict: true, schema: spec.jsonSchema.schema } } } : {}),
...(spec.tools ? { tools: spec.tools.map(t => ({ type: 'function' as const, function: { name: t.name, description: t.description,
parameters: t.parameters } })) } : {}),
});
const msg = r.choices[0].message; return {
data: spec.jsonSchema ? JSON.parse(msg.content ?? '{}') : msg, raw: r,
usage: { in: r.usage?.prompt_tokens ?? 0, out: r.usage?.completion_tokens ?? 0 },
};
}

private async anthropic(model: string, spec: CallSpec) { /* symmetric implementation */ }

Step 14 — AI step handler (run-time AI, made deterministic)

// packages/engine/src/handlers/ai.handler.ts import { z } from 'zod';
import { jsonSchemaToZod } from '@orchestra/core';

export class AiStepHandler implements StepHandler { type = 'ai';
constructor(private gw: ModelGateway, private billing: BillingService, private keys: ByoKeyService) {}

async execute(c: HandlerCtx): Promise<StepOutcome> { const step = c.step as any;
const budget = await this.billing.reserveAiCredits(c.orgId, step.model); if (!budget.ok)
return { kind: 'error', error: new EngineError('budget', budget.reason) };
const byoKey = await this.keys.forOrg(c.orgId);
// flat-rate credit if present
const base = { purpose: 'flow_ai_step' as const, model: step.model, attribution: { orgId: c.orgId, runId: c.run.id }, byoKey };

switch (step.operation) { case 'generate':
case 'summarize': {
const r = await this.gw.call<any>({ ...base, system: step.operation === 'summarize'
? 'Summarise faithfully. Never invent facts not present in the input.'
: (c.props.system ?? 'You are a precise assistant.'),
messages: [{ role: 'user', content: String(c.props.prompt ?? c.props.input) }], maxTokens: c.props.maxTokens ?? 1024,
});
return { kind: 'ok', output: { text: r.data.content ?? r.data,
usage: r.usage } };
}

case 'classify': {
const labels: string[] = c.props.labels;
const schema = { type: 'object', additionalProperties: false, properties: { label: { type: 'string', enum: labels },
confidence: { type: 'number' }, rationale: { type: 'string' } },
required: ['label','confidence','rationale'] };
const Out = z.object({ label: z.enum(labels as [string, ...string[]]),
confidence: z.number().min(0).max(1), rationale: z.string() });
const { data } = await this.gw.callJson({ ...base, jsonSchema: { name: 'classification', schema },
system: `Classify the input into exactly one label: ${labels.join(', ')}. ` +
`If genuinely uncertain, choose the closest label and report low confidence.`, messages: [{ role: 'user', content: String(c.props.input) }],
}, v => Out.parse(v));

// Abstention: below threshold the step reports needsReview instead of guessing const min = c.props.minConfidence ?? 0;
if (data.confidence < min)
return { kind: 'ok', output: { ...data, label: null, needsReview: true } }; return { kind: 'ok', output: data };
}

case 'extract': {
const Out = jsonSchemaToZod(c.props.schema);
// packages/engine/src/handlers/ai.handler.ts import { z } from 'zod';
import { jsonSchemaToZod } from '@orchestra/core';

export class AiStepHandler implements StepHandler { type = 'ai';
constructor(private gw: ModelGateway, private billing: BillingService, private keys: ByoKeyService) {}

async execute(c: HandlerCtx): Promise<StepOutcome> { const step = c.step as any;
const budget = await this.billing.reserveAiCredits(c.orgId, step.model); if (!budget.ok)
return { kind: 'error', error: new EngineError('budget', budget.reason) };
const byoKey = await this.keys.forOrg(c.orgId);
// flat-rate credit if present
const base = { purpose: 'flow_ai_step' as const, model: step.model, attribution: { orgId: c.orgId, runId: c.run.id }, byoKey };

switch (step.operation) { case 'generate':
case 'summarize': {
const r = await this.gw.call<any>({ ...base, system: step.operation === 'summarize'
? 'Summarise faithfully. Never invent facts not present in the input.'
: (c.props.system ?? 'You are a precise assistant.'),
messages: [{ role: 'user', content: String(c.props.prompt ?? c.props.input) }], maxTokens: c.props.maxTokens ?? 1024,
});
return { kind: 'ok', output: { text: r.data.content ?? r.data,
usage: r.usage } };
}

case 'classify': {
const labels: string[] = c.props.labels;
const schema = { type: 'object', additionalProperties: false, properties: { label: { type: 'string', enum: labels },
confidence: { type: 'number' }, rationale: { type: 'string' } },
required: ['label','confidence','rationale'] };
const Out = z.object({ label: z.enum(labels as [string, ...string[]]),
confidence: z.number().min(0).max(1), rationale: z.string() });
const { data } = await this.gw.callJson({ ...base, jsonSchema: { name: 'classification', schema },
system: `Classify the input into exactly one label: ${labels.join(', ')}. ` +
`If genuinely uncertain, choose the closest label and report low confidence.`, messages: [{ role: 'user', content: String(c.props.input) }],
}, v => Out.parse(v));

// Abstention: below threshold the step reports needsReview instead of guessing const min = c.props.minConfidence ?? 0;
if (data.confidence < min)
return { kind: 'ok', output: { ...data, label: null, needsReview: true } }; return { kind: 'ok', output: data };
}

case 'extract': {
const Out = jsonSchemaToZod(c.props.schema);


const { data } = await this.gw.callJson({ ...base, jsonSchema: { name: 'extraction', schema: c.props.schema },system: 'Extract only values explicitly present in the input. ' +'Use null for anything absent. Never infer or invent values.', messages: [{ role: 'user', content: typeof c.props.input === 'string'? c.props.input : JSON.stringify(c.props.input) }],}, v => Out.parse(v));return { kind: 'ok', output: data };}case 'embed': {const [v] = await this.gw.embedBatch([String(c.props.input)]);return { kind: 'ok', output: { embedding: v, dimensions: v.length } };}default:return { kind: 'error',error: new EngineError('validation', `AI_OP_UNSUPPORTED:${step.operation}`) };}}}const { data } = await this.gw.callJson({ ...base, jsonSchema: { name: 'extraction', schema: c.props.schema },system: 'Extract only values explicitly present in the input. ' +'Use null for anything absent. const { data } = await this.gw.callJson({ ...base, jsonSchema: { name: 'extraction', schema: c.props.schema },
system: 'Extract only values explicitly present in the input. ' +
'Use null for anything absent. Never infer or invent values.', messages: [{ role: 'user', content: typeof c.props.input === 'string'
? c.props.input : JSON.stringify(c.props.input) }],
}, v => Out.parse(v));
return { kind: 'ok', output: data };
}

case 'embed': {
const [v] = await this.gw.embedBatch([String(c.props.input)]);
return { kind: 'ok', output: { embedding: v, dimensions: v.length } };
}
default:
return { kind: 'error',
error: new EngineError('validation', `AI_OP_UNSUPPORTED:${step.operation}`) };
}
}
}
const { data } = await this.gw.callJson({ ...base, jsonSchema: { name: 'extraction', schema: c.props.schema },
system: 'Extract only values explicitly present in the input. ' +
'Use null for anything absent. Never infer or invent values.', messages: [{ role: 'user', content: typeof c.props.input === 'string'
? c.props.input : JSON.stringify(c.props.input) }],
}, v => Out.parse(v));
return { kind: 'ok', output: data };
}

const [v] = await this.gw.embedBatch([String(c.props.input)]);
return { kind: 'ok', output: { embedding: v, dimensions: v.length } };
}
default:
return { kind: 'error',
error: new EngineError('validation', `AI_OP_UNSUPPORTED:${step.operation}`) };
}
}
}
Step 15 — Tool registry + agent runtime
One registry serves the agent handler and the MCP server. An agent therefore can never call something MCP would refuse.
// packages/ai/src/tool-registry.ts export interface ToolBinding {name: string;description: string; parameters: any;// 'slack  send_message'// JSON Schema derived from PropDefspiece: string; operation: string; connectionId: string | null; sideEffect: 'read'|'create'|'update'|'delete';}@Injectable()export class ToolRegistry {constructor(private reg: PieceRegistry, private conns: ConnectionsService, private pool: SandboxPool, private rbac: RbacService) {}/** Build tool bindings from a step's declared toolset (or an MCP session's grants). */ async bind(orgId: string, userId: string,requested: Array<{ piece: string; operation: string;connectionId: string|null }>): Promise<ToolBinding[]> { const out: ToolBinding[] = [];for (const r of requested) {await this.rbac.assertCanUsePiece(orgId, userId, r.piece);// governance policy const a = this.reg.getAction(r.piece, r.operation);out.push({name: `${r.piece}  ${r.operation}`,description: `${this.reg.resolve(r.piece).displayName}: ${a.description}`, parameters: propsToJsonSchema(a.props),piece: r.piece, operation: r.operation,connectionId: r.connectionId, sideEffect: a.sideEffect,});}return out;}async invoke(orgId: string, b: ToolBinding, args: any, idempotencyKey: string) { let auth: unknown;if (b.connectionId)auth = (await this.conns.loadForExecution(orgId, b.connectionId)).auth; return this.pool.run({ piece: b.piece, operation: b.operation, auth,propsValue: args, idempotencyKey, timeoutMs: 60_000 });}}export function propsToJsonSchema(props: Record<string, any>) { return {type: 'object', additionalProperties: false,properties: Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(props).map(([k, p]) => [k, {type: p.kind === 'number' ? // packages/ai/src/tool-registry.ts export interface ToolBinding {
name: string;
description: string; parameters: any;
// 'slack  send_message'
// JSON Schema derived from PropDefs
piece: string; operation: string; connectionId: string | null; sideEffect: 'read'|'create'|'update'|'delete';
}

@Injectable()
export class ToolRegistry {
constructor(private reg: PieceRegistry, private conns: ConnectionsService, private pool: SandboxPool, private rbac: RbacService) {}

/** Build tool bindings from a step's declared toolset (or an MCP session's grants). */ async bind(orgId: string, userId: string,
requested: Array<{ piece: string; operation: string;
connectionId: string|null }>): Promise<ToolBinding[]> { const out: ToolBinding[] = [];
for (const r of requested) {
await this.rbac.assertCanUsePiece(orgId, userId, r.piece);// governance policy const a = this.reg.getAction(r.piece, r.operation);
out.push({
name: `${r.piece}  ${r.operation}`,
description: `${this.reg.resolve(r.piece).displayName}: ${a.description}`, parameters: propsToJsonSchema(a.props),
piece: r.piece, operation: r.operation,
connectionId: r.connectionId, sideEffect: a.sideEffect,
});
}
return out;
}

async invoke(orgId: string, b: ToolBinding, args: any, idempotencyKey: string) { let auth: unknown;
if (b.connectionId)
auth = (await this.conns.loadForExecution(orgId, b.connectionId)).auth; return this.pool.run({ piece: b.piece, operation: b.operation, auth,
propsValue: args, idempotencyKey, timeoutMs: 60_000 });
}
}

export function propsToJsonSchema(props: Record<string, any>) { return {
type: 'object', additionalProperties: false,
properties: Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(props).map(([k, p]) => [k, {
type: p.kind === 'number' ? 'number' : p.kind === 'checkbox' ? 'boolean' : 'string', description: [p.displayName, p.description, p.aiHint].filter(Boolean).join(' — '),
}])),
required: Object.entries(props).filter(([, p]: any) => p.required).map(([k]) => k),

// packages/engine/src/handlers/agent.handler.ts export class AgentHandler implements StepHandler {
type = 'agent';
constructor(private gw: ModelGateway, private tools: ToolRegistry, private db: Db, private billing: BillingService) {}

async execute(c: HandlerCtx): Promise<StepOutcome> { const p = c.props as any;
const bindings = await this.tools.bind(c.orgId, c.run.createdBy, p.tools); const messages: any[] = [{ role: 'user', content: p.input }];
let spent = 0;

for (let iter = 1; iter <= (p.maxIterations ?? 8); iter++) { if (spent >= p.maxCreditBudget)
return { kind: 'error',
error: new EngineError('budget', 'AGENT_BUDGET_EXCEEDED') };

const r = await this.gw.call<any>({ purpose: 'agent',
system: [ p.instructions, '',
'Rules:',
'- Use the provided tools to gather facts. Never fabricate tool results.',
'- One tool call at a time; read the result before deciding the next step.',
'- Before any create/update/delete tool call, state in one sentence why it is needed.', '- When the objective is met, reply with a final answer and no tool call.',
].join('\n'), messages,
tools: bindings.map(b => ({ name: b.name, description: b.description,
parameters: b.parameters })), attribution: { orgId: c.orgId, runId: c.run.id },
});
spent += creditsFor(r.usage);

const msg = r.data;
const call = msg.tool_calls?.[0];

// Persist the reasoning as a queryable artifact (Make-style transparency) await this.db.agentTranscripts.insert({
runId: c.run.id, stepId: c.step.id, iteration: iter,
thought: typeof msg.content === 'string' ? msg.content : null, toolName: call?.function?.name ?? null,
toolArgs: call ? safeJson(call.function.arguments) : null, tokensIn: r.usage.tokensIn, tokensOut: r.usage.tokensOut, costUsd: r.usage.costUsd,
});

if (!call)
return { kind: 'ok', output: { answer: msg.content, iterations: iter,
creditsUsed: spent } };

const binding = bindings.find(b => b.name === call.function.name); if (!binding) {
messages.push({ role: 'tool', tool_call_id: call.id, content: `ERROR: unknown tool ${call.function.name}` });
continue;
}

let result: unknown; try {
result = await this.tools.invoke(c.orgId, binding, safeJson(call.function.arguments),
`${c.run.id}:${c.step.id}:${iter}`);
} catch (e: any) {
result = { error: EngineError.from(e).message };// let the model recover
}
await this.db.agentTranscripts.updateLastResult(c.run.id, c.step.id, iter, result); messages.push({ role: 'assistant', content: null, tool_calls: [call] }); messages.push({ role: 'tool', tool_call_id: call.id,
content: JSON.stringify(result).slice(0, 20_000) });
}
return { kind: 'error',
error: new EngineError('budget', 'AGENT_MAX_ITERATIONS_REACHED') };
}
}

Step 16 — MCP bridge
// apps/mcp/src/server.tsimport { Server } from '@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/server/index.js';import { StdioServerTransport } from '@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/server/stdio.js';export async function startMcpServer(deps: {tools: ToolRegistry; flows: FlowsService; db: Db; session: McpSession;}) {const server = new Server({ name: 'orchestra', version: '1.0.0' },{ capabilities: { tools: {} } });server.setRequestHandler('tools/list', async () => {// 1) every connected piece action the session is allowed to use const grants = await deps.db.connections.listUsable(deps.session.orgId, deps.session.projectId);const pieceTools = await deps.tools.bind(deps.session.orgId, deps.session.userId, grants.flatMap(g => actionsOf(g.pieceName).map(op => ({piece: g.pieceName, operation: op, connectionId: g.id }))));// 2) every published flow whose trigger is agent_call, exposed as a tool//(this is the Copilot Studio "flow as agent tool" pattern)const flows = await deps.db.flows.publishedWithAgentTrigger(deps.session.projectId);return { tools: [...pieceTools.map(t => ({ name: t.name, description: t.description,inputSchema: t.parameters })),...flows.map(f => ({ name: `flow ${slug(f.name)}`, description: f.definition.trigger.props.description, inputSchema: f.definition.trigger.props.inputSchema })),]};});server.setRequestHandler('tools/call', async (req) => { const { name, arguments: args } = req.params;if (name.startsWith('flow  ')) {const run = await deps.flows.runManually(deps.session.orgId,await deps.db.flows.bySlug(deps.session.projectId, name.slice(6)), args); const done = await waitForRun(deps.db, run.id, 120_000);return { content: [{ type: 'text', text: JSON.stringify(done.context) }] };}const [piece, operation] = name.split('  ');const conn = await deps.db.connections.pick(deps.session.orgId, piece); // apps/mcp/src/server.ts
import { Server } from '@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/server/index.js';
import { StdioServerTransport } from '@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/server/stdio.js';

export async function startMcpServer(deps: {
tools: ToolRegistry; flows: FlowsService; db: Db; session: McpSession;
}) {
const server = new Server({ name: 'orchestra', version: '1.0.0' },
{ capabilities: { tools: {} } });

server.setRequestHandler('tools/list', async () => {
// 1) every connected piece action the session is allowed to use const grants = await deps.db.connections.listUsable(
deps.session.orgId, deps.session.projectId);
const pieceTools = await deps.tools.bind(deps.session.orgId, deps.session.userId, grants.flatMap(g => actionsOf(g.pieceName).map(op => ({
piece: g.pieceName, operation: op, connectionId: g.id }))));

// 2) every published flow whose trigger is agent_call, exposed as a tool
//(this is the Copilot Studio "flow as agent tool" pattern)
const flows = await deps.db.flows.publishedWithAgentTrigger(deps.session.projectId);

return { tools: [
...pieceTools.map(t => ({ name: t.name, description: t.description,
inputSchema: t.parameters })),
...flows.map(f => ({ name: `flow ${slug(f.name)}`, description: f.definition.trigger.props.description, inputSchema: f.definition.trigger.props.inputSchema })),
]};
});

server.setRequestHandler('tools/call', async (req) => { const { name, arguments: args } = req.params;
if (name.startsWith('flow  ')) {
const run = await deps.flows.runManually(deps.session.orgId,
await deps.db.flows.bySlug(deps.session.projectId, name.slice(6)), args); const done = await waitForRun(deps.db, run.id, 120_000);
return { content: [{ type: 'text', text: JSON.stringify(done.context) }] };
}
const [piece, operation] = name.split('  ');
const conn = await deps.db.connections.pick(deps.session.orgId, piece); const out = await deps.tools.invoke(deps.session.orgId,
{ name, piece, operation, connectionId: conn?.id ?? null, description: '', parameters: {}, sideEffect: 'update' },
args, `mcp:${req.id}`);
return { content: [{ type: 'text', text: JSON.stringify(out) }] };
});

await server.connect(new StdioServerTransport());
}
// apps/mcp/src/server.ts
import { Server } from '@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/server/index.js';
import { StdioServerTransport } from '@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/server/stdio.js';

export async function startMcpServer(deps: {
tools: ToolRegistry; flows: FlowsService; db: Db; session: McpSession;
}) {
const server = new Server({ name: 'orchestra', version: '1.0.0' },
{ capabilities: { tools: {} } });

server.setRequestHandler('tools/list', async () => {
// 1) every connected piece action the session is allowed to use const grants = await deps.db.connections.listUsable(
deps.session.orgId, deps.session.projectId);
const pieceTools = await deps.tools.bind(deps.session.orgId, deps.session.userId, grants.flatMap(g => actionsOf(g.pieceName).map(op => ({
piece: g.pieceName, operation: op, connectionId: g.id }))));

// 2) every published flow whose trigger is agent_call, exposed as a tool
//(this is the Copilot Studio "flow as agent tool" pattern)
const flows = await deps.db.flows.publishedWithAgentTrigger(deps.session.projectId);

return { tools: [
...pieceTools.map(t => ({ name: t.name, description: t.description,
inputSchema: t.parameters })),
...flows.map(f => ({ name: `flow ${slug(f.name)}`, description: f.definition.trigger.props.description, inputSchema: f.definition.trigger.props.inputSchema })),
]};
});

server.setRequestHandler('tools/call', async (req) => { const { name, arguments: args } = req.params;
if (name.startsWith('flow  ')) {
const run = await deps.flows.runManually(deps.session.orgId,
await deps.db.flows.bySlug(deps.session.projectId, name.slice(6)), args); const done = await waitForRun(deps.db, run.id, 120_000);
return { content: [{ type: 'text', text: JSON.stringify(done.context) }] };
}
const [piece, operation] = name.split('  ');
const conn = await deps.db.connections.pick(deps.session.orgId, piece); const out = await deps.tools.invoke(deps.session.orgId,
{ name, piece, operation, connectionId: conn?.id ?? null, description: '', parameters: {}, sideEffect: 'update' },
args, `mcp:${req.id}`);
return { content: [{ type: 'text', text: JSON.stringify(out) }] };
});

await server.connect(new StdioServerTransport());
}
Because MCP tool calls reuse the caller's existing encrypted Connections, an external LLM client (an IDE agent, a desktop assistant, a custom app) gains action capability with no additional credential exchange.


Part 6 — The Copilot Engine: Idea In, Working Workfiow Out

This is the centrepiece. The user types one sentence — "when a lead fills my Typeform, score it with AI, put it in HubSpot, and ping sales if the budget is over 50k" — and Copilot produces a complete, validated, editable Draft flow, pre-wired with existing connections and field mappings, streaming its reasoning as it works. The human reviews, connects anything missing, tests, and publishes.

The pipeline

user prompt (+ optional image/file, + optional existing draft)
│
▼
┌───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ STAGE 1 INTENT PARSE→ structured Intent object│
│ extract: trigger phrase, action phrases, conditions, loops,│
│human-approval needs, named channels/lists/sheets│
├───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ STAGE 2 CANDIDATE RETRIEVAL→ hybrid search over piece_embeddings│
│ for each phrase, fetch top-12 OperationCards (never the full catalog)│
├───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ STAGE 3 OPERATION SELECTION→ LLM picks exact piece + operation │
│ constrained decoding: enum of retrieved candidate ids only│
├───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ STAGE 4 CONNECTION RESOLUTION→ pick existing conn, else needsHuman│
│ NEVER creates credentials (hard boundary)│
├───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ STAGE 5 SCHEMA HYDRATION→ real output schemas for mapping│
│ sampleOutput from piece, or live "test trigger" if connected│
├───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ STAGE 6 FIELD MAPPING→ per-step props with {{tokens}}│
│ semantic field match + type check + per-field confidence│
├───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ STAGE 7 GRAPH ASSEMBLY→ FlowDefinition (zod-parsed)│
│ logic nodes inserted for conditions / loops / approvals│
├───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ STAGE 8 VALIDATE + REPAIR LOOP → FlowValidator; ≤2 LLM repair passes│
│ low-confidence fields blanked and flagged, never guessed│
├───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ STAGE 9 PERSIST AS DRAFT→ real flow record + JSON-Patch audit│
│ status stays 'draft'; publish remains a human action│
└───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
│
▼ SSE stream: stage events + reasoning + patches → builder side-panel human: review → connect missing apps → Test step → Publish

Two autonomy modes, following Zapier's documented pattern (Zapier Help Center):
auto_buildauto_build— apply every patch immediately, summarise afterwards.
auto_build
auto_build
ask_as_you_buildask_as_you_build— emit each patch as a proposal; apply only on user confirmation.
ask_as_you_build
ask_as_you_build

Stage 1 — Intent parsing

export const Intent = z.object({ summary: z.string(),

// packages/copilot/src/intent.ts import { z } from 'zod';

export const Intent = z.object({ summary: z.string(),
trigger: z.object({ phrase: z.string(),
appHint: z.string().nullable(),
kind: z.enum(['app_event','schedule','webhook','form','chat','manual']), scheduleExpr: z.string().nullable(),
namedResource: z.string().nullable(),// "#sales", "Leads sheet", "Support form"
}),
steps: z.array(z.object({ order: z.number().int(), phrase: z.string(),
appHint: z.string().nullable(),

intentKind: z.enum(['app_action','ai','agent','http','code','table']), namedResource: z.string().nullable(),
aiTask: z.enum(['generate','summarize','classify','extract','none']).default('none'),
})),
logic: z.array(z.object({
kind: z.enum(['filter','branch','router','loop','delay','approval']), afterStepOrder: z.number().int(),
naturalCondition: z.string().nullable(), approvalOf: z.string().nullable(),
})),
ambiguities: z.array(z.string()),// surfaced to the user, never guessed
});
export type TIntent = z.infer<typeof Intent>;

export const INTENT_SYSTEM = `You convert a plain-language automation request into a structured plan. You do NOT choose apps or field names — a later stage does that.

Rules:
Exactly one trigger. If the request implies a time ("every morning") use kind=schedule and emit a cron expression. If it implies an inbound event from an app, use app_event.
One step per distinct operation, in execution order. Split compound sentences: "add to CRM and notify Slack" is TWO steps.
Set intentKind=ai only when the request needs generation, summarising, classification or extraction of unstructured content. Never use ai for simple field copying.
Set intentKind=agent only when the request explicitly needs autonomous
multi-step decision making ("figure out", "research and decide", "handle it").
Emit logic nodes for any condition ("only if", "when over", "unless"), iteration ("for each", "every item"), waiting ("after 2 days"), or human sign-off
("get approval", "have my manager check").
Capture named resources verbatim: channel names, sheet names, list names, labels.
Anything genuinely unclear goes in ambiguities. Do NOT invent a value to fill a gap.

Return only JSON matching the schema.`;

export async function parseIntent(gw: ModelGateway, prompt: string,
attribution: any, attachments?: string[]) { const { data } = await gw.callJson({
purpose: 'copilot_plan', system: INTENT_SYSTEM,
jsonSchema: { name: 'intent', schema: zodToJsonSchema(Intent) }, messages: [{ role: 'user', content: attachments?.length
? [{ type: 'text', text: prompt },
...attachments.map(url => ({ type: 'image_url', image_url: { url } }))]
: prompt }], attribution,
}, v => Intent.parse(v)); return data;
}



Stages 2–3 — Retrieval then constrained operation selection
The LLM never sees the whole catalog. Retrieval narrows to ~12 candidates per phrase; the selector then chooses from an enum, so it structurally cannot hallucinate an app or operation name.

export async function resolveConnections(deps: { conns: ConnectionsService; reg: PieceRegistry;
}, orgId: string, projectId: string, userEmail: string,

const piece = deps.reg.resolve(ch.card.piece); if (piece.auth.type === 'none') {

out.push({ ...ch, connectionId: null, needsHumanAuth: false, authType: 'none' }); continue;
}
if (!cache.has(ch.card.piece))
cache.set(ch.card.piece, await deps.conns.pickForCopilot( orgId, projectId, userEmail, ch.card.piece));

...ch,
connectionId: pick.connectionId, connectionLabel: pick.label,
// Copilot may SELECT an existing connection; it may never CREATE one. needsHumanAuth: pick.needsHuman,
authType: piece.auth.type,
});
}
return out;
}
out.push({ ...ch, connectionId: null, needsHumanAuth: false, authType: 'none' }); continue;
}
if (!cache.has(ch.card.piece))
cache.set(ch.card.piece, await deps.conns.pickForCopilot( orgId, projectId, userEmail, ch.card.piece));

...ch,
connectionId: pick.connectionId, connectionLabel: pick.label,
// Copilot may SELECT an existing connection; it may never CREATE one. needsHumanAuth: pick.needsHuman,
authType: piece.auth.type,

Stages 5–6 — Schema hydration and semantic field mapping
Mapping quality is the single biggest determinant of whether a generated flow works on the first run. Two inputs make it accurate: the rєal upstream output schema, and the dєclarєd prop schema with aiHint .

export const FieldMap = z.object({ stepId: z.string(),

value: z.union([z.string(), z.number(), z.boolean(), z.null()]), confidence: z.number().min(0).max(1),
rationale: z.string(),
})),
});

args: {
intent: TIntent;
plans: ResolvedStepPlan[];
/** stepId → JSON shape of that step's output (sample or live-tested) */ upstreamSchemas: Record<string, unknown>;
attribution: any;
}) {

.map(([k, v]) => `- ${k} (${v.type}${v.required ? ', REqUIRED' : ''})` +

`${pp.example !== undefined ? ` e.g. ${JSON.stringify(pp.example)}` : ''}`)
.join('\n')).join('\n');
