Rules:
Reference upstream data with {{step_id.path}} tokens exactly as listed in AVAILABLE DATA. Never invent a token or a path that is not listed.
A step may only reference steps that run BEFORE it.
Type must match: a number prop needs a numeric field or literal; a boolean prop needs a boolean. Never map an object into a text prop — pick a leaf field.
Prefer semantic equivalence over name similarity: an "email" prop takes an email-shaped field even if the source calls it "contact_address".
Static text may be mixed with tokens: "New lead: {{trigger.name}} ({{trigger.budget}})".

/** Turn a sample payload into dotted paths with types — what the LLM can reference. */ export function flattenPaths(obj: unknown, prefix = ''):

return [{ path: prefix.replace(/^\./, ''), type: typeof obj, example: obj }];

/** Turn a sample payload into dotted paths with types — what the LLM can reference. */ export function flattenPaths(obj: unknown, prefix = ''):
Array<{ path: string; type: string; example?: unknown }> { if (obj === null || obj === undefined) return [];
if (Array.isArray(obj)) return obj.length
? flattenPaths(obj[0], `${prefix}[0]`)
.concat([{ path: prefix.replace(/^\./, ''), type: 'array' }])
: [{ path: prefix.replace(/^\./, ''), type: 'array' }]; if (typeof obj === 'object')
return Object.entries(obj as any).flatMap(([k, v]) => flattenPaths(v, prefix ? `${prefix}.${k}` : k));
return [{ path: prefix.replace(/^\./, ''), type: typeof obj, example: obj }];
}

Stage 7 — Graph assembly

// packages/copilot/src/assemble.ts
import { FlowDefinition, TFlowDefinition } from '@orchestra/core';

export function assembleGraph(args: { intent: TIntent;
plans: ResolvedStepPlan[];
props: Record<string, Record<string, unknown>>;
conditions: Record<number, any>;// afterStepOrder → compiled Condition
}): TFlowDefinition {

const triggerPlan = args.plans.find(p => p.phraseOrder === -1)!; const trigger = buildTrigger(args.intent, triggerPlan);

// Build leaf steps in order const leaves = args.plans
.filter(p => p.phraseOrder >= 0)
.sort((a, b) => a.phraseOrder - b.phraseOrder)
.map(p => {
const id = `step_${p.phraseOrder}`;
const intentStep = args.intent.steps.find(s => s.order === p.phraseOrder)!; if (intentStep.intentKind === 'ai')
return { id, name: slug(intentStep.phrase), type: 'ai' as const, model: 'auto', operation: intentStep.aiTask === 'none'
? 'generate' : intentStep.aiTask, props: args.props[id] ?? {} };
if (intentStep.intentKind === 'agent')
return { id, name: slug(intentStep.phrase), type: 'agent' as const, props: { instructions: intentStep.phrase, tools: [],
input: '{{trigger}}', maxIterations: 8, maxCreditBudget: 50 } };
return {
id, name: slug(intentStep.phrase), type: 'piece_action' as const, piece: { name: p.card.piece, version: '*' },
operation: p.card.operation, connectionId: p.connectionId, props: args.props[id] ?? {},
retry: { maxAttempts: 3, backoff: 'exponential' as const }, onError: 'fail',
};
});

// Wrap/insert logic nodes, walking the intent's logic list in reverse so that
// inserting a wrapper never shifts the indices of not-yet-processed nodes. let steps: any[] = leaves;
for (const l of [...args.intent.logic].sort((a, b) => b.afterStepOrder - a.afterStepOrder)) { const idx = steps.findIndex(s => s.id === `step_${l.afterStepOrder}`);
const insertAt = idx === -1 ? steps.length : idx + 1;

if (l.kind === 'filter')
steps.splice(insertAt, 0, { id: `filter_${l.afterStepOrder}`, type: 'filter',
condition: args.conditions[l.afterStepOrder] ?? { op: 'exists', left: '{{trigger}}' } });

if (l.kind === 'branch' || l.kind === 'router') {
const tail = steps.slice(insertAt);// everything after moves inside steps = steps.slice(0, insertAt);
steps.push({ id: `router_${l.afterStepOrder}`, type: 'router', branches: [
{ id: 'b_match', label: l.naturalCondition ?? 'Condition met', condition: args.conditions[l.afterStepOrder], steps: tail },
{ id: 'b_default', label: 'Otherwise', default: true, steps: [] },
]});
}

if (l.kind === 'approval')
steps.splice(insertAt, 0, { id: `approval_${l.afterStepOrder}`, type: 'approval', props: { title: l.approvalOf ?? 'Approve to continue',
assigneeRole: 'admin', timeoutHours: 24, onTimeout: 'reject' } });

if (l.kind === 'delay')
steps.splice(insertAt, 0, { id: `delay_${l.afterStepOrder}`, type: 'delay', props: { mode: 'duration', seconds: 86400 } });


if (l.kind === 'loop') {const tail = steps.slice(insertAt); steps = steps.slice(0, insertAt);steps.push({ id: `loop_${l.afterStepOrder}`, type: 'loop',props: { items: `{{step_${l.afterStepOrder}.items}}`, concurrency: 1 }, steps: tail });}}return FlowDefinition.parse({ schemaVersion: 1, trigger, steps, settings: {} });}/** Natural language → typed Condition, compiled by a tiny focused LLM call. */ export async function compileCondition(gw: ModelGateway, natural: string,availablePaths: string[], attribution: any) {const schema = { type: 'object', additionalProperties: false, properties: { op: { type: 'string', enum: ['eq','neq','gt','gte','lt','lte','contains','not_contains','starts_with','ends_with','exists','not_exists','matches','in', 'and','or'] },left: { type: ['string','number','boolean','null'] },right: { type: ['string','number','boolean','array','null'] }, operands: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object' } },}, required: ['op'] };const { data } = await gw.callJson({ purpose: 'copilot_map',system: `Compile the condition into the operator schema. Use only these data paths:${availablePaths.map(p => `{{${p}}}`).join(', ')}Numbers must be numeric literals, not strings ("over 50k" → right: 50000). Combine multiple clauses with op "and"/"or" plus an operands array.`,jsonSchema: { name: 'condition', schema }, messages: [{ role: 'user', content: natural }], attribution,}, v => v as any); return data;}if (l.kind === 'loop') {const tail = steps.slice(insertAt); steps = steps.slice(0, insertAt);steps.push({ id: `loop_${l.afterStepOrder}`, type: 'loop',props: { items: `{{step_${l.afterStepOrder}.items}}`, concurrency: 1 }, steps: tail });}}return FlowDefinition.parse({ schemaVersion: 1, trigger, steps, settings: {} });}/** Natural language → typed Condition, compiled by a tiny focused LLM call. */ export async function compileCondition(gw: ModelGateway, natural: string,availablePaths: string[], attribution: any) {const schema = { type: 'object', additionalProperties: false, properties: { op: { type: 'string', enum: ['eq','neq','gt','gte','lt','lte','contains','not_contains','starts_with','ends_with','exists','not_exists','matches','in', 'and','or'] },left: { type: ['string','number','boolean','null'] },right: { type: ['string','number','boolean','array','null'] }, operands: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object' } },}, required: ['op'] };const { data } = await gw.callJson({ purpose: 'copilot_map',system: `Compile the condition into the operator schema. Use only these data paths:${availablePaths.map(p => `{{${p}}}`).join(', ')}Numbers must be numeric literals, not strings ("over 50k" → right: 50000). Combine multiple clauses with op "and"/"or" plus an operands array.`,jsonSchema: { name: 'condition', schema }, messages: [{ role: 'user', content: natural }], attribution,}, v => v as any); return data;}

if (l.kind === 'loop') {
const tail = steps.slice(insertAt); steps = steps.slice(0, insertAt);
steps.push({ id: `loop_${l.afterStepOrder}`, type: 'loop',
props: { items: `{{step_${l.afterStepOrder}.items}}`, concurrency: 1 }, steps: tail });
}
}

return FlowDefinition.parse({ schemaVersion: 1, trigger, steps, settings: {} });
}

/** Natural language → typed Condition, compiled by a tiny focused LLM call. */ export async function compileCondition(gw: ModelGateway, natural: string,
availablePaths: string[], attribution: any) {
const schema = { type: 'object', additionalProperties: false, properties: { op: { type: 'string', enum: ['eq','neq','gt','gte','lt','lte','contains',
'not_contains','starts_with','ends_with','exists','not_exists','matches','in', 'and','or'] },
left: { type: ['string','number','boolean','null'] },
right: { type: ['string','number','boolean','array','null'] }, operands: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object' } },
}, required: ['op'] };
const { data } = await gw.callJson({ purpose: 'copilot_map',
system: `Compile the condition into the operator schema. Use only these data paths:
${availablePaths.map(p => `{{${p}}}`).join(', ')}
Numbers must be numeric literals, not strings ("over 50k" → right: 50000). Combine multiple clauses with op "and"/"or" plus an operands array.`,
jsonSchema: { name: 'condition', schema }, messages: [{ role: 'user', content: natural }], attribution,
}, v => v as any); return data;
}

if (l.kind === 'loop') {
const tail = steps.slice(insertAt); steps = steps.slice(0, insertAt);
steps.push({ id: `loop_${l.afterStepOrder}`, type: 'loop',
props: { items: `{{step_${l.afterStepOrder}.items}}`, concurrency: 1 }, steps: tail });
}
}

return FlowDefinition.parse({ schemaVersion: 1, trigger, steps, settings: {} });
}

/** Natural language → typed Condition, compiled by a tiny focused LLM call. */ export async function compileCondition(gw: ModelGateway, natural: string,
availablePaths: string[], attribution: any) {
const schema = { type: 'object', additionalProperties: false, properties: { op: { type: 'string', enum: ['eq','neq','gt','gte','lt','lte','contains',
'not_contains','starts_with','ends_with','exists','not_exists','matches','in', 'and','or'] },
left: { type: ['string','number','boolean','null'] },
right: { type: ['string','number','boolean','array','null'] }, operands: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object' } },
}, required: ['op'] };
const { data } = await gw.callJson({ purpose: 'copilot_map',
system: `Compile the condition into the operator schema. Use only these data paths:
${availablePaths.map(p => `{{${p}}}`).join(', ')}
Numbers must be numeric literals, not strings ("over 50k" → right: 50000). Combine multiple clauses with op "and"/"or" plus an operands array.`,
jsonSchema: { name: 'condition', schema }, messages: [{ role: 'user', content: natural }], attribution,
}, v => v as any); return data;
}

Stages 8–9 — Validate, repair, persist: the orchestrator
// packages/copilot/src/copilot.service.ts import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';import { applyPatch, compare } from 'fast-json-patch';import { FlowDefinition, TFlowDefinition } from '@orchestra/core';export type CopilotEvent =| { type: 'stage'; stage: string; label: string }| { type: 'reasoning'; text: string }| { type: 'proposal'; patch: any[]; summary: string; confidence: number }| { type: 'applied'; patch: any[]; summary: string }| { type: 'todo'; kind: 'connect'|'fill_field'|'confirm'; message: string; target: { stepId: string; prop?: string; piece?: string } }| { type: 'done'; flowId: string; publishable: boolean; issues: Issue[] }| { type: 'error'; message: string };@Injectable()export class CopilotService { constructor(private gw: ModelGateway, private index: CatalogIndex, private reg: PieceRegistry, private conns: ConnectionsService, private pool: SandboxPool,private validator: FlowValidator, private flows: FlowsService, private db: Db,) {}/*** Idea → Draft flow. // packages/copilot/src/copilot.service.ts import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { applyPatch, compare } from 'fast-json-patch';
import { FlowDefinition, TFlowDefinition } from '@orchestra/core';

export type CopilotEvent =
| { type: 'stage'; stage: string; label: string }
| { type: 'reasoning'; text: string }
| { type: 'proposal'; patch: any[]; summary: string; confidence: number }
| { type: 'applied'; patch: any[]; summary: string }
| { type: 'todo'; kind: 'connect'|'fill_field'|'confirm'; message: string; target: { stepId: string; prop?: string; piece?: string } }
| { type: 'done'; flowId: string; publishable: boolean; issues: Issue[] }
| { type: 'error'; message: string };

@Injectable()
export class CopilotService { constructor(
private gw: ModelGateway, private index: CatalogIndex, private reg: PieceRegistry, private conns: ConnectionsService, private pool: SandboxPool,
private validator: FlowValidator, private flows: FlowsService, private db: Db,
) {}

/**
* Idea → Draft flow. Yields events for SSE streaming into the builder side-panel.
* Never publishes. Never creates a credential.
*/
async *generate(args: {
orgId: string; projectId: string; userId: string; userEmail: string; prompt: string; attachments?: string[];
flowId?: string;// continue editing an existing draft mode: 'auto_build' | 'ask_as_you_build';
}): AsyncGenerator<CopilotEvent> {

const session = await this.db.copilotSessions.create({
orgId: args.orgId, projectId: args.projectId, userId: args.userId, flowId: args.flowId ?? null, mode: args.mode });
const attribution = { orgId: args.orgId, copilotSessionId: session.id };
// packages/copilot/src/copilot.service.ts import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { applyPatch, compare } from 'fast-json-patch';
import { FlowDefinition, TFlowDefinition } from '@orchestra/core';

export type CopilotEvent =
| { type: 'stage'; stage: string; label: string }
| { type: 'reasoning'; text: string }
| { type: 'proposal'; patch: any[]; summary: string; confidence: number }
| { type: 'applied'; patch: any[]; summary: string }
| { type: 'todo'; kind: 'connect'|'fill_field'|'confirm'; message: string; target: { stepId: string; prop?: string; piece?: string } }
| { type: 'done'; flowId: string; publishable: boolean; issues: Issue[] }
| { type: 'error'; message: string };

@Injectable()
export class CopilotService { constructor(
private gw: ModelGateway, private index: CatalogIndex, private reg: PieceRegistry, private conns: ConnectionsService, private pool: SandboxPool,
private validator: FlowValidator, private flows: FlowsService, private db: Db,
) {}

/**
* Idea → Draft flow. Yields events for SSE streaming into the builder side-panel.
* Never publishes. Never creates a credential.
*/
async *generate(args: {
orgId: string; projectId: string; userId: string; userEmail: string; prompt: string; attachments?: string[];
flowId?: string;// continue editing an existing draft mode: 'auto_build' | 'ask_as_you_build';
}): AsyncGenerator<CopilotEvent> {

const session = await this.db.copilotSessions.create({
orgId: args.orgId, projectId: args.projectId, userId: args.userId, flowId: args.flowId ?? null, mode: args.mode });
const attribution = { orgId: args.orgId, copilotSessionId: session.id };

try {
//STAGE 1
yield { type: 'stage', stage: 'intent', label: 'Understanding your request' };
const intent = await parseIntent(this.gw, args.prompt, attribution, args.attachments); yield { type: 'reasoning', text:
`Plan: ${intent.summary}\nTrigger: ${intent.trigger.phrase}\n` + intent.steps.map(s => `${s.order}. ${s.phrase}`).join('\n') };
for (const a of intent.ambiguities)
yield { type: 'todo', kind: 'confirm', message: a, target: { stepId: 'trigger' } };

//STAGES 2–3
yield { type: 'stage', stage: 'apps', label: 'Choosing apps and events' }; const choices = await resolveOperations({ gw: this.gw, index: this.index },
intent, attribution);
for (const c of choices)
yield { type: 'reasoning', text:
`${c.phraseOrder === -1 ? 'Trigger' : `Step ${c.phraseOrder}`}: ` +
`${c.card.pieceDisplay} → ${c.card.display} ` +
`(confidence ${c.confidence.toFixed(2)}) — ${c.reason}` };

//STAGE 4
yield { type: 'stage', stage: 'connections', label: 'Matching your connected accounts' }; const plans = await resolveConnections({ conns: this.conns, reg: this.reg },
args.orgId, args.projectId, args.userEmail, choices); for (const p of plans) {
if (p.needsHumanAuth)
yield { type: 'todo', kind: 'connect',
message: `Connect your ${p.card.pieceDisplay} account ` +
`(${p.authType === 'oauth2' ? 'sign-in popup'
: 'API key'}) — I can't do this for you.`, target: { stepId: p.phraseOrder === -1 ? 'trigger'
: `step_${p.phraseOrder}`,
piece: p.card.piece } }; else if (p.connectionId)
yield { type: 'reasoning',
text: `Using existing ${p.card.pieceDisplay} connection "${p.connectionLabel}".` };
}

//STAGE 5
yield { type: 'stage', stage: 'schemas', label: 'Reading data shapes' }; const triggerPlan = plans.find(p => p.phraseOrder === -1)!;
const schemas = await hydrateSchemas(
{ reg: this.reg, conns: this.conns, pool: this.pool }, args.orgId, plans, triggerPlan);

//STAGE 6
yield { type: 'stage', stage: 'mapping', label: 'Mapping fields between steps' }; const { props, flags } = await mapFields({ gw: this.gw, reg: this.reg },
{ intent, plans, upstreamSchemas: schemas, attribution });

// conditions
const paths = Object.entries(schemas).flatMap(([sid, shape]) => flattenPaths(shape).map(p => `${sid}.${p.path}`));
const conditions: Record<number, any> = {}; for (const l of intent.logic)
if (l.naturalCondition)
conditions[l.afterStepOrder] = await compileCondition( this.gw, l.naturalCondition, paths, attribution);

//STAGE 7
yield { type: 'stage', stage: 'assemble', label: 'Building the flow' }; let def = assembleGraph({ intent, plans, props, conditions });

// ---------- STAGE 8: validate → repair (max 2 passes) ----------yield { type: 'stage', stage: 'validate', label: 'Checking the flow' }; let issues = await this.validator.lint(args.orgId, def);
for (let pass = 0; pass < 2; pass++) { const fixable = issues.filter(i =>
i.severity === 'error' && i.code !== 'CONNECTION_REqUIRED'); if (!fixable.length) break;
yield { type: 'reasoning',
text: `Repair pass ${pass + 1}: ${fixable.map(i => i.code).join(', ')}` }; def = await this.repair(def, fixable, schemas, attribution);
issues = await this.validator.lint(args.orgId, def);
}

// ---------- STAGE 9: persist as DRAFT ----------
const flow = args.flowId
? await this.db.flows.byId(args.orgId, args.flowId)
: await this.db.flows.create({ orgId: args.orgId, projectId: args.projectId, name: intent.summary.slice(0, 80), createdBy: args.userId,

origin: 'copilot', status: 'draft', draftDefinition: emptyDefinition() });

const patch = compare(flow.draftDefinition, def);
const summary = `${plans.length - 1} action step(s), ` +
`${intent.logic.length} logic node(s)`;

if (args.mode === 'ask_as_you_build') { yield { type: 'proposal', patch, summary,
confidence: avg(choices.map(c => c.confidence)) };
await this.db.copilotActions.insert({ sessionId: session.id, kind: 'plan',
patch, reasoning: intent.summary, confidence: avg(choices.map(c => c.confidence)), accepted: null });
// The client calls POST /copilot/:session/accept to apply.
} else {
await this.flows.saveDraft(args.orgId, flow.id, def);// re-validates via zod await this.db.copilotActions.insert({ sessionId: session.id, kind: 'plan',
patch, reasoning: intent.summary, confidence: avg(choices.map(c => c.confidence)), accepted: true });
yield { type: 'applied', patch, summary };
}

for (const f of flags)
yield { type: 'todo', kind: 'fill_field',
message: `${f.stepId}: I left "${f.prop}" blank — ${f.reason}`, target: { stepId: f.stepId, prop: f.prop } };

// Publishing stays human. Copilot only reports readiness. yield { type: 'done', flowId: flow.id,
publishable: !issues.some(i => i.severity === 'error'), issues };

} catch (e: any) {
yield { type: 'error', message: e.message };
}
}

/** Targeted repair: the model sees only the failures and the legal vocabulary. */ private async repair(def: TFlowDefinition, issues: Issue[],
schemas: Record<string, unknown>, attribution: any) { const legalTokens = Object.entries(schemas).flatMap(([sid, shape]) =>
flattenPaths(shape).map(p => `{{${sid}.${p.path}}}`)); const { data } = await this.gw.callJson({
purpose: 'copilot_repair', system:
`Fix ONLY the listed validation errors in this flow definition.
Return the complete corrected flow definition JSON.
Do not add, remove or reorder steps unless an error requires it.
Use only these data tokens: ${legalTokens.slice(0, 200).join(' ')}
If a required field cannot be filled correctly, set it to "" and leave it for the human.
Never change any connectionId.`, jsonSchema: { name: 'flow_definition',
schema: zodToJsonSchema(FlowDefinition) }, messages: [{ role: 'user', content:
`ERRORS:
${issues.map(i => `- [${i.code}] ${i.path}: ${i.message}`).join('\n')}

FLOW:
${JSON.stringify(def, null, 2)}` }], attribution,
}, v => FlowDefinition.parse(v)); return data;
}
}

Capability boundary, enforced in code
// packages/copilot/src/guardrails.ts/*** Copilot's capability envelope. These are service-layer checks, NOT prompt text —* a jailbroken prompt still cannot cross them.*/export const COPILOT_CAPABILITIES = { canWriteDraft: true,canPublish: false, canCreateConnection: false, canSelectExistingConnection: true, canReadCredential: false,canExecuteWriteActionDuringAuthoring: false,// test steps are human-initiated canExecuteReadActionForSchema: true,// polling a trigger for a sample is OK// packages/copilot/src/guardrails.ts/*** Copilot's capability envelope. These are service-layer checks, NOT prompt text —* a jailbroken prompt still cannot cross them.*/export const COPILOT_CAPABILITIES = { canWriteDraft: true,canPublish: false, canCreateConnection: false, canSelectExistingConnection: true, canReadCredential: false,canExecuteWriteActionDuringAuthoring: false,// test steps are human-initiated canExecuteReadActionForSchema: true,// polling a trigger for a sample is OK
// packages/copilot/src/guardrails.ts
/**
* Copilot's capability envelope. These are service-layer checks, NOT prompt text —
* a jailbroken prompt still cannot cross them.
*/
export const COPILOT_CAPABILITIES = { canWriteDraft: true,
canPublish: false, canCreateConnection: false, canSelectExistingConnection: true, canReadCredential: false,
canExecuteWriteActionDuringAuthoring: false,// test steps are human-initiated canExecuteReadActionForSchema: true,// polling a trigger for a sample is OK
// packages/copilot/src/guardrails.ts
/**
* Copilot's capability envelope. These are service-layer checks, NOT prompt text —
* a jailbroken prompt still cannot cross them.
*/
export const COPILOT_CAPABILITIES = { canWriteDraft: true,
canPublish: false, canCreateConnection: false, canSelectExistingConnection: true, canReadCredential: false,
canExecuteWriteActionDuringAuthoring: false,// test steps are human-initiated canExecuteReadActionForSchema: true,// polling a trigger for a sample is OK


canDeleteFlow: false, canChangeBilling: false,} as const;export function assertCopilotAllowed(op: keyof typeof COPILOT_CAPABILITIES) { if (!COPILOT_CAPABILITIES[op])throw new ForbiddenException(`COPILOT_FORBIDDEN:${op}`);}/** Wrap any service Copilot touches. */ export class CopilotScopedFlows {constructor(private inner: FlowsService) {}saveDraft(...a: Parameters<FlowsService['saveDraft']>) { return this.inner.saveDraft(...a); } publish(): never { assertCopilotAllowed('canPublish'); throw new Error('unreachable'); }}canDeleteFlow: false, canChangeBilling: false,} as const;export function assertCopilotAllowed(op: keyof typeof COPILOT_CAPABILITIES) { if (!COPILOT_CAPABILITIES[op])throw new ForbiddenException(`COPILOT_FORBIDDEN:${op}`);}/** Wrap any service Copilot touches. */ export class CopilotScopedFlows {constructor(private inner: FlowsService) {}saveDraft(...a: Parameters<FlowsService['saveDraft']>) { return this.inner.saveDraft(...a); } publish(): never { assertCopilotAllowed('canPublish'); throw new Error('unreachable'); }}
canDeleteFlow: false, canChangeBilling: false,
} as const;

export function assertCopilotAllowed(op: keyof typeof COPILOT_CAPABILITIES) { if (!COPILOT_CAPABILITIES[op])
throw new ForbiddenException(`COPILOT_FORBIDDEN:${op}`);
}

/** Wrap any service Copilot touches. */ export class CopilotScopedFlows {
constructor(private inner: FlowsService) {}
saveDraft(...a: Parameters<FlowsService['saveDraft']>) { return this.inner.saveDraft(...a); } publish(): never { assertCopilotAllowed('canPublish'); throw new Error('unreachable'); }
}
canDeleteFlow: false, canChangeBilling: false,
} as const;

export function assertCopilotAllowed(op: keyof typeof COPILOT_CAPABILITIES) { if (!COPILOT_CAPABILITIES[op])
throw new ForbiddenException(`COPILOT_FORBIDDEN:${op}`);
}

/** Wrap any service Copilot touches. */ export class CopilotScopedFlows {
constructor(private inner: FlowsService) {}
saveDraft(...a: Parameters<FlowsService['saveDraft']>) { return this.inner.saveDraft(...a); } publish(): never { assertCopilotAllowed('canPublish'); throw new Error('unreachable'); }
}
In-builder Copilot: the assist surface
Generation is one entry point. The Copilot that matters day to day is the one embedded in the builder, exactly as Zapier and Copilot Studio present it.

export class CopilotController {
constructor(private copilot: CopilotService, private assist: AssistService) {}

/** Streamed generation into the side panel. */ @Post('generate')
@Sse()
generate(@Req() req, @Body() b: { prompt: string; flowId?: string;
mode?: 'auto_build'|'ask_as_you_build'; attachments?: string[] }) {
const gen = this.copilot.generate({
orgId: req.user.orgId, projectId: req.body.projectId, userId: req.user.id, userEmail: req.user.email, prompt: b.prompt, flowId: b.flowId, attachments: b.attachments, mode: b.mode ?? 'auto_build' });
return from(gen).pipe(map(ev => ({ data: JSON.stringify(ev) })));
}

/** "Ask as you build": apply a proposed patch after human confirmation. */ @Post(':sessionId/accept')
accept(@Req() req, @Param('sessionId') s: string, @Body() b: { actionId: string }) { return this.copilot.acceptProposal(req.user.orgId, s, b.actionId, req.user.id);
}

/** Inline: suggest a value for one field the user is focused on. */ @Post('suggest-field')
suggestField(@Req() req, @Body() b: { flowId: string; stepId: string; prop: string }) { return this.assist.suggestField(req.user.orgId, b);
}

/** Inline: plain-language explanation of what a field does. */ @Post('explain-field')
explainField(@Req() req, @Body() b: { piece: string; operation: string; prop: string }) { return this.assist.explainField(b);
}

/** Inline: "what should come next?" */ @Post('suggest-next-step')
suggestNext(@Req() req, @Body() b: { flowId: string }) {
return this.assist.suggestNextStep(req.user.orgId, b.flowId);
}

/** Refinement in plain language against the existing draft. */ @Post('refine')
@Sse()
refine(@Req() req, @Body() b: { flowId: string; instruction: string }) {
return from(this.copilot.refine({ orgId: req.user.orgId, userId: req.user.id, userEmail: req.user.email, flowId: b.flowId, instruction: b.instruction }))
.pipe(map(ev => ({ data: JSON.stringify(ev) })));
}

// apps/api/src/copilot/copilot.controller.ts @Controller('copilot')
export class CopilotController {

}

/** "Ask as you build": apply a proposed patch after human confirmation. */ @Post(':sessionId/accept')

}

/** Inline: plain-language explanation of what a field does. */ @Post('explain-field')
explainField(@Req() req, @Body() b: { piece: string; operation: string; prop: string }) { return this.assist.explainField(b);
}

suggestNext(@Req() req, @Body() b: { flowId: string }) {

}

/** Refinement in plain language against the existing draft. */ @Post('refine')

refine(@Req() req, @Body() b: { flowId: string; instruction: string }) {

.pipe(map(ev => ({ data: JSON.stringify(ev) })));
}

/** Ops copilot: diagnose a failed run and propose a fix. */ @Post('diagnose-run')
diagnose(@Req() req, @Body() b: { runId: string }) { return this.assist.diagnoseRun(req.user.orgId, b.runId);


}}}}
}
}
}
}

// apps/api/src/copilot/assist.service.ts @Injectable()
export class AssistService {
constructor(private gw: ModelGateway, private reg: PieceRegistry, private db: Db, private index: CatalogIndex,
private validator: FlowValidator) {}

/** Field-level suggestion: uses the real upstream context of THIS draft. */
async suggestField(orgId: string, a: { flowId: string; stepId: string; prop: string }) { const flow = await this.db.flows.byId(orgId, a.flowId);
const def = flow.draftDefinition as TFlowDefinition; const step = findStep(def, a.stepId);
const action = this.reg.getAction((step as any).piece.name, (step as any).operation); const prop = action.props[a.prop];
const upstream = await upstreamSampleContext(this.db, this.reg, def, a.stepId);

const { data } = await this.gw.callJson({ purpose: 'copilot_map',
system: `Suggest up to 3 values for one input field. Use {{step.path}} tokens from AVAILABLE DATA only. Rank best first. If nothing fits, return an empty list.`,
jsonSchema: { name: 'suggestions', schema: { type: 'object', additionalProperties: false, properties: { suggestions: { type: 'array',
items: { type: 'object', additionalProperties: false, properties: { value: { type: 'string' }, why: { type: 'string' },
confidence: { type: 'number' } },
required: ['value','why','confidence'] } } }, required: ['suggestions'] } }, messages: [{ role: 'user', content:
`FIELD: ${prop.displayName} (${prop.kind}${prop.required ? ', required' : ''})
${prop.description ?? ''} ${prop.aiHint ?? ''}
STEP: ${(step as any).piece.name} → ${(step as any).operation} AVAILABLE DATA:
${flattenPaths(upstream).map(p => `- {{${p.path}}} (${p.type})` +
`${p.example !== undefined ? ` e.g. ${JSON.stringify(p.example)}` : ''}`).join('\n')}` }], attribution: { orgId },
}, v => v as any); return data.suggestions;
}

async explainField(a: { piece: string; operation: string; prop: string }) { const action = this.reg.getAction(a.piece, a.operation);
const p = action.props[a.prop];
const { data } = await this.gw.call({
purpose: 'copilot_map', cacheable: true,// static → cache aggressively system: 'Explain this automation input field in two sentences, ' +
'plainly, for a non-technical user. Include one concrete example value.', messages: [{ role: 'user', content:
`App: ${a.piece}\nOperation: ${action.displayName}\n` +
`Field: ${p.displayName} (${p.kind})\nDocs: ${p.description ?? ''} ${p.aiHint ?? ''}` }], attribution: { orgId: 'system' },
});
return { explanation: data.content };
}

/** Next-step suggestion, constrained to the real catalog. */ async suggestNextStep(orgId: string, flowId: string) {
const flow = await this.db.flows.byId(orgId, flowId); const def = flow.draftDefinition as TFlowDefinition; const describe = summariseFlow(def);
const candidates = await this.index.search(
`next likely step after: ${describe}`, 'action', 15); const { data } = await this.gw.callJson({
purpose: 'copilot_plan',
system: 'Suggest the 3 most useful next steps for this flow. ' + 'Choose only from the candidate ids provided.',
jsonSchema: { name: 'next_steps', schema: { type: 'object', additionalProperties: false, properties: { steps: { type: 'array', items: {
type: 'object', additionalProperties: false, properties: { candidateId: { type: 'string',
enum: candidates.map(c => `${c.piece}:action:${c.operation}`) }, label: { type: 'string' }, why: { type: 'string' } },
required: ['candidateId','label','why'] } } }, required: ['steps'] } }, messages: [{ role: 'user', content:
`FLOW SO FAR:\n${describe}\n\nCANDIDATES:\n` +
candidates.map(c => `${c.piece}:action:${c.operation} | ${c.pieceDisplay} → ` +
`${c.display} | ${c.description}`).join('\n') }],


attribution: { orgId },}, v => v as any); return data.steps;}}attribution: { orgId },}, v => v as any); return data.steps;}}
attribution: { orgId },
}, v => v as any); return data.steps;
}
}

}, v => v as any); return data.steps;
}

Ops copilot: diagnose a failed run, propose a patch
// apps/api/src/copilot/diagnose.ts export const Diagnosis = z.object({cause: z.string(),category: z.enum(['auth','mapping','validation','rate_limit','logic', 'third_party_outage','data_quality','configuration']),userFix: z.string(),// what the human should dopatch: z.array(z.any()),// RFC-6902 patch against the draft patchExplanation: z.string(),confidence: z.number().min(0).max(1), safeToAutoApply: z.boolean(),});export async function diagnoseRun(deps: { gw: ModelGateway; db: Db; reg: PieceRegistry },orgId: string, runId: string) { const run = await deps.db.runs.byId(runId);const steps = await deps.db.runSteps.byRun(runId);const def = (await deps.db.flowVersions.byId(run.flowVersionId)).definition; const failed = steps.find(s => s.status === 'failed');const { data } = await deps.gw.callJson({ purpose: 'ops_diagnose',system:`Diagnose one failed automation run.Name the single most likely root cause. Do not list possibilities.Propose a minimal RFC-6902 JSON Patch against the flow definition that would fix it.Set safeToAutoApply=false for anything touching credentials, destructive actions, or logic whose intent you must guess.If the cause is external (third-party outage, revoked token, rate limit), return an empty patch and explain the human action instead.`,jsonSchema: { name: 'diagnosis', schema: zodToJsonSchema(Diagnosis) }, messages: [{ role: 'user', content:`FLOW DEFINITION:${JSON.stringify(def, null, 2)}FAILED STEP: ${failed?.stepId} (${failed?.stepType}), error class ${failed?.errorClass} ERROR: ${JSON.stringify(failed?.errorJson)}RESOLVED INPUT: ${JSON.stringify(failed?.inputJson)}STEP HISTORY:${steps.map(s => `${s.stepId} ${s.status} ${s.durationMs}ms ` +`out=${JSON.stringify(s.outputJson)?.slice(0, 400)}`).join('\n')}` }], attribution: { orgId, runId },}, v => Diagnosis.parse(v));return data;// The UI renders the patch as a reviewable diff. Human applies it.}// apps/api/src/copilot/diagnose.ts export const Diagnosis = z.object({cause: z.string(),category: z.enum(['auth','mapping','validation','rate_limit','logic', 'third_party_outage','data_quality','configuration']),userFix: z.string(),// what the human should dopatch: z.array(z.any()),// RFC-6902 patch against the draft patchExplanation: z.string(),confidence: z.number().min(0).max(1), safeToAutoApply: z.boolean(),});export async function diagnoseRun(deps: { gw: ModelGateway; db: Db; reg: PieceRegistry },orgId: string, runId: string) { const run = await deps.db.runs.byId(runId);const steps = await deps.db.runSteps.byRun(runId);const def = (await deps.db.flowVersions.byId(run.flowVersionId)).definition; const failed = steps.find(s => s.status === 'failed');const { data } = await deps.gw.callJson({ purpose: 'ops_diagnose',system:`Diagnose one failed automation run.Name the single most likely root cause. // apps/api/src/copilot/diagnose.ts export const Diagnosis = z.object({
cause: z.string(),
category: z.enum(['auth','mapping','validation','rate_limit','logic', 'third_party_outage','data_quality','configuration']),
userFix: z.string(),// what the human should do
patch: z.array(z.any()),// RFC-6902 patch against the draft patchExplanation: z.string(),
confidence: z.number().min(0).max(1), safeToAutoApply: z.boolean(),
});

export async function diagnoseRun(deps: { gw: ModelGateway; db: Db; reg: PieceRegistry },
orgId: string, runId: string) { const run = await deps.db.runs.byId(runId);
const steps = await deps.db.runSteps.byRun(runId);
const def = (await deps.db.flowVersions.byId(run.flowVersionId)).definition; const failed = steps.find(s => s.status === 'failed');

const { data } = await deps.gw.callJson({ purpose: 'ops_diagnose',
system:
`Diagnose one failed automation run.
Name the single most likely root cause. Do not list possibilities.
Propose a minimal RFC-6902 JSON Patch against the flow definition that would fix it.
Set safeToAutoApply=false for anything touching credentials, destructive actions, or logic whose intent you must guess.
If the cause is external (third-party outage, revoked token, rate limit), return an empty patch and explain the human action instead.`,
jsonSchema: { name: 'diagnosis', schema: zodToJsonSchema(Diagnosis) }, messages: [{ role: 'user', content:
`FLOW DEFINITION:
${JSON.stringify(def, null, 2)}

FAILED STEP: ${failed?.stepId} (${failed?.stepType}), error class ${failed?.errorClass} ERROR: ${JSON.stringify(failed?.errorJson)}
RESOLVED INPUT: ${JSON.stringify(failed?.inputJson)}

STEP HISTORY:
${steps.map(s => `${s.stepId} ${s.status} ${s.durationMs}ms ` +
`out=${JSON.stringify(s.outputJson)?.slice(0, 400)}`).join('\n')}` }], attribution: { orgId, runId },
}, v => Diagnosis.parse(v));

return data;// The UI renders the patch as a reviewable diff. Human applies it.
}

});

Copilot evaluation harness
Without this, Copilot quality silently regresses on every prompt or model change. This is the gate that must pass in CI.
// packages/copilot/eval/harness.ts export interface GoldenCase {id: string; prompt: string;availableConnections: string[]; expect: {triggerOp: string; actionOps: string[];logicKinds: string[];// pieces the fake tenant has connected// "typeform:trigger:new_submission"// ordered// ['router','approval']requiredMappings: Array<{ stepId: string; prop: string; tokenContains: string }>; mustBePublishableAfterAuth: boolean;};}export interface EvalResult {// packages/copilot/eval/harness.ts export interface GoldenCase {id: string; prompt: string;availableConnections: string[]; expect: {triggerOp: string; actionOps: string[];logicKinds: string[];// pieces the fake tenant has connected// "typeform:trigger:new_submission"// ordered// ['router','approval']requiredMappings: Array<{ stepId: string; prop: string; tokenContains: string }>; mustBePublishableAfterAuth: boolean;};}export interface EvalResult {
// packages/copilot/eval/harness.ts export interface GoldenCase {
id: string; prompt: string;
availableConnections: string[]; expect: {
triggerOp: string; actionOps: string[];
logicKinds: string[];
// pieces the fake tenant has connected

// "typeform:trigger:new_submission"
// ordered
// ['router','approval']
requiredMappings: Array<{ stepId: string; prop: string; tokenContains: string }>; mustBePublishableAfterAuth: boolean;
};
}

export interface EvalResult {
// packages/copilot/eval/harness.ts export interface GoldenCase {
id: string; prompt: string;
availableConnections: string[]; expect: {
triggerOp: string; actionOps: string[];
logicKinds: string[];
// pieces the fake tenant has connected

// "typeform:trigger:new_submission"
// ordered
// ['router','approval']
requiredMappings: Array<{ stepId: string; prop: string; tokenContains: string }>; mustBePublishableAfterAuth: boolean;
};
}

export interface EvalResult {

export async function runEval(cases: GoldenCase[], copilot: CopilotService,
validator: FlowValidator): Promise<EvalResult[]> { const out: EvalResult[] = [];
for (const c of cases) { const t0 = Date.now();
const tenant = await seedFakeTenant(c.availableConnections); let def: TFlowDefinition | null = null;
let cost = 0;
for await (const ev of copilot.generate({ ...tenant, prompt: c.prompt,
mode: 'auto_build' })) { if (ev.type === 'done') def = await loadDraft(ev.flowId);
}
const got = extractOps(def!);
const issues = await validator.lint(tenant.orgId, def!); out.push({
caseId: c.id,
triggerCorrect: got.triggerOp === c.expect.triggerOp, actionSetF1: f1(got.actionOps, c.expect.actionOps),
orderCorrect: JSON.stringify(got.actionOps) === JSON.stringify(c.expect.actionOps), logicCorrect: setEq(got.logicKinds, c.expect.logicKinds),
mappingRecall: c.expect.requiredMappings.filter(m =>
String(propOf(def!, m.stepId, m.prop) ?? '').includes(m.tokenContains)).length
/ Math.max(1, c.expect.requiredMappings.length), hallucinatedOps: got.unknownOps.length,
invalidTokens: issues.filter(i => i.code === 'UNKNOWN_REFERENCE').length, publishableAfterAuth: !issues.some(i =>
i.severity === 'error' && i.code !== 'CONNECTION_REqUIRED'), latencyMs: Date.now() - t0,
costUsd: cost,
});
}
return out;
}
caseId: string; triggerCorrect: boolean; actionSetF1: number; orderCorrect: boolean; logicCorrect: boolean; mappingRecall: number;
hallucinatedOps: number;// ops not in the catalog — must always be 0 invalidTokens: number;// tokens not resolvable — must always be 0 publishableAfterAuth: boolean;
latencyMs: number; costUsd: number;
}

export async function runEval(cases: GoldenCase[], copilot: CopilotService,
validator: FlowValidator): Promise<EvalResult[]> { const out: EvalResult[] = [];
for (const c of cases) { const t0 = Date.now();
const tenant = await seedFakeTenant(c.availableConnections); let def: TFlowDefinition | null = null;
let cost = 0;
for await (const ev of copilot.generate({ ...tenant, prompt: c.prompt,
mode: 'auto_build' })) { if (ev.type === 'done') def = await loadDraft(ev.flowId);
}
const got = extractOps(def!);
const issues = await validator.lint(tenant.orgId, def!); out.push({
caseId: c.id,
triggerCorrect: got.triggerOp === c.expect.triggerOp, actionSetF1: f1(got.actionOps, c.expect.actionOps),
orderCorrect: JSON.stringify(got.actionOps) === JSON.stringify(c.expect.actionOps), logicCorrect: setEq(got.logicKinds, c.expect.logicKinds),
mappingRecall: c.expect.requiredMappings.filter(m =>
String(propOf(def!, m.stepId, m.prop) ?? '').includes(m.tokenContains)).length
/ Math.max(1, c.expect.requiredMappings.length), hallucinatedOps: got.unknownOps.length,
invalidTokens: issues.filter(i => i.code === 'UNKNOWN_REFERENCE').length, publishableAfterAuth: !issues.some(i =>
i.severity === 'error' && i.code !== 'CONNECTION_REqUIRED'), latencyMs: Date.now() - t0,
costUsd: cost,
});
}
return out;
}
CI gatєs (fail thє build if brєachєd):

Metric
Gate
Why
hallucinatedOps
must be 0 across all cases
Constrained decoding is broken if this is non-zero
invalidTokens
must be 0
A generated flow that cannot resolve its own references is defective
triggerCorrect
≥ 0.95
The trigger is the hardest thing for a user to fix later
actionSetF1
≥ 0.90
Missing/extra steps are the loudest failure
mappingRecall
≥ 0.85
Below this, users hand-fix everything and stop trusting Copilot
publishableAfterAuth
≥ 0.80
The real product promise: connect accounts, then publish
p95 latency
≤ 25 s
Beyond this, users abandon the stream
Seed the golden set with 60–100 cases spanning: single-app, three-app chains, conditions, loops, approvals, AI steps, schedule triggers, ambiguous prompts (which must produce ambiguities , not guesses), and adversarial prompts (which must not produce destructive actions).

Refinement — editing an existing draft in plain language
"It's not filtering out test submissions" must work as well as the first prompt. Refinement differs from generation in one crucial way: the model must produce a minimal patch, not a fresh flow, or it will silently destroy the user's manual edits.

export async function* refine(deps: {
gw: ModelGateway; db: Db; index: CatalogIndex; validator: FlowValidator; flows: FlowsService; reg: PieceRegistry;
}, args: { orgId: string; userId: string; userEmail: string;
flowId: string; instruction: string }): AsyncGenerator<CopilotEvent> {

await deps.flows.saveDraft(args.orgId, args.flowId, next);
yield { type: 'applied', patch: data.patch, summary: data.explanation }; yield { type: 'done', flowId: args.flowId,

Part 7 — API Surface, Testing, Observability, Security

7.1 REST controllers

// apps/api/src/flows/flows.controller.ts @Controller('projects/:projectId/flows') @UseGuards(AuthGuard, RbacGuard)
export class FlowsController {
constructor(private flows: FlowsService, private runs: RunCreationService, private validator: FlowValidator) {}
@Get()
@Rbac('flow:read')
list(@Req() r, @Param('projectId') p: string, @query() q: Listquery) { return this.flows.list(r.user.orgId, p, q);
}
@Post()
@Rbac('flow:write')
create(@Req() r, @Param('projectId') p: string, @Body() b: CreateFlowDto) { return this.flows.create(r.user.orgId, p, r.user.id, b);
}

@Get(':id') @Rbac('flow:read')
get(@Req() r, @Param('id') id: string) { return this.flows.get(r.user.orgId, id); }

@Patch(':id/draft') @Rbac('flow:write')
saveDraft(@Req() r, @Param('id') id: string, @Body() b: { definition: unknown }) { return this.flows.saveDraft(r.user.orgId, id, FlowDefinition.parse(b.definition));
}

@Post(':id/validate') @Rbac('flow:read') validate(@Req() r, @Param('id') id: string) {
return this.flows.validateDraft(r.user.orgId, id);
}

@Post(':id/publish') @Rbac('flow:publish')
publish(@Req() r, @Param('id') id: string, @Body() b: { note?: string }) { return this.flows.publish(r.user.orgId, id, r.user.id, b.note);
}

@Post(':id/rollback/:version') @Rbac('flow:publish')
rollback(@Req() r, @Param('id') id: string, @Param('version') v: string) { return this.flows.rollback(r.user.orgId, id, Number(v), r.user.id);
}

@Post(':id/enable') @Rbac('flow:publish')
enable(@Req() r, @Param('id') id: string) { return this.flows.setEnabled(r.user.orgId, id, true); }

@Post(':id/disable') @Rbac('flow:publish')
disable(@Req() r, @Param('id') id: string) { return this.flows.setEnabled(r.user.orgId, id, false); }

/** Single-step test — the authoring loop's inner cycle. */ @Post(':id/steps/:stepId/test') @Rbac('flow:write')
testStep(@Req() r, @Param('id') id: string, @Param('stepId') s: string, @Body() b: { sampleContext?: Record<string, unknown> }) {
return this.flows.testStep(r.user.orgId, id, s, b.sampleContext ?? {});
}

/** Whole-draft test run — writes to third parties, so it is explicit and audited. */ @Post(':id/test-run') @Rbac('flow:write')
testRun(@Req() r, @Param('id') id: string, @Body() b: { payload?: unknown }) { return this.runs.createTestRun(r.user.orgId, id, r.user.id, b.payload ?? {});
}
}
// apps/api/src/flows/flows.controller.ts @Controller('projects/:projectId/flows') @UseGuards(AuthGuard, RbacGuard)
export class FlowsController {
constructor(private flows: FlowsService, private runs: RunCreationService, private validator: FlowValidator) {}
@Get()
@Rbac('flow:read')
list(@Req() r, @Param('projectId') p: string, @query() q: Listquery) { return this.flows.list(r.user.orgId, p, q);
}
@Post()
@Rbac('flow:write')
create(@Req() r, @Param('projectId') p: string, @Body() b: CreateFlowDto) { return this.flows.create(r.user.orgId, p, r.user.id, b);
}

@Get(':id') @Rbac('flow:read')
get(@Req() r, @Param('id') id: string) { return this.flows.get(r.user.orgId, id); }

@Patch(':id/draft') @Rbac('flow:write')
saveDraft(@Req() r, @Param('id') id: string, @Body() b: { definition: unknown }) { return this.flows.saveDraft(r.user.orgId, id, FlowDefinition.parse(b.definition));
}

@Post(':id/validate') @Rbac('flow:read') validate(@Req() r, @Param('id') id: string) {
return this.flows.validateDraft(r.user.orgId, id);
}

@Post(':id/publish') @Rbac('flow:publish')
publish(@Req() r, @Param('id') id: string, @Body() b: { note?: string }) { return this.flows.publish(r.user.orgId, id, r.user.id, b.note);
}

@Post(':id/rollback/:version') @Rbac('flow:publish')
rollback(@Req() r, @Param('id') id: string, @Param('version') v: string) { return this.flows.rollback(r.user.orgId, id, Number(v), r.user.id);
}

@Post(':id/enable') @Rbac('flow:publish')
enable(@Req() r, @Param('id') id: string) { return this.flows.setEnabled(r.user.orgId, id, true); }

@Post(':id/disable') @Rbac('flow:publish')
disable(@Req() r, @Param('id') id: string) { return this.flows.setEnabled(r.user.orgId, id, false); }

/** Single-step test — the authoring loop's inner cycle. */ @Post(':id/steps/:stepId/test') @Rbac('flow:write')
testStep(@Req() r, @Param('id') id: string, @Param('stepId') s: string, @Body() b: { sampleContext?: Record<string, unknown> }) {
return this.flows.testStep(r.user.orgId, id, s, b.sampleContext ?? {});
}

/** Whole-draft test run — writes to third parties, so it is explicit and audited. */ @Post(':id/test-run') @Rbac('flow:write')
testRun(@Req() r, @Param('id') id: string, @Body() b: { payload?: unknown }) { return this.runs.createTestRun(r.user.orgId, id, r.user.id, b.payload ?? {});
}
}
// apps/api/src/runs/runs.controller.ts @Controller('runs') @UseGuards(AuthGuard, RbacGuard)
export class RunsController {
constructor(private db: Db, private runs: RunCreationService) {}

@Get() @Rbac('run:read')
list(@Req() r, @query() q: { flowId?: string; status?: string; from?: string;
to?: string; cursor?: string; limit?: string }) {
return this.db.runs.list(r.user.orgId, { ...q, limit: Math.min(+(q.limit ?? 50), 200) });
}
// apps/api/src/runs/runs.controller.ts @Controller('runs') @UseGuards(AuthGuard, RbacGuard)
export class RunsController {
constructor(private db: Db, private runs: RunCreationService) {}

@Get() @Rbac('run:read')
list(@Req() r, @query() q: { flowId?: string; status?: string; from?: string;
to?: string; cursor?: string; limit?: string }) {
return this.db.runs.list(r.user.orgId, { ...q, limit: Math.min(+(q.limit ?? 50), 200) });
}



@Get(':id') @Rbac('run:read')async get(@Req() r, @Param('id') id: string) {const run = await this.db.runs.byId(id, r.user.orgId); return { ...run, steps: await this.db.runSteps.byRun(id) };}@Get(':id/steps/:stepId/payload') @Rbac('run:read_payload') payload(@Req() r, @Param('id') id: string, @Param('stepId') s: string) {return this.db.runSteps.payload(r.user.orgId, id, s);// separate, higher permission}@Post(':id/replay') @Rbac('run:replay') replay(@Req() r, @Param('id') id: string,@Body() b: { fromStepId?: string; useLatestVersion?: boolean }) { return this.runs.replay(r.user.orgId, id, r.user.id, b);}@Post(':id/cancel') @Rbac('run:write')cancel(@Req() r, @Param('id') id: string) { return this.runs.cancel(r.user.orgId, id); }}@Get(':id') @Rbac('run:read')async get(@Req() r, @Param('id') id: string) {const run = await this.db.runs.byId(id, r.user.orgId); return { ...run, steps: await this.db.runSteps.byRun(id) };}@Get(':id/steps/:stepId/payload') @Rbac('run:read_payload') payload(@Req() r, @Param('id') id: string, @Param('stepId') s: string) {return this.db.runSteps.payload(r.user.orgId, id, s);// separate, higher permission}@Post(':id/replay') @Rbac('run:replay') replay(@Req() r, @Param('id') id: string,@Body() b: { fromStepId?: string; useLatestVersion?: boolean }) { return this.runs.replay(r.user.orgId, id, r.user.id, b);}@Post(':id/cancel') @Rbac('run:write')cancel(@Req() r, @Param('id') id: string) { return this.runs.cancel(r.user.orgId, id); }}
@Get(':id') @Rbac('run:read')
async get(@Req() r, @Param('id') id: string) {
const run = await this.db.runs.byId(id, r.user.orgId); return { ...run, steps: await this.db.runSteps.byRun(id) };
}

@Get(':id/steps/:stepId/payload') @Rbac('run:read_payload') payload(@Req() r, @Param('id') id: string, @Param('stepId') s: string) {
return this.db.runSteps.payload(r.user.orgId, id, s);// separate, higher permission
}

@Post(':id/replay') @Rbac('run:replay') replay(@Req() r, @Param('id') id: string,
@Body() b: { fromStepId?: string; useLatestVersion?: boolean }) { return this.runs.replay(r.user.orgId, id, r.user.id, b);
}

@Post(':id/cancel') @Rbac('run:write')
cancel(@Req() r, @Param('id') id: string) { return this.runs.cancel(r.user.orgId, id); }
}
@Get(':id') @Rbac('run:read')
async get(@Req() r, @Param('id') id: string) {
const run = await this.db.runs.byId(id, r.user.orgId); return { ...run, steps: await this.db.runSteps.byRun(id) };
}

@Get(':id/steps/:stepId/payload') @Rbac('run:read_payload') payload(@Req() r, @Param('id') id: string, @Param('stepId') s: string) {
return this.db.runSteps.payload(r.user.orgId, id, s);// separate, higher permission
}

@Post(':id/replay') @Rbac('run:replay') replay(@Req() r, @Param('id') id: string,
@Body() b: { fromStepId?: string; useLatestVersion?: boolean }) { return this.runs.replay(r.user.orgId, id, r.user.id, b);
}

@Post(':id/cancel') @Rbac('run:write')
cancel(@Req() r, @Param('id') id: string) { return this.runs.cancel(r.user.orgId, id); }
}

// apps/api/src/connections/connections.controller.ts @Controller('connections')
@UseGuards(AuthGuard, RbacGuard) export class ConnectionsController {
constructor(private conns: ConnectionsService) {}

@Get() @Rbac('connection:read')
list(@Req() r, @query('piece') piece?: string) {
return this.conns.list(r.user.orgId, piece);// metadata only — never secrets
}

@Post('api-key') @Rbac('connection:write')
createApiKey(@Req() r, @Body() b: { piece: string; label: string;
projectId?: string; values: Record<string,string> }) { return this.conns.createSecret(r.user.orgId, r.user.id, b);
}

@Post('oauth/start') @Rbac('connection:write')
start(@Req() r, @Body() b: { piece: string; projectId?: string; scopes?: string[] }) { return this.conns.startOAuth(r.user.orgId, r.user.id, b);// returns { authorizeUrl }
}

@Post(':id/test') @Rbac('connection:write')
test(@Req() r, @Param('id') id: string) { return this.conns.test(r.user.orgId, id); }

@Delete(':id') @Rbac('connection:delete') remove(@Req() r, @Param('id') id: string) {
return this.conns.remove(r.user.orgId, id);// 409 if any enabled flow depends on it
}
}
// apps/api/src/connections/connections.controller.ts @Controller('connections')
@UseGuards(AuthGuard, RbacGuard) export class ConnectionsController {
constructor(private conns: ConnectionsService) {}

@Get() @Rbac('connection:read')
list(@Req() r, @query('piece') piece?: string) {
return this.conns.list(r.user.orgId, piece);// metadata only — never secrets
}

@Post('api-key') @Rbac('connection:write')
createApiKey(@Req() r, @Body() b: { piece: string; label: string;
projectId?: string; values: Record<string,string> }) { return this.conns.createSecret(r.user.orgId, r.user.id, b);
}

@Post('oauth/start') @Rbac('connection:write')
start(@Req() r, @Body() b: { piece: string; projectId?: string; scopes?: string[] }) { return this.conns.startOAuth(r.user.orgId, r.user.id, b);// returns { authorizeUrl }
}

@Post(':id/test') @Rbac('connection:write')
test(@Req() r, @Param('id') id: string) { return this.conns.test(r.user.orgId, id); }

@Delete(':id') @Rbac('connection:delete') remove(@Req() r, @Param('id') id: string) {
return this.conns.remove(r.user.orgId, id);// 409 if any enabled flow depends on it
}
}

7.2 Testing strategy
Layer
Tool
What is tested
Gate
Unit
Vitest
Expression resolver, condition evaluator, flattenPaths , retry/backoff maths, crypto envelope round-trip
90% line coverage on
packages/core
Schema
Vitest + zod
Every piece's props parse; every sampleOutput matches its outputSchema
100% of pieces
Piece contract
Vitest + nock
Each action against recorded HTTP fixtures; auth refresh path; 429 handling
Every action has ≥1 happy + ≥1 error test
Executor
Vitest, in-memory queue
Branching, loops, dehydrate/ rehydrate on delay and approval, resume-after-crash idempotency
All step types
Integration
Testcontainers (Postgres + Redis)
Publish → activate trigger → webhook → run → step rows; RLS isolation between two orgs
Green before merge
Copilot eval
§6.10 harness
Golden set, hallucination and mapping gates
Gates in §6.10
Load
k6
5,000 webhooks/min sustained; p95 enqueue latency < 150 ms; queue depth recovery
Pre-release
Chaos
custom
Kill worker mid-step, revoke a token, 500 from a third party, Redis failover
Zero duplicate side effects
Security
Semgrep + npm audit + manual
Sandbox escape attempts, SSRF via HTTP step, credential leakage into logs
Zero high findings

const calls: string[] = []; const flow = defineFlow([action('a', () => { calls.push('a'); return { ok: true }; }),action('b', () => { calls.push('b'); throw new WorkerKilled(); }),action('c', () => { calls.push('c'); return { ok: true }; }),]);const run = await start(flow);expect(calls).toEqual(['a', 'b']);// crashed inside b await resume(run.id);// BullMq redelivery expect(calls).toEqual(['a', 'b', 'b', 'c']); // 'a' NEVER re-runs});const calls: string[] = []; const flow = defineFlow([action('a', () => { calls.push('a'); return { ok: true }; }),action('b', () => { calls.push('b'); throw new WorkerKilled(); }),action('c', () => { calls.push('c'); return { ok: true }; }),]);const run = await start(flow);expect(calls).toEqual(['a', 'b']);// crashed inside b await resume(run.id);// BullMq redelivery expect(calls).toEqual(['a', 'b', 'b', 'c']); // 'a' NEVER re-runs});

// packages/observability/src/metrics.ts
import { Counter, Histogram, Gauge } from 'prom-client';

export const M = {
runsStarted: new Counter({ name: 'orchestra_runs_started_total',
labelNames: ['org','flow','trigger_type'] as const, help: 'Runs started' }), runsFinished: new Counter({ name: 'orchestra_runs_finished_total',
labelNames: ['org','flow','status'] as const, help: 'Runs finished' }), runDuration: new Histogram({ name: 'orchestra_run_duration_seconds',
labelNames: ['flow'] as const, buckets: [0.1,0.5,1,2,5,10,30,60,300,1800], help: 'End-to-end run duration' }),
stepDuration: new Histogram({ name: 'orchestra_step_duration_seconds', labelNames: ['piece','operation','status'] as const,
buckets: [0.05,0.1,0.25,0.5,1,2,5,10,30,60], help: 'Step duration' }), stepErrors: new Counter({ name: 'orchestra_step_errors_total',
labelNames: ['piece','operation','error_class'] as const, help: 'Step errors' }), queueDepth: new Gauge({ name: 'orchestra_queue_depth',
labelNames: ['queue','state'] as const, help: 'queue depth' }), copilotGenerations: new Counter({ name: 'orchestra_copilot_generations_total',
labelNames: ['org','mode','outcome'] as const, help: 'Copilot generations' }), copilotStage: new Histogram({ name: 'orchestra_copilot_stage_seconds',
labelNames: ['stage'] as const, buckets: [0.2,0.5,1,2,4,8,16,32], help: 'Copilot stage latency' }),
copilotPublishRate: new Counter({ name: 'orchestra_copilot_published_total', labelNames: ['org','edited_before_publish'] as const,
help: 'Generated flows that reached publish' }), aiTokens: new Counter({ name: 'orchestra_ai_tokens_total',
labelNames: ['purpose','model','direction'] as const, help: 'Token usage' }), aiCostUsd: new Counter({ name: 'orchestra_ai_cost_usd_total',
labelNames: ['purpose','model'] as const, help: 'Model spend' }), credits: new Counter({ name: 'orchestra_credits_consumed_total',
labelNames: ['org','kind'] as const, help: 'Billable credits' }),

Tracing. One OpenTelemetry trace per run: root span run.execute carries run_id , flow_id , flow_version , org_id ; a child span per step carries step_id , piece , operation , attempt ; HTTP calls inside pieces auto-instrument. Copilot generations get their own trace with a span per stage, so the p95 stage histogram is directly attributable.
Structurєd logs. JSON, one line per event, always including org_id , run_id / copilot_session_id , step_id . A redaction layer scrubs anything matching known credential shapes and every value that came out of

export const REDACT_KEYS = /^(authorization|api[-_]?key|token|secret|password| refresh_token|access_token|client_secret|signature|cookie)$/i;

export function redact(v: unknown, depth = 0): unknown { if (depth > 8) return '[deep]';
if (Array.isArray(v)) return v.map(x => redact(x, depth + 1)); if (v && typeof v === 'object')
return Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(v).map(([k, val]) =>
[k, REDACT_KEYS.test(k) ? '[redacted]' : redact(val, depth + 1)]));

Metering and billing
> 0 in production

Dual metering resolves the conflict between the two source specs — organisations choose the model:

export type MeterMode = 'run' | 'step';

export function creditsFor(mode: MeterMode, run: RunRecord, steps: StepRecord[]) { const base = mode === 'run'
? 1
: steps.filter(s => s.status === 'succeeded' && BILLABLE.has(s.stepType)).length;

// AI is metered separately in every mode — the underlying cost is real and variable. const aiCredits = steps.reduce((n, s) => n + (s.aiCreditsUsed ?? 0), 0);

// apps/worker/src/metering.ts
export type MeterMode = 'run' | 'step';

export function creditsFor(mode: MeterMode, run: RunRecord, steps: StepRecord[]) { const base = mode === 'run'
? 1
: steps.filter(s => s.status === 'succeeded' && BILLABLE.has(s.stepType)).length;

// AI is metered separately in every mode — the underlying cost is real and variable. const aiCredits = steps.reduce((n, s) => n + (s.aiCreditsUsed ?? 0), 0);
return { base, aiCredits, total: base + aiCredits };
}

Free-tier and internal steps stay unbilled so that adding a filter never costs a customer money — the property Activepieces highlights against per-task pricing, and the one that most changes how users design flows.

Part 9 — Delivery Plan

Each phase ends with acceptance criteria that are demonstrable, not aspirational.

Phase 1 — Deterministic core (weeks 1–8)
Scope: monorepo, Postgres schema + RLS, Piece SDK, 10 pieces (HTTP, Webhook, Schedule, Code, Slack, Gmail, Google Sheets, HubSpot, Airtable, Data Tables), envelope encryption, OAuth + API-key connections, draft/publish/ version/rollback, executor with filter/branch/router/loop/delay, BullMQ workers, run history with step I/O.
Acceptance: publish a 5-step flow with a branch; 1,000 webhook-triggered runs at p95 < 3 s end-to-end; kill a worker mid-run and prove no duplicated side effect; two orgs cannot read each other's runs.

Phase 2 — AI plane (weeks 9–14)
Scope: ModelGateway with purpose routing, budgets, caching and repair; AI step (generate/summarize/classify with abstention/extract/embed); catalog embeddings + hybrid retrieval; approval step + Todos; Data Tables maturity.
Acceptance: an AI classify step abstains rather than mis-labelling on a deliberately ambiguous input set; hybrid retrieval reaches recall@12 ≥ 0.95 on a labelled query set; AI spend appears per-org within 60 s of a run.

Phase 3 — Copilot generation (weeks 15–22)
Scope: the entire Part 6 pipeline; SSE side panel; both autonomy modes; connection pre-selection; refinement; inline field suggestion/explanation; eval harness with the golden set and CI gates.
Acceptance: the §6.10 gates all pass on a 60-case golden set; a naive user goes from one sentence to a published, successfully running flow in under 5 minutes for 8 of 10 scripted scenarios; zero hallucinated operations across the full eval; Copilot cannot publish or create a credential — proven by test, not by prompt.

Phase 4 — Agents and tools (weeks 23–30)
Scope: ToolRegistry, agent step with typed tools and budgets, agent transcripts with a visible reasoning panel,
agent_callagent_callMCP server exposing pieces andflows as tools, flows-as-tools registration.
agent_call
agent_call
Acceptance: an agent completes a 6-tool task within budget; every decision is inspectable in the reasoning panel; the same ToolRegistry code path serves both the agent and MCP — verified by a single shared test suite; an agent cannot call a tool outside its flow's declared list.

Phase 5 — Scale and enterprise (weeks 31–40)
Scope: queue partitioning, KEDA autoscaling, run partitioning + archival, ops copilot diagnosis, SSO/SCIM, git sync via definition_hash , environments and promotion, audit export, SLA dashboards.
Acceptance: 50,000 runs/hour sustained with p95 step latency flat; a failed run diagnosis proposes a correct patch on 70% of a labelled failure set; promote a flow dev → prod with no definition drift, proven by matching hashes.

Phase 6 — Ecosystem (weeks 41–48)
Scope: 100+ pieces, piece submission pipeline with automated contract tests, template gallery seeded from Copilot-generated flows, embedded/white-label builder, public API + SDKs.
Acceptance: an external contributor lands a piece through CI without core-team code changes; template install-to-run conversion is measured and reported.

Glossary
Piєcє — a versioned integration package declaring auth, triggers and actions. Opєration — one trigger or action within a piece. Connєction — an encrypted, org-scoped credential instance for a piece. Flow — a container with one mutable draft and an ordered list of immutable published versions. Flow vєrsion — a content-addressed, immutable definition; runs pin one. Run — one execution of one flow version. Stєp run — one node execution with resolved input, output, timing and error class. Triggєr — the single entry point of a flow. Exprєssion / tokєn —
{{step.path}}{{step.path}}reference resolved from prior step output. Dєhydration — persisting a run's cursor and releasing the worker while waiting for a delay or approval. Rєhydration — resuming from that cursor. Draft — the editable definition; the only thing Copilot may write. Copilot — the AI authoring layer: generation, refinement, inline assistance, diagnosis. Agєnt — a runtime that chooses its own tool sequence within budgets. Tool — a typed callable exposed to an agent or over MCP. Abstєntion — an AI step returning "unknown" instead of a low-confidence guess. Crєdit — the billing unit; run-metered or step-metered per org, with AI metered separately. Todo — a pending human decision blocking a dehydrated run.

Pre-launch checklist
definition_hashdefinition_hashCorrєctnєss — no duplicated side effect after worker kill; replay produces identical results for pure steps; published versions immutable; rollback restores exact prior definition; RLS proven across orgs;
definition_hash
definition_hash
stable across environments.
Copilot — all §6.10 gates green; capability envelope enforced by service-layer tests; every generated flow either publishable-after-auth or carrying explicit todos; reasoning visible for every decision; prompts versioned and rollback-able.
Sєcurity — credentials never in logs, API responses, or model prompts; SSRF allow-list active; sandbox escape suite green; audit log covers all privileged actions; per-org AI spend hard ceiling enforced.
Opєrations — dashboards live; alerts routed and tested with a synthetic failure; runbooks rehearsed; partition maintenance scheduled; DLQ monitored; graceful shutdown verified under rollout.
Product — one-sentence-to-running-flow measured on real users; template gallery seeded; error messages name the human action, not the internal cause.
